tan(Number.isFinite(q.bank)?q.bank:0)*(ns.lat||0);
 const t=terrainEase(clamp((Math.abs(ns.lat||0)-edge)/Math.max(8,spread.far*.72),0,1));
 return lerp(q.y+bankY,ground,t);
}
function apexPointSegmentDistance2D(px,pz,a,b){
 const dx=b[0]-a[0],dz=b[1]-a[1],l2=dx*dx+dz*dz||1,t=clamp(((px-a[0])*dx+(pz-a[1])*dz)/l2,0,1),x=a[0]+dx*t,z=a[1]+dz*t;return Math.hypot(px-x,pz-z);
}
function apexPointInsideClosedContour(x,z,P){
 if(!P||P.length<3)return false;
 let inside=false;
 for(let i=0,j=P.length-1;i<P.length;j=i++){
   const a=P[i],b=P[j];
   if((a[1]>z)!==(b[1]>z)){
     const cross=a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*(z-a[1])/((b[1]-a[1])||1e-9);
     if(x<cross)inside=!inside;
   }
 }
 return inside;
}
// Terrain plates are polygons with an elevation over the local finish datum. The
// larger base polygon sets the domain and background elevation, nested/overlapping
// plates supersede it. Greater elevation wins overlaps unless data-apex-priority
// explicitly requests a different ordering. No circuit names or track IDs here.
function apexPrepareTerrainPlateGrid(){
 const cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld,base=cw&&cw.terrainBase;
 if(!cw||!base||!Array.isArray(base.rings)||!base.rings.length)return null;
 if(cw._terrainContourGrid&&cw._terrainContourGrid.plateMesh)return cw._terrainContourGrid;
 const cfg=apexTerrainContourConfig(),plates=apexTerrainContourList(),baseRings=base.rings.filter(r=>Array.isArray(r)&&r.length>=3);
 if(!baseRings.length)return null;
 let minX=Infinity,maxX=-Infinity,minZ=Infinity,maxZ=-Infinity;
 const bound=P=>{let x0=Infinity,x1=-Infinity,z0=Infinity,z1=-Infinity;for(const p of P){x0=Math.min(x0,p[0]);x1=Math.max(x1,p[0]);z0=Math.min(z0,p[1]);z1=Math.max(z1,p[1])}return{x0,x1,z0,z1}};
 for(const P of baseRings)for(const p of P){minX=Math.min(minX,p[0]);maxX=Math.max(maxX,p[0]);minZ=Math.min(minZ,p[1]);maxZ=Math.max(maxZ,p[1]);}
 // A few metres of margin allow stable interpolation at authored boundaries.
 const mobile=typeof mobilePerformanceMode==='function'&&mobilePerformanceMode(),budget=mobile?6200:11000;
 let cell=mobile?24:18,nx=Math.ceil((maxX-minX)/cell)+1,nz=Math.ceil((maxZ-minZ)/cell)+1;
 if(nx*nz>budget){cell*=Math.sqrt(nx*nz/budget);nx=Math.max(2,Math.ceil((maxX-minX)/cell)+1);nz=Math.max(2,Math.ceil((maxZ-minZ)/cell)+1)}
 // The last grid node must reach the far edge; the exported base polygon is definitive.
 cell=Math.max((maxX-minX)/Math.max(1,nx-1),(maxZ-minZ)/Math.max(1,nz-1));
 const vals=new Float32Array(nx*nz),rawVals=new Float32Array(nx*nz),inside=new Uint8Array(nx*nz),mask=new Uint8Array((nx-1)*(nz-1));
 const raw=plates.filter(c=>c&&c.closed&&Array.isArray(c.points)&&c.points.length>=3).map((c,i)=>{
   const b=bound(c.points),e=(+c.elevationM)*cfg.scale+cfg.offset;
   return{...b,c,e,priority:Number.isFinite(+c.priority)?+c.priority:0,originalIndex:i};
 }).sort((a,b)=>(a.priority-b.priority)||(a.e-b.e)||(a.originalIndex-b.originalIndex));
 const open=plates.filter(c=>c&&!c.closed&&Array.isArray(c.points)&&c.points.length>=2).map(c=>({...bound(c.points),c,e:(+c.elevationM)*cfg.scale+cfg.offset}));
 const baseHeight=(+base.elevationM)*cfg.scale+cfg.offset,blend=Math.max(cell*.75,cfg.plateBoundaryBlendM||Math.max(12,cell*1.15));
 const inBase=(x,z)=>baseRings.some(P=>apexPointInsideClosedContour(x,z,P));
 function edgeDistance(x,z,P){
   let d=Infinity;
   for(let k=0,n=P.length;k<n;k++)d=Math.min(d,apexPointSegmentDistance2D(x,z,P[k],P[(k+1)%n]));
   return d;
 }
 for(let iz=0;iz<nz;iz++)for(let ix=0;ix<nx;ix++){
   const x=Math.min(maxX,minX+ix*cell),z=Math.min(maxZ,minZ+iz*cell),i=iz*nx+ix;
   if(!inBase(x,z))continue;
   inside[i]=1;
   let y=baseHeight;
   // Higher plates laid on larger plates define the same piece of terrain;
   // a narrow seam blends heights rather than erecting vertical walls.
   for(const p of raw){
     if(x<p.x0||x>p.x1||z<p.z0||z>p.z1||!apexPointInsideClosedContour(x,z,p.c.points))continue;
     const d=edgeDistance(x,z,p.c.points),t=terrainEase(Math.min(1,d/blend));
     y=lerp(y,p.e,t);
   }
   // Legacy open contour paths remain valid: act as narrow surveyed constraints.
   for(const p of open){
     if(x<p.x0-blend||x>p.x1+blend||z<p.z0-blend||z>p.z1+blend)continue;
     let d=Infinity,P=p.c.points;
     for(let k=1;k<P.length;k++)d=Math.min(d,apexPointSegmentDistance2D(x,z,P[k-1],P[k]));
     if(d<blend)y=lerp(y,p.e,terrainEase(1-d/blend)*.94);
   }
   // FASE 1: ground grid uses the same shoulder and no-camber corridor as
   // objects and the visual road. Plate heights remain authoritative outside it.
   rawVals[i]=y;vals[i]=apexPhase1GroundBlend(x,z,y);
 }
 for(let iz=0;iz<nz-1;iz++)for(let ix=0;ix<nx-1;ix++){
   const x=minX+(ix+.5)*cell,z=minZ+(iz+.5)*cell;
   mask[iz*(nx-1)+ix]=inBase(x,z)?1:0;
 }
 cw._terrainContourGrid={minX,maxX,minZ,maxZ,cell,nx,nz,vals,rawVals,inside,mask,plateMesh:true,fineCache:new Map(),
   baseLevelM:baseHeight,tone:base.terrainTone||'GREEN'};
 return cw._terrainContourGrid;
}
function apexPlateTerrainGrid(){const cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld;return cw&&cw.terrainBase?apexPrepareTerrainPlateGrid():null;}
function apexPrepareTerrainContourGrid(){
 const cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld;
 if(cw&&cw.terrainBase)return apexPrepareTerrainPlateGrid();
 const contours=apexTerrainContourList();
 if(!cw||!contours.length)return null;
 if(cw._terrainContourGrid)return cw._terrainContourGrid;
 const cfg=apexTerrainContourConfig(),relative=cfg.reference==='RELATIVE_TO_BASE'||cfg.reference==='RELATIVE'||cfg.reference==='LOCAL_RELATIVE',local=cfg.reference==='LOCAL_ABSOLUTE'||cfg.reference==='START_FINISH_ZERO';
 let minX=Infinity,maxX=-Infinity,minZ=Infinity,maxZ=-Infinity;
 const mini=trackData.model&&trackData.model.mini;if(mini){minX=mini.mnx;maxX=mini.mxx;minZ=mini.mnz;maxZ=mini.mxz}
 for(const c of contours)for(const p of (c.points||[])){if(!p)continue;minX=Math.min(minX,p[0]);maxX=Math.max(maxX,p[0]);minZ=Math.min(minZ,p[1]);maxZ=Math.max(maxZ,p[1])}
 if(!Number.isFinite(minX))return null;
 const margin=70;minX-=margin;maxX+=margin;minZ-=margin;maxZ+=margin;
 let cell=22,nx=Math.max(2,Math.ceil((maxX-minX)/cell)+1),nz=Math.max(2,Math.ceil((maxZ-minZ)/cell)+1);
 const budget=(typeof mobilePerformanceMode==='function'&&mobilePerformanceMode())?7000:11000;
 if(nx*nz>budget){cell*=Math.sqrt(nx*nz/budget);nx=Math.max(2,Math.ceil((maxX-minX)/cell)+1);nz=Math.max(2,Math.ceil((maxZ-minZ)/cell)+1)}
 const vals=new Float32Array(nx*nz),soft=3.0,influence=cfg.influenceM,bucketSize=84,buckets=new Map();
 const bk=(i,k)=>i+','+k,segments=[],types=[];
 // Spatial hash construido UNA sola vez. Mantiene tiempos de carga razonables
 // con muchas curvas muy próximas (level_2, level_2.5, etc.).
 for(let ci=0;ci<contours.length;ci++){
   const c=contours[ci],P=c&&c.points;
   if(!c||!Number.isFinite(+c.elevationM)||!Array.isArray(P)||P.length<2){types.push(null);continue}
   const elev=(+c.elevationM)*cfg.scale+cfg.offset,poly=c.closed&&P.length>=3?P:null;
   types.push({elev,poly});
   const count=P.length-(P.length>2&&Math.hypot(P[0][0]-P[P.length-1][0],P[0][1]-P[P.length-1][1])<.25?0:1);
   for(let t=1;t<=count;t++){
     const a=P[t-1],b=P[t%P.length];if(!a||!b)continue;
     const x0=a[0],z0=a[1],x1=b[0],z1=b[1],dx=x1-x0,dz=z1-z0,l2=dx*dx+dz*dz;
     if(l2<.001)continue;
     const si=segments.length;segments.push({ci,x0,z0,dx,dz,l2});
     const bx0=Math.floor((Math.min(x0,x1)-influence)/bucketSize),bx1=Math.floor((Math.max(x0,x1)+influence)/bucketSize),bz0=Math.floor((Math.min(z0,z1)-influence)/bucketSize),bz1=Math.floor((Math.max(z0,z1)+influence)/bucketSize);
     for(let bx=bx0;bx<=bx1;bx++)for(let bz=bz0;bz<=bz1;bz++){
       const key=bk(bx,bz);let arr=buckets.get(key);if(!arr){arr=[];buckets.set(key,arr)}arr.push(si);
     }
   }
 }
 for(let iz=0;iz<nz;iz++)for(let ix=0;ix<nx;ix++){
   const x=Math.min(maxX,minX+ix*cell),z=Math.min(maxZ,minZ+iz*cell),fallback=apexProceduralTerrainHeightAtWorld(x,z),nearest=new Float64Array(types.length);
   nearest.fill(Infinity);
   const cand=buckets.get(bk(Math.floor(x/bucketSize),Math.floor(z/bucketSize)))||[];
   for(let k=0;k<cand.length;k++){
     const sg=segments[cand[k]],t=clamp(((x-sg.x0)*sg.dx+(z-sg.z0)*sg.dz)/sg.l2,0,1),d=Math.hypot(x-(sg.x0+sg.dx*t),z-(sg.z0+sg.dz*t));
     if(d<nearest[sg.ci])nearest[sg.ci]=d;
   }
   const near=[];let inside=false;
   for(let ci=0;ci<types.length;ci++){
     const m=types[ci];if(!m)continue;
     const d=nearest[ci];if(Number.isFinite(d)&&d<=influence){near.push({d,e:m.elev,ci})}
     if(local&&m.poly&&d<=influence&&apexPointInsideClosedContour(x,z,m.poly))inside=true;
   }
   let y=fallback;
   if(near.length){
     near.sort((a,b)=>a.d-b.d);
     if(near[0].d<.65){y=relative?fallback+near[0].e:near[0].e}
     else{
       let sum=0,weight=0;
       for(let i=0;i<Math.min(5,near.length);i++){
         const v=near[i],w=1/((v.d+soft)*(v.d+soft));sum+=w*v.e;weight+=w;
       }
       const h=sum/Math.max(1e-9,weight),distance=near[0].d;
       if(relative){const fade=terrainEase(clamp(1-distance/influence,0,1));y=fallback+h*fade}
       else if(local){
         // Las curvas SVG son alturas absolutas respecto a la META=0, nunca
         // sumas sobre el terreno cercano. Fuera de su ámbito: transición al terreno base.
         const fade=inside?1:terrainEase(clamp(1-Math.max(0,distance-12)/Math.max(1,influence-12),0,1));
         y=lerp(fallback,h,fade);
       }else{
         const wf=1/(72*72);y=(sum+fallback*wf)/(weight+wf);
       }
     }
   }
   vals[iz*nx+ix]=y;
 }
 cw._terrainContourGrid={minX,maxX,minZ,maxZ,cell,nx,nz,vals};return cw._terrainContourGrid;
}
function apexTerrainHeightAtWorld(x,z,fallback=null){
 const g=apexPrepareTerrainContourGrid();if(!g)return Number.isFinite(fallback)?fallback:apexProceduralTerrainHeightAtWorld(x,z);
 if(x<g.minX||x>g.maxX||z<g.minZ||z>g.maxZ)return Number.isFinite(fallback)?fallback:apexProceduralTerrainHeightAtWorld(x,z);
 const fx=clamp((x-g.minX)/g.cell,0,g.nx-1),fz=clamp((z-g.minZ)/g.cell,0,g.nz-1),ix=Math.min(g.nx-2,Math.floor(fx)),iz=Math.min(g.nz-2,Math.floor(fz)),tx=fx-ix,tz=fz-iz;
 if(g.plateMesh&&!g.mask[iz*(g.nx-1)+ix])return Number.isFinite(fallback)?fallback:g.baseLevelM;
 const a=g.vals[iz*g.nx+ix],b=g.vals[iz*g.nx+ix+1],c=g.vals[(iz+1)*g.nx+ix],d=g.vals[(iz+1)*g.nx+ix+1];
 const ya=g.plateMesh&&!g.inside[iz*g.nx+ix]?g.baseLevelM:a,yb=g.plateMesh&&!g.inside[iz*g.nx+ix+1]?g.baseLevelM:b,yc=g.plateMesh&&!g.inside[(iz+1)*g.nx+ix]?g.baseLevelM:c,yd=g.plateMesh&&!g.inside[(iz+1)*g.nx+ix+1]?g.baseLevelM:d;
 return lerp(lerp(ya,yb,tx),lerp(yc,yd,tx),tz);
}

// v5.230.45 · Malla 3D real del polígono terrain-base. Muestreo hecho al importar;
// cada frame se envían únicamente las celdas visibles (LOD 2x en móvil).
function apexCollectTerrainPlateCells(cam){
 const g=apexPlateTerrainGrid();if(!g)return null;
 if(apexInspectionFullScene()){
 if(!g._inspectionCells){const out=[];let fineCount=0;for(let iz=0;iz<g.nz-1;iz++)for(let ix=0;ix<g.nx-1;ix++)if(g.mask[iz*(g.nx-1)+ix]){
 const x=g.minX+(ix+.5)*g.cell,z=g.minZ+(iz+.5)*g.cell,kind=apexTerrainCellNeedsDetail(g,x,z,g.cell);
 const fine=kind&&(kind==='PORTAL'||kind==='TUNNEL'||fineCount++<220);out.push({ix,iz,x1:ix+1,z1:iz+1,x,z,kind:fine?kind:null,dist2:0});
 }g._inspectionCells=out;}
 return g._inspectionCells.map(c=>({...c,z:worldToCamera(c.x,g.baseLevelM,c.z,cam).z})).sort((a,b)=>b.z-a.z);
 }
 const mobile=mobilePerformanceMode(),radius=mobile?315:490,step=mobile?2:1,
   minI=clamp(Math.floor((cam.x-radius-g.minX)/g.cell),0,g.nx-2),
   maxI=clamp(Math.ceil((cam.x+radius-g.minX)/g.cell),0,g.nx-2),
   minZ=clamp(Math.floor((cam.z-radius-g.minZ)/g.cell),0,g.nz-2),
   maxZ=clamp(Math.ceil((cam.z+radius-g.minZ)/g.cell),0,g.nz-2),
   maxCells=mobile?420:1050,cells=[];
 for(let iz=minZ;iz<=maxZ;iz+=step)for(let ix=minI;ix<=maxI;ix+=step){
   const x=g.minX+(ix+.5*step)*g.cell,z=g.minZ+(iz+.5*step)*g.cell,
     dx=x-cam.x,dz=z-cam.z,dist2=dx*dx+dz*dz;
   if(dist2>radius*radius)continue;
   const C=worldToCamera(x,g.baseLevelM,z,cam);
   if(C.z<-.5*g.cell||C.z>radius+g.cell||Math.abs(C.x)>Math.max(110,C.z*1.35+g.cell*3))continue;
   const x1=Math.min(ix+step,g.nx-1),z1=Math.min(iz+step,g.nz-1);
   let covered=false;
   for(let zz=iz;zz<z1&&!covered;zz++)for(let xx=ix;xx<x1;xx++)if(g.mask[zz*(g.nx-1)+xx]){covered=true;break}
   if(!covered)continue;
   // Cerca del coche o de cualquier boca de túnel usamos la resolución exacta.
   // Así el recorte del portal no depende del LOD móvil ni aparece como un agujero grande.
   const portalCell=mobile&&!!apexPortalInfluenceAtWorld(x,z,g.cell*1.8),
     useStep=mobile&&(dist2<(105*105)||portalCell)?1:step;
   if(useStep===1&&step>1){
     for(let zz=iz;zz<z1;zz++)for(let xx=ix;xx<x1;xx++){
       if(g.mask[zz*(g.nx-1)+xx])cells.push({ix:xx,iz:zz,x1:xx+1,z1:zz+1,z:C.z,dist2});
     }
   }else cells.push({ix,iz,x1,z1,z:C.z,dist2});
 }
 // The nearest cells take priority when the mesh exceeds its per-frame budget.
 if(cells.length>maxCells){cells.sort((a,b)=>a.dist2-b.dist2);cells.length=maxCells;}
 cells.sort((a,b)=>b.z-a.z);
 return cells;
}
// Malla adaptativa: lejos 18–25 m, junto a superficies duras 3–6 m. La
// clasificación usa la MISMA resolución en Canvas y WebGL y no depende del GP.
function apexTerrainRawInterpolated(g,x,z){
 const fx=clamp((x-g.minX)/g.cell,0,g.nx-1),fz=clamp((z-g.minZ)/g.cell,0,g.nz-1),ix=Math.min(g.nx-2,Math.floor(fx)),iz=Math.min(g.nz-2,Math.floor(fz)),tx=fx-ix,tz=fz-iz;
 const h=(u,v)=>g.inside[v*g.nx+u]?g.rawVals[v*g.nx+u]:g.baseLevelM;
 return lerp(lerp(h(ix,iz),h(ix+1,iz),tx),lerp(h(ix,iz+1),h(ix+1,iz+1),tx),tz);
}
function apexTerrainCellNeedsDetail(g,x,z,span){
 // Las bocas tienen prioridad: necesitamos una teselación más fina para recortar
 // únicamente el vano, sin quitar la montaña que lo rodea.
 if(apexPortalInfluenceAtWorld(x,z,span*1.15+2.0))return 'PORTAL';
 const tunnel=apexNearestGradeSegment(x,z,'TUNNEL',span*1.0+30);
 if(tunnel&&tunnel.dist<tunnel.width*.5+span*1.2+4)return 'TUNNEL';
 const st=nearestTrackState(x,z,null,{radius:95,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:90});
 if(st&&Math.abs(st.lat)<span*1.5+envProfile(st.s).half+apexPhase1Config().blendM)return 'TRACK';
 const sg=apexNearestGradeSegment(x,z,'PAVED',span*1.1+36);
 return sg&&sg.dist<sg.width*.5+span*1.4+12?'PAVED':null;
}
function apexTerrainFinePatch(g,ix,iz,kind){
 const key=iz*g.nx+ix,old=g.fineCache.get(key);if(old)return old;
 const x0=g.minX+ix*g.cell,z0=g.minZ+iz*g.cell,
       x1=Math.min(g.maxX,x0+g.cell),z1=Math.min(g.maxZ,z0+g.cell),n=kind==='PORTAL'?20:(kind==='TUNNEL'?18:4),
       dx=(x1-x0)/n,dz=(z1-z0)/n,points=[],quads=[];
 const cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld,base=cw&&cw.terrainBase,
   baseRings=base&&base.rings||[];
 for(let j=0;j<=n;j++)for(let i=0;i<=n;i++){
  const x=x0+dx*i,z=z0+dz*j,inside=baseRings.some(r=>apexPointInsideClosedContour(x,z,r));
  const ns=nearestTrackState(x,z,null,{radius:160,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:155}),env=envProfile(ns.s),
    mainPaved=(!apexPhase1StructureAtWorld(x,z,ns.s)&&Math.abs(ns.lat)<=apexRoadFlatLimit(ns.s,env,ns.lat>=0?1:-1)),
    sg=apexNearestGradeSegment(x,z,null,42),otherPaved=sg&&sg.kind==='PIT'&&sg.dist<sg.width*.5;
  const raw=apexTerrainRawInterpolated(g,x,z),ground=apexPhase1GroundBlend(x,z,raw,ns),y=ground;
  points.push({x,y,z,inside,paved:mainPaved||!!otherPaved});
 }
 for(let j=0;j<n;j++)for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
  const A=points[j*(n+1)+i],B=points[j*(n+1)+i+1],C=points[(j+1)*(n+1)+i+1],D=points[(j+1)*(n+1)+i];
  const x=(A.x+C.x)*.5,z=(A.z+C.z)*.5;
  if(!baseRings.some(r=>apexPointInsideClosedContour(x,z,r)))continue;

  // v5.230.62 · RECORTE LOCAL DE EMBOCADURA.
  // Sólo quitamos la superficie que físicamente atravesaría el hueco de la boca.
  // El resto de la placa, incluidos laterales y montaña sobre el túnel, queda intacto.
  const portal=apexPortalTerrainCutAtWorld(x,z,Math.max(dx,dz)*.58);
  if(portal){
    const terrainTop=Math.max(A.y,B.y,C.y,D.y);
    if(terrainTop>portal.q.y+.10)continue;
  }

  const ns=nearestTrackState(x,z,null,{radius:70,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:65}),
    env=envProfile(ns.s),structure=apexPhase1StructureAtWorld(x,z,ns.s),
    mainPaved=!structure&&Math.abs(ns.lat)<apexRoadFlatLimit(ns.s,env,ns.lat>=0?1:-1),other=apexNearestGradeSegment(x,z,'PAVED',25);
  // v5.230.56: ROAD no perfora la placa de terreno. Se dibuja después como cinta
  // apoyada sobre la cota natural. PIT sí puede sustituir el terreno de su huella.
  if(mainPaved||(other&&other.kind==='PIT'&&other.dist<other.width*.5))continue;
  // La pista y los viales se dibujan después. Omitimos sólo las celdillas
  // íntegramente pavimentadas para evitar que el suelo se pinte SOBRE ellas.
  if(A.paved&&B.paved&&C.paved&&D.paved)continue;
  // El propio túnel sigue siendo el molde exacto de v61/v62. No cambiamos
  // absolutamente nada de la geometría visual para ganar rendimiento.
  for(const poly of apexClipTerrainQuadByTunnel(A,B,C,D))if(poly&&poly.length>=3)quads.push(poly);
 }
 if(!g._keepCompiledFineCache&&g.fineCache.size>540){const first=g.fineCache.keys().next().value;g.fineCache.delete(first)}
 g.fineCache.set(key,quads);return quads;
}
function apexForEachTerrainPlateCell(cam,fn){
 const g=apexPlateTerrainGrid(),cells=apexCollectTerrainPlateCells(cam);if(!g||!cells)return false;
 const h=(ix,iz)=>g.inside[iz*g.nx+ix]?g.vals[iz*g.nx+ix]:g.baseLevelM;
 let detailed=0;const pressure=mobileRenderPressure(),ordinaryFineM=mobilePerformanceMode()?lerp(72,118,pressure):lerp(105,165,pressure),structureFineM=mobilePerformanceMode()?230:285;
 for(const cell of cells){
  if(apexInspectionFullScene()){
   for(const poly of apexInspectorTerrainCellPolys(g,cell.ix,cell.iz)){const cp=poly.map(p=>worldToCamera(p.x,p.y,p.z,cam));fn(cp[0],cp[1],cp[2],cp[3]||cp[2],cell,g,cp);}
   continue;
  }
  const {ix,iz,x1,z1}=cell,x0=g.minX+ix*g.cell,z0=g.minZ+iz*g.cell,
    xB=Math.min(g.maxX,g.minX+x1*g.cell),zB=Math.min(g.maxZ,g.minZ+z1*g.cell),
    unitCell=x1===ix+1&&z1===iz+1,probe=cell.dist2<structureFineM*structureFineM&&unitCell,dKind=apexInspectionFullScene()?cell.kind:probe?apexTerrainCellNeedsDetail(g,(x0+xB)*.5,(z0+zB)*.5,g.cell):null,
    structural=dKind==='PORTAL'||dKind==='TUNNEL',allowDetail=dKind&&(apexInspectionFullScene()||structural||cell.dist2<ordinaryFineM*ordinaryFineM&&detailed<220);
  if(allowDetail){
   if(dKind!=='PORTAL')detailed++;for(const poly of apexTerrainFinePatch(g,ix,iz,dKind)){
    const cp=poly.map(p=>worldToCamera(p.x,p.y,p.z,cam));if(cp.length>=3)fn(cp[0],cp[1],cp[2],cp[3]||cp[2],cell,g,cp);
   }
   continue;
  }
  // v5.230.62 · PORTAL/TUNNEL visibles siempre pasan por el molde fino. En el LOD
  // lejano conservamos literalmente la altura SVG; nunca levantamos la montaña.
  const y00=h(ix,iz),y10=h(x1,iz),y11=h(x1,z1),y01=h(ix,z1);
  fn(worldToCamera(x0,y00,z0,cam),worldToCamera(xB,y10,z0,cam),
    worldToCamera(xB,y11,zB,cam),worldToCamera(x0,y01,zB,cam),cell,g);
 }
 return true;
}
// ---------- v5.230.62.2 · COMPILADOR ESTÁTICO + CACHE PERSISTENTE ----------
// El objetivo es pagar el coste de interpretación/teselación ANTES de conducir y
// reutilizarlo en posteriores aperturas del MISMO SVG/perfil. No cambia la geometría.
const APEX_CIRCUIT_COMPILE_REV=230682;
async function apexCompilerYield(){return new Promise(resolve=>setTimeout(resolve,0));}
function apexHash32String(str){let h=2166136261>>>0;str=String(str||'');for(let i=0;i<str.length;i++){h^=str.charCodeAt(i);h=Math.imul(h,16777619)>>>0}return ('00000000'+h.toString(16)).slice(-8)}
function apexCircuitCompileFingerprint(){
 const dir=trackData&&trackData.spec&&trackData.spec.dir||'',src=readEffectiveTrackSvg(dir),raw=src&&typeof src.rawSvg==='string'?src.rawSvg:'',cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld,cfg=readStoredCircuitJson(dir)||{},vp=trackData&&trackData.verticalProfile||cfg.verticalProfile||null;
 let geomSig=raw;if(!geomSig&&trackData&&Array.isArray(trackData.pts)){const P=trackData.pts,n=P.length,s=[];for(let i=0;i<n;i+=Math.max(1,Math.floor(n/96))){const p=P[i];s.push(Number(p[0]).toFixed(3)+','+Number(p[1]).toFixed(3))}geomSig=`${n}|${trackData.km}|${trackData.direction}|${s.join(';')}`}
 const meta=`${APEX_CIRCUIT_COMPILE_REV}|${dir}|${cw&&cw.profileRevision||0}|${JSON.stringify(vp||{})}|${JSON.stringify(cfg.banking||{})}`;
 return `${dir}:${apexHash32String(geomSig)}:${apexHash32String(meta)}`;
}
function apexPackFineCache(map){
 const keys=[],counts=[],sizes=[],coords=[];let entries=0,polys=0,verts=0;
 for(const [key,arr0] of map||[]){const arr=Array.isArray(arr0)?arr0:[];keys.push(+key|0);counts.push(Math.min(65535,arr.length));entries++;for(const poly of arr){const n=Math.min(255,Array.isArray(poly)?poly.length:0);sizes.push(n);polys++;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const p=poly[i]||{};coords.push(+p.x||0,+p.y||0,+p.z||0);verts++}}}
 return{keys:Int32Array.from(keys),counts:Uint16Array.from(counts),sizes:Uint8Array.from(sizes),coords:Float32Array.from(coords),entries,polys,verts};
}
function apexRestoreFineCache(map,packed){
 if(!map||!packed||!packed.keys||!packed.counts||!packed.sizes||!packed.coords)return 0;map.clear();let pi=0,ci=0;
 for(let e=0;e<packed.keys.length;e++){const arr=[],pc=packed.counts[e]||0;for(let p=0;p<pc;p++){const n=packed.sizes[pi++]||0,poly=[];for(let v=0;v<n;v++)poly.push({x:packed.coords[ci++],y:packed.coords[ci++],z:packed.coords[ci++]});if(poly.length)arr.push(poly)}map.set(packed.keys[e],arr)}return map.size;
}
function apexCompiledGridMatches(rec,g){const m=rec&&rec.grid;if(!m||!g)return false;return m.nx===g.nx&&m.nz===g.nz&&Math.abs((m.cell||0)-g.cell)<.01&&Math.abs((m.minX||0)-g.minX)<.05&&Math.abs((m.minZ||0)-g.minZ)<.05}
function apexCompilerProgress(cb,label,ratio){if(typeof cb==='function')try{cb(label,clamp(ratio,0,1))}catch(e){}}
function apexCircuitStaticCounts(cw){return{landmarks:(cw&&cw.landmarks||[]).length,walls:(cw&&cw.wallSegments||[]).length,wallRenderSegments:(cw&&cw._wallRenderSegments||[]).length,barriers:(cw&&cw.barrierSegments||[]).length,surfaces:(cw&&cw.surfaceZones||[]).length,terrainContours:(cw&&cw.terrainContours||[]).length,structureZones:(cw&&cw.structureZones||[]).length,brakeBoards:(cw&&cw.brakeBoards||[]).length};}
function apexCompileItemS(it,L){
 if(it&&it.anchor&&Number.isFinite(it.anchor.s))return mod(it.anchor.s,L);if(it&&Number.isFinite(it.s))return mod(it.s,L);
 let x=null,z=null;if(it&&it.anchor&&Number.isFinite(it.anchor.x)&&Number.isFinite(it.anchor.z)){x=it.anchor.x;z=it.anchor.z}else if(it&&Array.isArray(it.worldPath)&&it.worldPath.length){x=it.worldPath[0][0];z=it.worldPath[0][1]}else if(it&&Number.isFinite(it.x1)&&Number.isFinite(it.z1)){x=it.x1;z=it.z1}
 if(Number.isFinite(x)&&Number.isFinite(z)){const ns=nearestTrackState(x,z,null,{radius:500,allowGlobal:true,fallbackDist:480});if(ns&&Number.isFinite(ns.s))return mod(ns.s,L)}return 0;
}
async function apexCompileCircuitStatic(onProgress=null){
 if(!trackData||!trackData.pts||!trackData.pts.length)return null;
 const tAll=performance.now(),fingerprint=apexCircuitCompileFingerprint(),stats={revision:APEX_CIRCUIT_COMPILE_REV,appVersion:APP_VERSION,circuit:trackData.spec&&trackData.spec.dir||'',fingerprint,startedAt:new Date().toISOString(),stages:{},sectorM:96,sectors:0,terrainFineCells:0,terrainFinePolygons:0,terrainFineByKind:{PORTAL:0,TUNNEL:0,TRACK:0,PAVED:0},estimatedFineVertices:0,cache:{hit:false,persisted:false,loadMs:0,writeMs:0}};
 apexCompilerProgress(onProgress,'ÍNDICES',.02);let t0=performance.now();
 const cw=circuitWorldProfile(),grade=apexGradeSpatialIndex(),portals=apexTunnelPortalDescriptors(),terrain=apexPlateTerrainGrid()||apexPrepareTerrainContourGrid();
 if(cw&&cw.authoritative)apexRegroundImportedWorld();
 try{apexCollisionSegments();}catch(e){}
 try{apexPitSegments();}catch(e){}
 stats.stages.indicesMs=Number((performance.now()-t0).toFixed(2));stats.static=apexCircuitStaticCounts(cw);stats.tunnels=grade&&grade.tunnels?grade.tunnels.length:0;stats.portals=portals.length;
 await apexCompilerYield();

 // Sectorización completa: barata, se reconstruye siempre para mantener referencias vivas a los objetos del mundo.
 apexCompilerProgress(onProgress,'SECTORES',.10);t0=performance.now();const L=trackData.km*1000,sectorM=stats.sectorM,nSec=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(L/sectorM)),sectors=Array.from({length:nSec},(_,i)=>({i,s0:i*sectorM,s1:Math.min(L,(i+1)*sectorM),landmarks:[],walls:[],barriers:[],surfaces:[],brakeBoards:[],tunnels:[]})),sid=s=>Math.min(nSec-1,Math.max(0,Math.floor(mod(s,L)/sectorM)));
 for(let i=0;i<(cw.landmarks||[]).length;i++)sectors[sid(apexCompileItemS(cw.landmarks[i],L))].landmarks.push(i);
 for(let i=0;i<(cw._wallRenderSegments||cw.wallSegments||[]).length;i++){const it=(cw._wallRenderSegments||cw.wallSegments)[i];sectors[sid(apexCompileItemS(it,L))].walls.push(i)}
 for(let i=0;i<(cw.barrierSegments||[]).length;i++)sectors[sid(apexCompileItemS(cw.barrierSegments[i],L))].barriers.push(i);
 for(let i=0;i<(cw.surfaceZones||[]).length;i++)sectors[sid(apexCompileItemS(cw.surfaceZones[i],L))].surfaces.push(i);
 for(let i=0;i<(cw.brakeBoards||[]).length;i++)sectors[sid(apexCompileItemS(cw.brakeBoards[i],L))].brakeBoards.push(i);
 for(let i=0;i<(grade&&grade.tunnels||[]).length;i++){const t=grade.tunnels[i],a=sid(t.s0),b=sid(t.s1);sectors[a].tunnels.push(i);if(b!==a)sectors[b].tunnels.push(i)}
 const skylineLandmarks=[];for(let i=0;i<(cw.landmarks||[]).length;i++)if(apexIsSkylineLandmark(cw.landmarks[i]))skylineLandmarks.push(i);
 trackData.compiledCircuit={revision:APEX_CIRCUIT_COMPILE_REV,sectorM,sectors,gradeIndex:grade,portals,skylineLandmarks};stats.sectors=nSec;stats.skylineLandmarks=skylineLandmarks.length;stats.staticBatches=apexBuildCircuitStaticRenderBatches(cw);trackData.compiledCircuit.staticBatchStats=stats.staticBatches;stats.stages.sectorsMs=Number((performance.now()-t0).toFixed(2));
 await apexCompilerYield();

 // Antes de teselar de nuevo, intenta restaurar la malla fina ya compilada para esta huella exacta.
 apexCompilerProgress(onProgress,'CACHE',.16);t0=performance.now();let cached=null;
 if(terrain&&terrain.plateMesh&&terrain.mask&&terrain.fineCache){
   cached=await apexCompiledDbRead(stats.circuit);stats.cache.loadMs=Number((performance.now()-t0).toFixed(2));
   if(cached&&cached.revision===APEX_CIRCUIT_COMPILE_REV&&cached.fingerprint===fingerprint&&apexCompiledGridMatches(cached,terrain)&&cached.fineCache){
     const restored=apexRestoreFineCache(terrain.fineCache,cached.fineCache);terrain._keepCompiledFineCache=true;terrain._compiledFineReady=true;stats.cache.hit=true;stats.cache.persisted=true;stats.cache.restoredEntries=restored;
     const cs=cached.stats||{};stats.terrainFineCells=cs.terrainFineCells||restored;stats.terrainFinePolygons=cs.terrainFinePolygons||cached.fineCache.polys||0;stats.estimatedFineVertices=cs.estimatedFineVertices||cached.fineCache.verts||0;stats.terrainFineByKind=cs.terrainFineByKind||stats.terrainFineByKind;stats.terrainGrid={nx:terrain.nx,nz:terrain.nz,cellM:Number(terrain.cell.toFixed(2)),fineCacheEntries:terrain.fineCache.size};
   }
 }

 if(!stats.cache.hit){
   apexCompilerProgress(onProgress,'TERRENO',.18);t0=performance.now();
   if(terrain&&terrain.plateMesh&&terrain.mask&&terrain.fineCache){
     terrain._keepCompiledFineCache=true;const jobs=[];
     for(let iz=0;iz<terrain.nz-1;iz++)for(let ix=0;ix<terrain.nx-1;ix++){
       if(!terrain.mask[iz*(terrain.nx-1)+ix])continue;const x=terrain.minX+(ix+.5)*terrain.cell,z=terrain.minZ+(iz+.5)*terrain.cell,kind=apexTerrainCellNeedsDetail(terrain,x,z,terrain.cell);if(kind)jobs.push({ix,iz,kind});
     }
     const pri={PORTAL:0,TUNNEL:1,TRACK:2,PAVED:3};jobs.sort((a,b)=>(pri[a.kind]??9)-(pri[b.kind]??9));
     let chunkStart=performance.now();
     for(let j=0;j<jobs.length;j++){
       const job=jobs[j],polys=apexTerrainFinePatch(terrain,job.ix,job.iz,job.kind);stats.terrainFineCells++;stats.terrainFineByKind[job.kind]=(stats.terrainFineByKind[job.kind]||0)+1;stats.terrainFinePolygons+=polys.length;for(const p of polys)stats.estimatedFineVertices+=p.length;
       if(performance.now()-chunkStart>11){apexCompilerProgress(onProgress,'TERRENO',.18+.64*((j+1)/Math.max(1,jobs.length)));await apexCompilerYield();chunkStart=performance.now();}
     }
     terrain._compiledFineReady=true;stats.terrainGrid={nx:terrain.nx,nz:terrain.nz,cellM:Number(terrain.cell.toFixed(2)),fineCacheEntries:terrain.fineCache.size};
     stats.stages.terrainMs=Number((performance.now()-t0).toFixed(2));

     // Persistencia atómica sólo de datos estáticos compactados. La siguiente apertura evita la teselación completa.
     apexCompilerProgress(onProgress,'GUARDANDO CACHE',.84);const tw=performance.now();const packed=apexPackFineCache(terrain.fineCache),record={revision:APEX_CIRCUIT_COMPILE_REV,fingerprint,createdAt:new Date().toISOString(),grid:{nx:terrain.nx,nz:terrain.nz,cell:terrain.cell,minX:terrain.minX,minZ:terrain.minZ},fineCache:packed,stats:{terrainFineCells:stats.terrainFineCells,terrainFinePolygons:stats.terrainFinePolygons,terrainFineByKind:stats.terrainFineByKind,estimatedFineVertices:stats.estimatedFineVertices}};
     stats.cache.packedMB=Number(((packed.coords.byteLength+packed.keys.byteLength+packed.counts.byteLength+packed.sizes.byteLength)/1048576).toFixed(2));stats.cache.persisted=await apexCompiledDbWrite(stats.circuit,record);stats.cache.writeMs=Number((performance.now()-tw).toFixed(2));
   }
 }else stats.stages.terrainMs=0;

 apexCompilerProgress(onProgress,'FINALIZANDO',.94);await apexCompilerYield();
 t0=performance.now();try{trackLandmarks();authoredBrakeBoards();apexTunnelPortalDescriptors();if(trackData.model&&trackData.model.driver){driverModelSample(trackData.model.driver.curve||trackData.model.curve,0);}}catch(e){}
 stats.stages.finalizeMs=Number((performance.now()-t0).toFixed(2));stats.totalMs=Number((performance.now()-tAll).toFixed(2));stats.finishedAt=new Date().toISOString();stats.estimatedFineMeshMB=Number((stats.estimatedFineVertices*3*4/1048576).toFixed(2));trackData._compileStats=stats;if(trackData.compiledCircuit)trackData.compiledCircuit.stats=stats;
 apexCompilerProgress(onProgress,'LISTO',1);return stats;
}

function drawTerrainPlateMesh(cam,pitch){
 const _terrainT0=performance.now();
 apexForEachTerrainPlateCell(cam,(A,B,C,D,c,g,poly)=>{
   const col=g.tone==='BROWN'?'#886e51':g.tone==='SAND'?'#bc9c69':'#4e7650',fill=(c.ix+c.iz)%5===0?shadeColor(col,3):col;
   if(poly&&poly.length>=3)drawCamPoly(poly,pitch,fill,.58);else drawCamQuadTriangles(A,B,C,D,pitch,fill,.58);
 });
 renderProfileAdd('terrainMs',performance.now()-_terrainT0);
}

 const _terrainT0=performance.now();
 apexForEachTerrainPlateCell(cam,(A,B,C,D,c,g,poly)=>{
   const col=g.tone==='BROWN'?'#886e51':g.tone==='SAND'?'#bc9c69':'#4e7650';

 });
 renderProfileAdd('terrainMs',performance.now()-_terrainT0);
}

  portalStart:renderRibbonFrameAt(t.s0),portalEnd:renderRibbonFrameAt(t.s1)})):[];
};

window.apexTunnelTerrainInvariantAt=(x,z)=>{const g=apexPlateTerrainGrid(),raw=g?apexTerrainRawInterpolated(g,x,z):apexProceduralTerrainHeightAtWorld(x,z),ground=apexPhase1GroundBlend(x,z,raw),t=!!apexTunnelCutAt(x,z);return{tunnel:t,raw,ground,delta:ground-raw,preserved:!t||Math.abs(ground-raw)<1e-7};};
window.apexTunnelStyleDiagnostics=()=>{const idx=apexGradeSpatialIndex();return (idx&&idx.tunnels||[]).map(t=>({name:t.name||'tunnel',style:apexTunnelVisualProfile(t).style,portalStyle:apexTunnelVisualProfile(t).portalStyle,interiorStyle:apexTunnelVisualProfile(t).interiorStyle,lights:apexTunnelVisualProfile(t).hasLights,panels:apexTunnelVisualProfile(t).hasPanels,serviceWalk:apexTunnelVisualProfile(t).hasServiceWalk}));};

 outsideCutM:p.outsideDepth,insideCutM:p.insideDepth,trackY:p.q.y,x:p.q.x,z:p.q.z
}));
window.apexRoadTunnelDiagnostics=()=>{

};

window.apexTunnelInteriorClipDiagnostics=()=>{
 const idx=apexGradeSpatialIndex(),g=apexPlateTerrainGrid(),L=trackData&&trackData.km*1000;if(!idx||!g||!L)return[];
 return (idx.tunnels||[]).map(t=>{
   let sourceIntrusions=0,samples=0,minRoofClearance=Infinity;
   const span=Math.max(1,Number(t.span)||wrapDeltaS(t.s1,t.s0,L));
   for(let d=1;d<span-1;d+=Math.max(2.5,span/28)){
     const s=mod(t.s0+d,L),q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s),env=envProfile(s),st={s,q,env},half=tunnelApertureHalfWidth(st);
     for(const f of [-.92,-.55,0,.55,.92]){
       const side=f>=0?1:-1,P=ribbonWorldPoint(q,half*Math.abs(f),side,0),raw=apexTerrainRawInterpolated(g,P.x,P.z),m=apexTunnelClipMetrics({x:P.x,y:raw,z:P.z},t);if(!m)continue;
       samples++;const clear=raw-m.roofY;if(clear<0)sourceIntrusions++;minRoofClearance=Math.min(minRoofClearance,clear);
     }
   }
   return{name:t.name||'tunnel',samples,sourceIntrusions,mold:'EXACT_RENDER_GEOMETRY',terrainHeightModified:false,minSourceClearanceToInnerRoofM:Number.isFinite(minRoofClearance)?+minRoofClearance.toFixed(3):null};
 });
};
window.apexTunnelTerrainDiagnostics=()=>{
 const idx=apexGradeSpatialIndex(),g=apexPlateTerrainGrid(),L=trackData&&trackData.km*1000;if(!idx||!g||!L)return[];
 return idx.tunnels.map(t=>{
  const mid=mod(t.s0+wrapDeltaS(t.s1,t.s0,L)*.5,L),samples=[t.s0,mid,t.s1].map(s=>{
   const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s),raw=apexTerrainRawInterpolated(g,q.x,q.z),ground=apexPhase1GroundBlend(q.x,q.z,raw,{s,lat:0});
   return{s,trackY:q.y,roofTopY:q.y+t.topRiseM,rawTerrainY:raw,renderTerrainY:ground};
  });return{name:t.name,openingHeightM:t.roofH,roofTopRiseM:t.topRiseM,samples};
 });
};
window.apexPhase1GroundAt=(s,side=1,extra=0)=>{
 if(!trackData)return null;const e=envProfile(s),q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s),w=apexRoadFlatLimit(s,e,side),lat=side*(w+extra),x=q.x+q.nx*lat,z=q.z+q.nz*lat;
 return {s,side,lat,structure:(apexPhase1StructureAtWorld(x,z,s)||{}).kind||null,
  trackCenterY:q.y,bankDeg:q.bank*180/Math.PI,roadEdgeY:apexRoadSideHeight(q,e,side),
  flatLimitM:w,naturalY:apexTerrainHeightAtWorld(x,z,terrainGroundY({s,q,env:e})),groundY:apexWorldGroundY(x,z)};
};
// Read-only development helper for validating any imported circuit (not Madrid-specific).
window.apexTerrainDiagnostics=()=>{
 const cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld,g=cw&&cw._terrainContourGrid,q=trackData&&renderRibbonFrameAt(0);
 if(!g)return{imported:!!cw,base:!!(cw&&cw.terrainBase),grid:false};
 const values=g.vals||[],mn=Math.min(...values),mx=Math.max(...values);
 return{imported:!!cw,base:!!(cw&&cw.terrainBase),baseLevelM:g.baseLevelM,
   gridSize:[g.nx,g.nz],cellM:g.cell,mesh:g.plateMesh||false,
   terrainMinM:mn,terrainMaxM:mx,finishTrackY:q&&q.y,
   finishTerrainY:q&&apexTerrainHeightAtWorld(q.x,q.z,null),contourCount:cw.terrainContours.length};
};
function terrainSpread(st){
 const edge=terrainOuterWidth(st),spatial=terrainSpatialAt(st),k=Math.abs(curvatureAt(st.s));
 // En giro fuerte reducimos el radio de extrusión. En trazados compactos también
 // lo limita la mitad del espacio libre hasta el siguiente brazo del circuito.
 const curve=clamp(1/(1+k*46),.30,1);
 let curveFar=18+64*curve;
 const id=trackData&&trackData.spec?trackData.spec.dir:"";
 const spatialFar=Number.isFinite(spatial.clearance)?Math.max(20,spatial.clearance*.43-edge):82;
 const farMax=Math.max(20,finite(+apexCircuitVisualFeature('terrainFarMaxM',82),82));
 let far=clamp(Math.min(curveFar,spatialFar,farMax),20,farMax);
 if(st&&st.env&&st.env.scenery==='GRADE_CROSSING')far=Math.min(far,Math.max(20,finite(+apexCircuitVisualFeature('gradeCrossingTerrainFarMaxM',farMax),farMax)));
 // v5.230.37 · En un túnel con relieve SVG no recortamos la montaña a la franja
 // inmediata de pista. Si el SVG contiene relieve alrededor de un túnel, conservamos
 // sus cotas laterales con el mismo número de bandas.
 const authoredTunnelRelief=terrainAuthoredTunnelReliefAtS(st&&st.s);
 const j=readStoredCircuitJson(id)||{},tc=j.terrain||{},jsonFar=Number.isFinite(+tc.renderDistance)?+tc.renderDistance:null;
 if(st&&st.env&&st.env.tunnel)far=Math.max(far,Number.isFinite(jsonFar)?jsonFar:118);
 if(authoredTunnelRelief)far=Math.max(far,Number.isFinite(jsonFar)?jsonFar:176);
 const near=clamp(far*.22,6.0,10.5);
 const mid=clamp(far*.54,near+6,Math.max(near+6,far-7));
 return{near,mid,far};
}
function terrainEase(t){t=clamp(t,0,1);return t*t*(3-2*t)}
function terrainPointCamera(st,side,extra,cam,heightBlend=0,drop=0){
 const q=st.q,blend=terrainEase(heightBlend),dir=terrainSideDir(q,blend),w=apexRoadFlatLimit(st.s,st.env||envProfile(st.s),side)+extra;
 const baseGround=terrainGroundY(st),wx=q.x+dir.x*w*side,wz=q.z+dir.z*w*side,ground=apexTerrainHeightAtWorld(wx,wz,baseGround),preserveTunnel=!!(st&&st.env&&st.env.tunnel)||terrainTunnelPreserveAtS(st&&st.s);
 // Regla normal: el terreno enlaza suavemente con la carretera. Regla túnel: se anula
 // esa interpolación y se conserva la cota natural desde la propia boca del túnel.
 const edgeY=apexRoadSideHeight(q,st.env||envProfile(st.s),side);
 const y=(preserveTunnel?ground:apexPhase1GroundBlend(wx,wz,lerp(edgeY,ground,blend)))-drop;
 const P={x:wx,y,z:wz};
 return worldToCamera(P.x,P.y,P.z,cam);
}
function terrainSurfaceFor(st){
 if(st&&st.env&&st.env.tunnel)return "ASPHALT";
 return (st&&st.env&&st.env.offSurface)||"GRASS";
}
function terrainVisualProfile(st){
 const env=st&&st.env?st.env:envProfile(st&&Number.isFinite(st.s)?st.s:0),preserveTunnel=terrainTunnelPreserveAtS(st&&st.s),near=(env.tunnel||preserveTunnel)?((((readStoredCircuitJson(trackData&&trackData.spec?trackData.spec.dir:"")||{}).environment||{}).surfaces||{}).offSurface||"GRASS"):((env.runoffSurface&&env.runoffSurface!=="ASPHALT")?env.runoffSurface:(env.offSurface||"GRASS"));
 return {near,mid:near,far:near,detail:"NONE"};
}
function terrainWaterSideProfile(st){return {leftNear:null,leftMid:null,leftFar:null,rightNear:null,rightMid:null,rightFar:null}}
function terrainShade(surface,far=false,alt=false){
 const c=surfaceColor(surface,alt);
 return far?shadeColor(c,-12):shadeColor(c,alt?5:0);
}
function drawTerrainSegment(seg,cam,pitch){
 const a=seg.a,b=seg.b;if(!a||!b||!a.v||!b.v)return;
 if(apexPlateTerrainGrid()||['OVER','UNDER'].includes((authoredStructureZoneAtS(a.s)||{}).kind)||['OVER','UNDER'].includes((authoredStructureZoneAtS(b.s)||{}).kind))return;
 const visual=terrainVisualProfile(a),water=terrainWaterSideProfile(a);
 const seed=Math.floor((Math.abs(a.s)+Math.abs(b.s))*.17),sa=terrainSpread(a),sb=terrainSpread(b);

 // Cada extremo puede tener una anchura distinta. Así el terreno se estrecha de
 // forma gradual cerca de una horquilla o de otro brazo del circuito.
 const aLN=terrainPointCamera(a, 1,sa.near,cam,.04,.035),bLN=terrainPointCamera(b, 1,sb.near,cam,.04,.035);
 const aRN=terrainPointCamera(a,-1,sa.near,cam,.04,.035),bRN=terrainPointCamera(b,-1,sb.near,cam,.04,.035);
 const aLM=terrainPointCamera(a, 1,sa.mid, cam,.72,.07), bLM=terrainPointCamera(b, 1,sb.mid, cam,.72,.07);
 const aRM=terrainPointCamera(a,-1,sa.mid, cam,.72,.07), bRM=terrainPointCamera(b,-1,sb.mid, cam,.72,.07);
 const aLF=terrainPointCamera(a, 1,sa.far, cam,1.00,.12), bLF=terrainPointCamera(b, 1,sb.far, cam,1.00,.12);
 const aRF=terrainPointCamera(a,-1,sa.far, cam,1.00,.12), bRF=terrainPointCamera(b,-1,sb.far, cam,1.00,.12);

 const inTunnel=!!((a.env&&a.env.tunnel)||(b.env&&b.env.tunnel)),baseL=inTunnel?'#4b5055':terrainShade(water.leftNear||visual.near,false,seed&1),baseR=inTunnel?'#474c51':terrainShade(water.rightNear||visual.near,false,(seed+1)&1),
       midL=terrainShade(water.leftMid||visual.mid,false,(seed>>1)&1),midR=terrainShade(water.rightMid||visual.mid,false,((seed>>1)+1)&1),
       farL=terrainShade(water.leftFar||visual.far,true,(seed>>2)&1),farR=terrainShade(water.rightFar||visual.far,true,((seed>>2)+1)&1);

 drawCamQuadTriangles(a.v.lx,a.v.rx,b.v.rx,b.v.lx,pitch,terrainShade(visual.near,false,seed&1),.58);
 drawCamQuadTriangles(a.v.lx,aLN,bLN,b.v.lx,pitch,baseL,.58);
 drawCamQuadTriangles(aRN,a.v.rx,b.v.rx,bRN,pitch,baseR,.58);
 drawCamQuadTriangles(aLN,aLM,bLM,bLN,pitch,midL,.58);
 drawCamQuadTriangles(aRM,aRN,bRN,bRM,pitch,midR,.58);
 if(apexPlateTerrainGrid())return;
 if(terrainAuthoredTunnelReliefAtS(a.s)||terrainAuthoredTunnelReliefAtS(b.s)){
   const aRidge=lerp(sa.mid,sa.far,.56),bRidge=lerp(sb.mid,sb.far,.56),aLR=terrainPointCamera(a,1,aRidge,cam,.90,.09),bLR=terrainPointCamera(b,1,bRidge,cam,.90,.09),aRR2=terrainPointCamera(a,-1,aRidge,cam,.90,.09),bRR2=terrainPointCamera(b,-1,bRidge,cam,.90,.09);
   drawCamQuadTriangles(aLM,aLR,bLR,bLM,pitch,midL,.58);drawCamQuadTriangles(aLR,aLF,bLF,bLR,pitch,farL,.58);
   drawCamQuadTriangles(aRR2,aRM,bRM,bRR2,pitch,midR,.58);drawCamQuadTriangles(aRF,aRR2,bRR2,bRF,pitch,farR,.58);
 }else{
   drawCamQuadTriangles(aLM,aLF,bLF,bLM,pitch,farL,.58);
   drawCamQuadTriangles(aRF,aRM,bRM,bRF,pitch,farR,.58);
 }

 // Una referencia de profundidad muy discreta, solo en la transición.
 if(seg.z>8&&seg.z<155&&mod(seed,11)===0){
   drawCamLine(aLM,bLM,pitch,'rgba(0,0,0,.055)',Math.max(1,Math.min(1.6,95/Math.max(55,seg.z))),.58);
   drawCamLine(aRM,bRM,pitch,'rgba(0,0,0,.055)',Math.max(1,Math.min(1.6,95/Math.max(55,seg.z))),.58);
 }

 // Detalle muy ligero para que parques y callejeros no parezcan un plano vacío.
 if(seg.z>10&&seg.z<190){
   const laneW=Math.max(1,Math.min(1.9,92/Math.max(50,seg.z)));
   if(visual.detail==="ACCESS_ROAD"&&mod(seed,9)===0){
     const lc='rgba(92,99,106,.18)';
     drawCamLine(aLM,bLM,pitch,lc,laneW,.46);
     drawCamLine(aRM,bRM,pitch,lc,laneW,.46);
   }else if(visual.detail==="CITY_STREET"&&mod(seed,7)===0){
     const lc='rgba(215,222,228,.14)';
     drawCamLine(aLM,bLM,pitch,lc,laneW,.42);
     drawCamLine(aRM,bRM,pitch,lc,laneW,.42);
   }
 }
}

function drawTerrainSegmentMobile(seg,cam,pitch){
 const a=seg.a,b=seg.b;if(!a||!b||!a.v||!b.v)return;
 if(apexPlateTerrainGrid()||['OVER','UNDER'].includes((authoredStructureZoneAtS(a.s)||{}).kind)||['OVER','UNDER'].includes((authoredStructureZoneAtS(b.s)||{}).kind))return;
 const mp=mobileControlProfile(),supportOnly=seg.z>mp.terrainMax;
 // Nunca descartamos TODO el terreno de un tramo de pista que todavía es visible.
 // A distancia sólo conservamos el soporte lateral mínimo, evitando carreteras flotantes.
 const plateWorld=!!apexPlateTerrainGrid();
 const visual=terrainVisualProfile(a),seed=Math.floor((Math.abs(a.s)+Math.abs(b.s))*.17),sa=terrainSpread(a),sb=terrainSpread(b),t=renderQualityT();
 const nearExtra=Math.min(sa.near,lerp(7.4,10.2,t)),nearExtraB=Math.min(sb.near,lerp(7.4,10.2,t));
 const aLN=terrainPointCamera(a, 1,nearExtra,cam,.04,.035),bLN=terrainPointCamera(b, 1,nearExtraB,cam,.04,.035);
 const aRN=terrainPointCamera(a,-1,nearExtra,cam,.04,.035),bRN=terrainPointCamera(b,-1,nearExtraB,cam,.04,.035);
 const base=terrainShade(visual.near,false,seed&1);
 drawCamQuadTriangles(a.v.lx,a.v.rx,b.v.rx,b.v.lx,pitch,base,.58);
 drawCamQuadTriangles(a.v.lx,aLN,bLN,b.v.lx,pitch,base,.58);
 drawCamQuadTriangles(aRN,a.v.rx,b.v.rx,bRN,pitch,base,.58);
 if(!plateWorld&&((t>.24&&seg.z<mp.terrainMid)||supportOnly)){
   const supportMid=supportOnly?Math.max(22,lerp(13,30,t)):lerp(13,30,t),midExtraA=Math.min(sa.mid,supportMid),midExtraB=Math.min(sb.mid,supportMid);
   const aLM=terrainPointCamera(a, 1,midExtraA,cam,.66,.06),bLM=terrainPointCamera(b, 1,midExtraB,cam,.66,.06);
   const aRM=terrainPointCamera(a,-1,midExtraA,cam,.66,.06),bRM=terrainPointCamera(b,-1,midExtraB,cam,.66,.06);
   const mid=terrainShade(visual.mid,false,(seed>>1)&1);
   drawCamQuadTriangles(aLN,aLM,bLM,bLN,pitch,mid,.58);drawCamQuadTriangles(aRM,aRN,bRN,bRM,pitch,mid,.58);
   if(t>.68&&seg.z<mp.terrainFull){
     const farExtraA=Math.min(sa.far,lerp(28,62,t)),farExtraB=Math.min(sb.far,lerp(28,62,t));
     const aLF=terrainPointCamera(a, 1,farExtraA,cam,1,.10),bLF=terrainPointCamera(b, 1,farExtraB,cam,1,.10);
     const aRF=terrainPointCamera(a,-1,farExtraA,cam,1,.10),bRF=terrainPointCamera(b,-1,farExtraB,cam,1,.10);
     const far=terrainShade(visual.far,true,(seed>>2)&1);
     drawCamQuadTriangles(aLM,aLF,bLF,bLM,pitch,far,.58);drawCamQuadTriangles(aRF,aRM,bRM,bRF,pitch,far,.58);
   }
 }
}

function roadStepFrontExtent(st){
 if(!st||!st.v)return-Infinity;
 const pts=[st.v.c,st.v.l,st.v.r,st.v.lo,st.v.ro,st.v.lx,st.v.rx];
 let mz=-Infinity;
 for(const p of pts)if(p&&Number.isFinite(p.z))mz=Math.max(mz,p.z);
 return mz;
}
function roadSegmentDepth(a,b,near=.64){
 const pts=[a.v.l,a.v.r,a.v.lx,a.v.rx,b.v.l,b.v.r,b.v.lx,b.v.rx];
 let sum=0,n=0,maxZ=near;
 for(const p of pts){
   if(!p||!Number.isFinite(p.z)||p.z<near)continue;
   sum+=p.z;n++;maxZ=Math.max(maxZ,p.z);
 }
 // En segmentos que atraviesan el plano de cámara usamos la zona realmente visible,
 // no el centro de pista, que puede estar a z≈0 cuando el coche está perpendicular.
 return n?sum/n:maxZ;
}
function roadSegmentIsSane(a,b){
 if(!a||!b||!a.v||!b.v||!a.q||!b.q)return false;
 const req=[a.v.c,b.v.c,a.v.l,a.v.r,b.v.l,b.v.r,a.v.lx,a.v.rx,b.v.lx,b.v.rx];
 if(req.some(p=>!p||![p.x,p.y,p.z].every(Number.isFinite)))return false;

 const worldJump=Math.hypot(b.q.x-a.q.x,b.q.z-a.q.z);
 if(!Number.isFinite(worldJump)||worldJump>22)return false;
 const wa=Math.hypot(a.v.r.x-a.v.l.x,a.v.r.y-a.v.l.y,a.v.r.z-a.v.l.z),wb=Math.hypot(b.v.r.x-b.v.l.x,b.v.r.y-b.v.l.y,b.v.r.z-b.v.l.z);
 if(!Number.isFinite(wa)||!Number.isFinite(wb)||wa<1||wb<1||wa>Math.max(40,finite(a.env&&a.env.half,6.2)*4.6)||wb>Math.max(40,finite(b.env&&b.env.half,6.2)*4.6))return false;

 // No usamos ya la profundidad del centro. Si cualquier parte de la cinta entra
 // delante del near-plane, el segmento puede contribuir a la imagen.
 const front=Math.max(roadStepFrontExtent(a),roadStepFrontExtent(b));
 if(front<.50)return false;
 return true;
}
function apexRoadMeshSamples(){
 if(trackData._continuousRoadS)return trackData._continuousRoadS;
 const L=trackData.km*1000,n=trackData.pts.length,list=[0,L],step=L/n;
 for(let i=1;i<n;i++)list.push(i*step);
 for(let p=0;p<L;p+=Math.max(.7,2/(1+Math.abs(curvatureAt(p))*70)))list.push(p);
 for(const r of trackData.cornerRegions||[]){list.push(r.startS,r.endS);const ent=(trackData.authoredBanking||[]).find(b=>b.turn===r.turn);for(const p of ent&&ent.profile||[])list.push(mod(r.startS+p[0]*r.lengthM,L));}
 for(const p of trackData.verticalProfile&&trackData.verticalProfile.samples||[])list.push(clamp(p[0],0,1)*L);
 list.sort((a,b)=>a-b);return trackData._continuousRoadS=list.filter((v,i)=>!i||v-list[i-1]>.0001);
}
function collectRoadBranch(sign,cam,pitch,maxD=540,near=.64){
 const samples=apexRoadMeshSamples(),L=trackData.km*1000;
 if(apexInspectionFullScene()){if(sign<0)return[];return samples.map(p=>roadStep(p,p,cam,pitch));}
 const center=mod(player.s,L),raw=[roadStep(center,0,cam,pitch)];let lo=0,hi=samples.length;
 while(lo<hi){const m=(lo+hi)>>1;if(samples[m]<=center)lo=m+1;else hi=m;}
 // Exclude duplicate seam sample; walk fixed nodes around the lap in either direction.
 const count=samples.length-1;let index=sign>0?lo:lo-1;
 for(let walked=0;walked<count;walked++,index+=sign){const ss=samples[mod(index,count)],d=sign>0?mod(ss-center,L):mod(center-ss,L);if(d<.0001)continue;if(d>maxD)break;const st=roadStep(center+sign*d,sign*d,cam,pitch);if(st&&Number.isFinite(roadStepFrontExtent(st)))raw.push(st);}
 const end=roadStep(center+sign*Math.min(maxD,L-.001),sign*Math.min(maxD,L-.001),cam,pitch);if(end&&Number.isFinite(roadStepFrontExtent(end)))raw.push(end);
 return raw;
}

function drawNearRoadSafetyPatch(cam,pitch){
 if(apexInspectionActive())return;
 if(!player||!trackData)return;
 // Parche local de ~34 m alrededor del coche. Se dibuja antes de la cinta normal y
 // evita huecos de near-plane cuando el cockpit queda casi perpendicular al trazado.
 const q=poseAt(player.s),env=envProfile(player.s),roadHalf=env.half,outer=roadHalf+env.curb+env.runoff;
 const make=(along,lat,yOff=0)=>worldToCamera(q.x+q.tx*along+q.nx*lat,q.y+Math.tan(q.bank||0)*clamp(lat,-roadHalf,roadHalf)+yOff,q.z+q.tz*along+q.nz*lat,cam);
 const a=-17,b=17,outerL=apexRoadFlatLimit(player.s,env,1),outerR=apexRoadFlatLimit(player.s,env,-1);
 const o1=make(a,outerL),o2=make(a,-outerR),o3=make(b,-outerR),o4=make(b,outerL);
 const r1=make(a, roadHalf),r2=make(a,-roadHalf),r3=make(b,-roadHalf),r4=make(b, roadHalf);
 // Runoff completo primero; luego el asfalto principal. El clipping 3D contra near
 // rellena el primer plano incluso cuando uno de los bordes queda detrás de cámara.
 drawCamQuadTriangles(o1,o2,o3,o4,pitch,surfaceColor(env.runoffSurface,false),.34);
 drawCamQuadTriangles(r1,r2,r3,r4,pitch,palette().road,.34);
}

function drawRoad(camArg=null){
 const _roadT0=performance.now();let _roadCarsMs=0;
 const cam=camArg||driverCamera(),pitch=cam.pitch,near=.64,mp=mobileControlProfile(),inspectionFull=(typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene());
 const mainSign=(player&&player.reverse)?-1:1;
 // En inspección 3D recopilamos la vuelta completa en ambas direcciones para que al
 // alejar la cámara nunca desaparezcan tramos de pista. En carrera se mantiene el presupuesto normal.
 const fullD=inspectionFull&&trackData?trackData.km*1000*.515:0;
 const branches=inspectionFull?[
   collectRoadBranch(1,cam,pitch,fullD,near),
   collectRoadBranch(-1,cam,pitch,fullD,near)
 ]:mobilePerformanceMode()?[
   collectRoadBranch(mainSign,cam,pitch,mp.roadForward,near),
   collectRoadBranch(-mainSign,cam,pitch,mp.roadRear,near)
 ]:[
   collectRoadBranch(1,cam,pitch,mp.roadForward,near),
   collectRoadBranch(-1,cam,pitch,mp.roadRear,near)
 ];
 const segments=[];
 for(let bi=0;bi<branches.length;bi++){
   const steps=branches[bi];
   for(let i=0;i<steps.length-1;i++){
     const a=steps[i],b=steps[i+1];
     if(!roadSegmentIsSane(a,b))continue;
     segments.push({a,b,z:roadSegmentDepth(a,b,near),branch:bi});
   }
 }

 // PASADA 1 — terreno. Se dibuja todo antes de asfalto/coches: el circuito queda
 // siempre apoyado sobre suelo y las bandas de ramas cercanas pueden solaparse sin
 // tapar jamás la pista.
 const terrainSegments=segments.slice().sort((A,B)=>B.z-A.z);
 if(apexPlateTerrainGrid())drawTerrainPlateMesh(cam,pitch);
 for(const seg of terrainSegments){if(inspectionFull&&apexPlateTerrainGrid())continue;if(mobilePerformanceMode()&&!inspectionFull)drawTerrainSegmentMobile(seg,cam,pitch);else drawTerrainSegment(seg,cam,pitch);}
 apexDrawFallbackStructureBanks(terrainSegments,cam,pitch);
 drawNearRoadSafetyPatch(cam,pitch);

 // PASADA 2 — pista y rivales en profundidad global.
 const cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld,hasExactWalls=!!(cw&&cw.authoritative&&Array.isArray(cw.wallSegments)&&cw.wallSegments.length),wallItems=hasExactWalls?collectExactWallRenderItems(cam):[];
 if(!hasExactWalls)for(const seg of segments){
   if(mobilePerformanceMode()&&!inspectionFull&&seg.z>mp.barrierMax)continue;
   const wl=buildWallRenderItem(seg,1,cam);if(wl)wallItems.push({kind:'wall',z:wl.z,data:wl});
   const wr=buildWallRenderItem(seg,-1,cam);if(wr)wallItems.push({kind:'wall',z:wr.z,data:wr});
 }
 const pitItems=collectExactPitRenderItems(cam),boardItems=collectBrakeBoardSprites(cam,pitch).map(v=>({kind:'board',z:v.z,data:v}));
 const items=[
   ...segments.map(seg=>({kind:'road',z:seg.z,data:seg})),
   ...pitItems,
   ...wallItems,
   ...boardItems,
   ...collectRivalSprites(cam,pitch).map(v=>({kind:'rival',z:v.z,data:v}))
 ].sort((A,B)=>B.z-A.z);
 for(const it of items){
   if(it.kind==='road')drawRoadSegment(it.data,cam,pitch);
   else if(it.kind==='pitExact')drawExactPitRenderItem(it.data,pitch);
   else if(it.kind==='wall')drawWallRenderItem(it.data,pitch);
   else if(it.kind==='board')drawBrakeBoardSprite(it.data);
   else {const _carT0=performance.now();drawRivalSprite(it.data);_roadCarsMs+=performance.now()-_carT0;}
 }
 apexDrawGenericPortalFaces(cam,pitch);
 const _roadGross=performance.now()-_roadT0;
 renderProfileAdd('carsMs',_roadCarsMs);
 renderProfileAdd('trackMs',Math.max(0,_roadGross-_roadCarsMs));
}

function drawTrackside(a,b,cam,pitch){
 const env=a.env,ci=circuitIdentity(),side=(a.i%70===0)?1:-1,dist=env.half+env.curb+env.runoff+(env.wall?2.15:4.9),qa=a.q;
 const wa={x:qa.x+qa.nx*dist*side,z:qa.z+qa.nz*dist*side};
 const ca=worldToCamera(wa.x,qa.y,wa.z,cam),pa=projectLocal(ca.x,ca.y,ca.z,pitch);if(!pa)return;
 const s=Math.min(78,pa.scale*5.8),sc=env.scenery||"";

 if(sc==="URBAN"||sc==="MAIN_STRAIGHT"||sc==="CITY_RETURN"||sc==="HIGH_SPEED"){
   const h=Math.min(108,pa.scale*9.6),w=Math.min(58,pa.scale*4.8);ctx.fillStyle=trackData.spec.theme==="night"?"#101827":"#858d92";ctx.fillRect(pa.x-w/2,pa.y-h,w,h*.72);ctx.fillStyle="#555e64";ctx.fillRect(pa.x-w/2,pa.y-h,w,h*.10);ctx.fillStyle="rgba(225,238,242,.22)";for(let yy=pa.y-h+7;yy<pa.y-h*.22;yy+=9)ctx.fillRect(pa.x-w*.30,yy,w*.60,2);
 }else if(sc==="HARBOR"){
   ctx.fillStyle="#d8d9d7";ctx.fillRect(pa.x-s*.24,pa.y-s*.58,s*.48,s*.58);ctx.fillStyle="#466f80";ctx.fillRect(pa.x-s*.18,pa.y-s*.44,s*.36,s*.15);
 }else if(sc==="TUNNEL_PORTAL"){
   ctx.fillStyle="#8f9499";ctx.fillRect(pa.x-s*.46,pa.y-s*.66,s*.92,s*.18);ctx.fillStyle="#e8d9a7";for(let i=0;i<3;i++){ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(pa.x+(i-1)*s*.18,pa.y-s*.72,Math.max(1,s*.035),0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill()}
 }else if(sc==="PIT_FOREST"||sc==="FOREST"){
   ctx.fillStyle="#173d24";ctx.fillRect(pa.x-s*.045,pa.y-s*.72,s*.09,s*.74);ctx.fillStyle="#225f35";ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(pa.x,pa.y-s*1.65);ctx.lineTo(pa.x-s*.50,pa.y-s*.35);ctx.lineTo(pa.x+s*.50,pa.y-s*.35);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();if(sc==="PIT_FOREST"){ctx.fillStyle="#596168";ctx.fillRect(pa.x-s*.75,pa.y-s*.24,s*1.5,s*.22)}
 }else if(sc==="GRADE_CROSSING"||sc==="SVG_BRIDGE"){
   ctx.fillStyle="#6a6f76";ctx.fillRect(pa.x-s*.42,pa.y-s*.60,s*.84,s*.12);ctx.fillStyle="#8b9198";ctx.fillRect(pa.x-s*.24,pa.y-s*.58,s*.10,s*.60);ctx.fillRect(pa.x+s*.14,pa.y-s*.58,s*.10,s*.60);ctx.fillStyle="#d6c78d";for(let i=0;i<3;i++){ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(pa.x+(i-1)*s*.18,pa.y-s*.54,Math.max(1,s*.03),0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill()}
 }else if(sc==="PELOUSE"||sc==="GENERAL_ADMISSION"){
   ctx.fillStyle="#4d5560";ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(pa.x-s*.92,pa.y);ctx.lineTo(pa.x-s*.70,pa.y-s*.22);ctx.lineTo(pa.x-s*.45,pa.y-s*.40);ctx.lineTo(pa.x-s*.18,pa.y-s*.54);ctx.lineTo(pa.x+s*.55,pa.y-s*.54);ctx.lineTo(pa.x+s*.88,pa.y);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
   ctx.fillStyle="#90979f";for(let i=0;i<5;i++)ctx.fillRect(pa.x-s*.55+i*s*.22,pa.y-s*(.49-i*.065),s*.16,s*.035);ctx.fillStyle="#2d333b";ctx.fillRect(pa.x-s*.96,pa.y-s*.06,s*1.9,s*.08);
 }else if(sc==="FOREST"){
   ctx.fillStyle="#173d24";ctx.fillRect(pa.x-s*.05,pa.y-s*.58,s*.10,s*.60);ctx.fillStyle="#2e6f39";ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(pa.x,pa.y-s*1.48);ctx.lineTo(pa.x-s*.45,pa.y-s*.28);ctx.lineTo(pa.x+s*.45,pa.y-s*.28);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
 }else if(sc==="LIGHTS"){
   ctx.strokeStyle="#c9d3dc";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,s*.025);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(pa.x,pa.y);ctx.lineTo(pa.x,pa.y-s*1.65);ctx.stroke();ctx.fillStyle="#e8f6ff";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(pa.x,pa.y-s*1.65,Math.max(2,s*.08),0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 }else{
   ctx.fillStyle="#173d24";ctx.fillRect(pa.x-s*.05,pa.y-s*.58,s*.10,s*.60);ctx.fillStyle=env.scenery==="DUNES"?"#476b3d":"#1e6337";ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(pa.x,pa.y-s*1.48);ctx.lineTo(pa.x-s*.45,pa.y-s*.28);ctx.lineTo(pa.x+s*.45,pa.y-s*.28);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
 }

 // Baliza de pista cercana: pequeña, barata y muy eficaz para transmitir velocidad.
 if(!env.tunnel&&a.z>9&&a.z<240&&a.i%26===0){ctx.strokeStyle="#d9dde0";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,Math.min(2,pa.scale*.018));ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(pa.x,pa.y);ctx.lineTo(pa.x,pa.y-Math.min(25,pa.scale*.55));ctx.stroke()}
}

function rivalBodyPath(c,w,h,shift,bodyMul=1){
 const bw=bodyMul;
 c.beginPath();
 c.moveTo(-w*.315*bw,-h*.17);
 c.lineTo(-w*.300*bw,-h*.43);
 c.quadraticCurveTo(-w*.285*bw,-h*.60,shift-w*.185*bw,-h*.69);
 c.lineTo(shift-w*.105*bw,-h*.91);
 c.quadraticCurveTo(shift-w*.065*bw,-h*1.035,shift, -h*1.055);
 c.quadraticCurveTo(shift+w*.065*bw,-h*1.035,shift+w*.105*bw,-h*.91);
 c.lineTo(shift+w*.185*bw,-h*.69);
 c.quadraticCurveTo(w*.285*bw,-h*.60,w*.300*bw,-h*.43);
 c.lineTo(w*.315*bw,-h*.17);
 c.lineTo(w*.205*bw,-h*.055);
 c.lineTo(-w*.205*bw,-h*.055);
 c.closePath();
}
function drawRivalLiveryPattern(v,w,h,shift,bodyMul,lod){
 if(lod<1)return;
 ctx.save();
 rivalBodyPath(ctx,w,h,shift,bodyMul);ctx.clip();
 const sw=Math.max(1,w*.018);
 if(v.pattern==='center'){
   ctx.fillStyle=v.stripe;ctx.fillRect(shift-w*.036,-h*1.08,w*.072,h*.66);
   ctx.fillStyle=v.trim;ctx.fillRect(shift-w*.010,-h*1.08,w*.020,h*.55);
 }else if(v.pattern==='double'){
   ctx.fillStyle=v.stripe;ctx.fillRect(shift-w*.080,-h*1.04,w*.032,h*.58);ctx.fillRect(shift+w*.048,-h*1.04,w*.032,h*.58);
   ctx.fillStyle=v.trim;ctx.fillRect(shift-w*.012,-h*.92,w*.024,h*.42);
 }else if(v.pattern==='chevron'){
   ctx.strokeStyle=v.stripe;ctx.lineWidth=sw*2.2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-w*.25,-h*.32);ctx.lineTo(shift,-h*.73);ctx.lineTo(w*.25,-h*.32);ctx.stroke();
   ctx.strokeStyle=v.trim;ctx.lineWidth=sw;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-w*.18,-h*.43);ctx.lineTo(shift,-h*.70);ctx.lineTo(w*.18,-h*.43);ctx.stroke();
 }else if(v.pattern==='split'){
   ctx.fillStyle=v.secondary;ctx.fillRect(-w*.36,-h*.78,w*.36,h*.70);ctx.fillStyle=v.stripe;ctx.fillRect(-w*.018,-h*1.05,w*.036,h*.91);
 }else if(v.pattern==='flank'){
   ctx.strokeStyle=v.stripe;ctx.lineWidth=sw*2.0;for(const sg of [-1,1]){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(sg*w*.28,-h*.23);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(sg*w*.20,-h*.58,shift+sg*w*.075,-h*.93);ctx.stroke();}
 }else if(v.pattern==='bars'){
   ctx.fillStyle=v.stripe;ctx.save();ctx.translate(shift,-h*.62);ctx.rotate(-.18);ctx.fillRect(-w*.22,-h*.035,w*.44,h*.07);ctx.translate(0,h*.14);ctx.fillRect(-w*.18,-h*.025,w*.36,h*.05);ctx.restore();
 }else if(v.pattern==='blade'){
   ctx.fillStyle=v.stripe;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(shift-w*.026,-h*1.04);ctx.lineTo(shift+w*.070,-h*.58);ctx.lineTo(w*.18,-h*.19);ctx.lineTo(w*.105,-h*.18);ctx.lineTo(shift+w*.018,-h*.63);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
 }else if(v.pattern==='pinstripe'){
   ctx.strokeStyle=v.stripe;ctx.lineWidth=sw;for(const sg of [-1,1]){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(sg*w*.26,-h*.20);ctx.lineTo(shift+sg*w*.12,-h*.67);ctx.lineTo(shift+sg*w*.055,-h*.99);ctx.stroke();}
 }else if(v.pattern==='spear'){
   ctx.fillStyle=v.stripe;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(shift,-h*1.04);ctx.lineTo(shift+w*.055,-h*.62);ctx.lineTo(w*.25,-h*.27);ctx.lineTo(w*.13,-h*.29);ctx.lineTo(shift,-h*.69);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
 }else if(v.pattern==='lux'){
   ctx.strokeStyle=v.stripe;ctx.lineWidth=sw*1.6;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-w*.25,-h*.27);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(shift,-h*.47,w*.25,-h*.27);ctx.stroke();ctx.fillStyle=v.trim;ctx.fillRect(shift-w*.018,-h*.98,w*.036,h*.35);
 }else{ // sweep
   ctx.strokeStyle=v.stripe;ctx.lineWidth=sw*2.1;for(const sg of [-1,1]){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(sg*w*.27,-h*.22);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(sg*w*.17,-h*.47,shift+sg*w*.045,-h*.95);ctx.stroke();}
 }
 ctx.restore();
}

function drawPseudoLogoIcon(x,y,s,mode,c1,c2){
 ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);ctx.scale(s,s);ctx.fillStyle=c1;ctx.strokeStyle=c2||c1;ctx.lineWidth=.18;
 if(mode==='star'){ctx.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<8;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/4,r=i%2?0.46:1.0;const px=Math.cos(a)*r,py=Math.sin(a)*r;i?ctx.lineTo(px,py):ctx.moveTo(px,py);}ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();}
 else if(mode==='horns'){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-1,.65);ctx.lineTo(-.25,-.05);ctx.lineTo(0,.22);ctx.lineTo(.25,-.05);ctx.lineTo(1,.65);ctx.lineTo(.32,.45);ctx.lineTo(0,.84);ctx.lineTo(-.32,.45);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();}
 else if(mode==='shield'){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,-1);ctx.lineTo(.86,-.32);ctx.lineTo(.54,.95);ctx.lineTo(0,1.22);ctx.lineTo(-.54,.95);ctx.lineTo(-.86,-.32);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.fillStyle=c2;ctx.fillRect(-.13,-.62,.26,1.10);}
 else if(mode==='doubleBar'){ctx.fillRect(-1,-.82,2,.34);ctx.fillRect(-.75,.20,1.5,.28);}
 else if(mode==='aeroA'){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,-1);ctx.lineTo(1,.9);ctx.lineTo(.44,.9);ctx.lineTo(.20,.38);ctx.lineTo(-.20,.38);ctx.lineTo(-.44,.9);ctx.lineTo(-1,.9);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();}
 else if(mode==='hash'){for(const xx of [-.72,-.20,.32])ctx.fillRect(xx,-.84,.26,1.68);}
 else if(mode==='ring'){ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,.95,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.globalCompositeOperation='destination-out';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(0,0,.48,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';}
 else if(mode==='wave'){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-1,.22);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-.45,-.62,0,.05);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(.45,.72,1,-.12);ctx.lineWidth=.28;ctx.stroke();}
 else if(mode==='leaf'){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,-1);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(.86,-.30,.22,1);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-.70,.25,0,-1);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.strokeStyle=c2;ctx.lineWidth=.12;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-.02,-.82);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(.06,.12,.10,.78);ctx.stroke();}
 else if(mode==='crest'){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-.84,-.74);ctx.lineTo(.84,-.74);ctx.lineTo(.84,.06);ctx.lineTo(0,1.06);ctx.lineTo(-.84,.06);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.fillStyle=c2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,-.40);ctx.lineTo(.32,.05);ctx.lineTo(0,.46);ctx.lineTo(-.32,.05);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();}
 else {ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-1,.40);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-.15,-.90,.35,-.20);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(.55,.05,1,-.22);ctx.lineWidth=.28;ctx.stroke();}
 ctx.restore();
}
function rivalPseudoSponsorBlocks(teamKey,v,w,h,mode,mirror=1,lod=2){
 if(lod<1)return;
 ctx.save();
 ctx.scale(mirror,1);
 const stripe=v.stripe,trim=v.trim,secondary=v.secondary,b=teamBranding(teamKey);
 if(mode==='front'){
   ctx.fillStyle=stripe;
   ctx.fillRect(-w*.055,-h*.58,w*.11,h*.28);
   ctx.fillStyle=trim;
   ctx.fillRect(-w*.020,-h*.53,w*.040,h*.20);
   ctx.fillStyle=secondary;
   ctx.fillRect(-w*.23,-h*.095,w*.09,h*.028);
   ctx.fillRect(w*.14,-h*.095,w*.09,h*.028);
   ctx.fillStyle=stripe;
   ctx.fillRect(-w*.32,-h*.030,w*.11,h*.020);
   ctx.fillRect(w*.21,-h*.030,w*.11,h*.020);
 }else if(mode==='side'){
   ctx.fillStyle=stripe;
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-w*.03,-h*.50);ctx.lineTo(w*.17,-h*.61);ctx.lineTo(w*.37,-h*.54);ctx.lineTo(w*.24,-h*.43);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
   ctx.fillStyle=trim;
   ctx.fillRect(w*.10,-h*.47,w*.10,h*.030);
   ctx.fillStyle=secondary;
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-w*.22,-h*.28);ctx.lineTo(-w*.10,-h*.33);ctx.lineTo(w*.02,-h*.30);ctx.lineTo(-w*.05,-h*.23);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
   ctx.fillStyle=stripe;
   ctx.fillRect(w*.39,-h*.16,w*.08,h*.018);
 }
 if(mode==='front'){
   if(b.sponsor==='chevrons'){ctx.strokeStyle=stripe;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,w*.012);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-w*.18,-h*.18);ctx.lineTo(0,-h*.25);ctx.lineTo(w*.18,-h*.18);ctx.stroke();}
   else if(b.sponsor==='bars'){ctx.fillStyle=trim;ctx.fillRect(-w*.16,-h*.34,w*.07,h*.022);ctx.fillRect(w*.09,-h*.34,w*.07,h*.022);}
   else if(b.sponsor==='steps'){ctx.fillStyle=stripe;ctx.fillRect(-w*.17,-h*.23,w*.04,h*.07);ctx.fillRect(-w*.11,-h*.20,w*.04,h*.04);}
   else if(b.sponsor==='flash'){ctx.fillStyle=trim;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-w*.10,-h*.33);ctx.lineTo(w*.00,-h*.44);ctx.lineTo(w*.03,-h*.38);ctx.lineTo(-w*.02,-h*.25);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();}
   drawPseudoLogoIcon(0,-h*.43,w*.040,b.logo,trim,stripe);
 }else if(mode==='side'){
   if(b.sponsor==='slashes'||b.sponsor==='ribbon'){ctx.strokeStyle=trim;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,w*.009);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(w*.00,-h*.40);ctx.lineTo(w*.18,-h*.53);ctx.stroke();}
   else if(b.sponsor==='blocks'){ctx.fillStyle=trim;ctx.fillRect(w*.03,-h*.33,w*.08,h*.028);ctx.fillRect(w*.14,-h*.30,w*.06,h*.022);}
   else if(b.sponsor==='lux'){ctx.strokeStyle=trim;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,w*.007);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(w*.03,-h*.46);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(w*.18,-h*.55,w*.33,-h*.49);ctx.stroke();}
   drawPseudoLogoIcon(w*.17,-h*.46,w*.034,b.logo,trim,stripe);
 }
 ctx.restore();
}
function drawRivalRearTyre(tx,ty,tw,th,v,lod,teamKey=null){
 const mark=teamBranding(teamKey).tyreColor;
 ctx.fillStyle='#030405';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(tx,ty,tw,th,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 ctx.fillStyle='#14171a';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(tx,ty-th*.03,tw*.79,th*.94,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 ctx.fillStyle='#08090a';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(tx,ty-th*.03,tw*.59,th*.77,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 if(lod===2){
   ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.13)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,tw*.040);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(tx,ty-th*.03,tw*.76,-1.05,1.05);ctx.stroke();
   ctx.strokeStyle=mark;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,tw*.030);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(tx,ty-th*.03,tw*.83,-.88,-.18);ctx.stroke();ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(tx,ty-th*.03,tw*.83,.18,.88);ctx.stroke();
   ctx.fillStyle=v.trim;ctx.globalAlpha=.38;ctx.fillRect(tx-tw*.80,ty-th*.55,tw*.08,th*.75);ctx.globalAlpha=1;
 }
}
function drawRivalFrontTyre(tx,ty,tw,th,v,lod,camber=0,steer=0,teamKey=null){
 ctx.save();ctx.translate(tx,ty);ctx.rotate(steer);
 const mark=teamBranding(teamKey).tyreColor;
 const sx=1+Math.abs(camber)*.22,sy=1-Math.abs(camber)*.06;
 ctx.scale(sx,sy);
 const grad=ctx.createRadialGradient(-tw*.15,-th*.12,tw*.10,0,0,tw*.95);
 grad.addColorStop(0,'#20242a');grad.addColorStop(.45,'#0e1115');grad.addColorStop(1,'#020304');
 ctx.fillStyle=grad;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,tw,th,camber,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 ctx.fillStyle='#11151a';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(-tw*.05,-th*.02,tw*.78,th*.90,camber,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 ctx.fillStyle='#06080a';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(-tw*.12,-th*.03,tw*.55,th*.72,camber,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 if(lod>0){
   ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.16)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,tw*.032);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(-tw*.06,-th*.02,tw*.72,-1.0,1.0);ctx.stroke();
   ctx.strokeStyle=mark;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,tw*.028);ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,tw*.86,th*.92,camber,-.92,-.22);ctx.stroke();ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,tw*.86,th*.92,camber,.22,.92);ctx.stroke();
   ctx.globalAlpha=.42;ctx.fillStyle=v.trim;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(tw*.63,0,tw*.10,th*.58,camber,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.globalAlpha=1;
 }
 ctx.restore();
}
function drawRivalSideTyres(w,h,v,lod,teamKey=null){
 const mark=teamBranding(teamKey).tyreColor;
 const rt={x:-w*.16,y:-h*.22,rw:w*.17,rh:h*.36};
 const ft={x:w*.47,y:-h*.17,rw:w*.12,rh:h*.28};
 const tyres=[rt,ft];
 for(const T of tyres){
   const grad=ctx.createRadialGradient(T.x-T.rw*.18,T.y-T.rh*.10,T.rw*.12,T.x,T.y,T.rw);
   grad.addColorStop(0,'#20242a');grad.addColorStop(.4,'#101318');grad.addColorStop(1,'#030405');
   ctx.fillStyle=grad;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(T.x,T.y,T.rw,T.rh,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
   ctx.fillStyle='#12161b';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(T.x-T.rw*.03,T.y,T.rw*.78,T.rh*.88,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
   ctx.fillStyle='#050608';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(T.x-T.rw*.10,T.y,T.rw*.58,T.rh*.65,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
   ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.13)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,w*.006);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(T.x-T.rw*.02,T.y,T.rw*.74,-.85,.85);ctx.stroke();
   if(lod===2){ctx.strokeStyle=mark;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,w*.005);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(T.x,T.y,T.rw*.88,-.78,.78);ctx.stroke();ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.28)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(T.x+T.rw*.12,T.y,T.rw*.46,T.rh*.48,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.globalAlpha=.42;ctx.fillStyle=v.trim;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(T.x+T.rw*.67,T.y,T.rw*.10,T.rh*.53,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.globalAlpha=1;}
 }
}
function drawRivalFrontSprite(a){
 const team=teamInfo(a.r.team),v=teamRearVisual(a.r.team),spec=carCatalog[team.model]||carCatalog.A26;
 const col=team.color,dark=shadeColor(col,-62),light=shadeColor(col,34);
 const shape=spec.rear||'narrow',shapeMul=shape==='wide'?1.06:shape==='compact'?.96:1;
 const w=clamp(a.p.scale*2.92*shapeMul,3.0,196),h=w*.70,x=a.p.x,y=a.p.y;
 const lod=Math.max(1,Math.min(w<10?1:w<26?1:2,Number.isFinite(a.visualLod)?Math.max(1,a.visualLod):2)),yaw=clamp(a.sideToCam*.18,-.16,.16),shift=yaw*w*.08;
 ctx.save();ctx.translate(x,y);
 ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.32)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,h*.030,w*.56,h*.090,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 if(lod===0){
   ctx.fillStyle='#060709';ctx.fillRect(-w*.50,-h*.13,w, h*.08);
   ctx.fillRect(-w*.45,-h*.45,w*.18,h*.35);ctx.fillRect(w*.27,-h*.45,w*.18,h*.35);
   ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-w*.10,-h*.58);ctx.lineTo(w*.10,-h*.58);ctx.lineTo(w*.06,-h*.08);ctx.lineTo(-w*.06,-h*.08);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
   ctx.restore();return;
 }
 // rear wing faintly visible above the halo
 if(lod===2){ctx.fillStyle=v.carbon;ctx.fillRect(-w*.16,-h*.86,w*.32,h*.055);ctx.fillStyle='#0d1115';ctx.fillRect(-w*.012,-h*.85,w*.024,h*.18);}
 // front wheels dominate
 drawRivalFrontTyre(shift-w*.36,-h*.24,w*.18,h*.38,v,lod,.03,-yaw*.7,a.r.team);
 drawRivalFrontTyre(shift+w*.36,-h*.24,w*.18,h*.38,v,lod,-.03,-yaw*.7,a.r.team);
 // suspension arms
 if(lod===2){
   ctx.strokeStyle='#171b20';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,w*.014);ctx.lineCap='round';
   for(const sg of [-1,1]){
     ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(shift+sg*w*.05,-h*.28);ctx.lineTo(shift+sg*w*.25,-h*.33);ctx.stroke();
     ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(shift+sg*w*.07,-h*.18);ctx.lineTo(shift+sg*w*.27,-h*.21);ctx.stroke();
     ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(shift+sg*w*.08,-h*.40);ctx.lineTo(shift+sg*w*.24,-h*.28);ctx.stroke();
   }
 }
 // front wing
 ctx.fillStyle=v.carbon;
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-w*.48,-h*.13);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0,-h*.20,w*.48,-h*.13);ctx.lineTo(w*.45,-h*.01);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0,-h*.06,-w*.45,-h*.01);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
 ctx.fillStyle='#171a1e';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-w*.40,-h*.075);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0,-h*.125,w*.40,-h*.075);ctx.lineTo(w*.38,-h*.03);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0,-h*.06,-w*.38,-h*.03);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
 for(const sg of [-1,1]){ctx.fillStyle=v.carbon;ctx.fillRect(sg*w*.43-w*.025,-h*.18,w*.05,h*.18);ctx.strokeStyle=v.stripe;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,w*.008);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(sg*w*.445,-h*.17);ctx.lineTo(sg*w*.445,-h*.04);ctx.stroke();}
 // body side shoulders and inlets
 ctx.fillStyle=dark;
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-w*.31,-h*.57);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-w*.35,-h*.44,-w*.32,-h*.27);ctx.lineTo(-w*.15,-h*.20);ctx.lineTo(-w*.10,-h*.48);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(w*.31,-h*.57);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(w*.35,-h*.44,w*.32,-h*.27);ctx.lineTo(w*.15,-h*.20);ctx.lineTo(w*.10,-h*.48);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
 // main nose and chassis
 ctx.fillStyle=col;
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(shift-w*.13,-h*.70);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(shift-w*.16,-h*.58,shift-w*.095,-h*.11);ctx.lineTo(shift+w*.095,-h*.11);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(shift+w*.16,-h*.58,shift+w*.13,-h*.70);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(shift, -h*.78,shift-w*.13,-h*.70);ctx.fill();
 ctx.fillStyle=light;ctx.globalAlpha=.18;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(shift-w*.020,-h*.67);ctx.lineTo(shift+w*.020,-h*.67);ctx.lineTo(shift+w*.010,-h*.15);ctx.lineTo(shift-w*.010,-h*.15);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.globalAlpha=1;
 // sidepod openings
 ctx.fillStyle='#06080c';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(shift-w*.21,-h*.45,w*.10,h*.09,-.12,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(shift+w*.21,-h*.45,w*.10,h*.09,.12,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 // halo and helmet
 ctx.fillStyle='#0b0f12';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(shift,-h*.63,w*.07,h*.08,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.fillStyle=v.stripe;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(shift,-h*.655,w*.036,Math.PI,0);ctx.fill();
 ctx.fillStyle=v.carbon;ctx.fillRect(shift-w*.010,-h*.73,w*.020,h*.38);
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(shift-w*.16,-h*.69);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(shift-w*.06,-h*.84,shift,-h*.84);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(shift+w*.06,-h*.84,shift+w*.16,-h*.69);ctx.lineTo(shift+w*.12,-h*.65);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(shift+w*.05,-h*.77,shift,-h*.78);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(shift-w*.05,-h*.77,shift-w*.12,-h*.65);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
 // mirrors and livery
 if(lod===2){ctx.fillStyle=v.carbon;ctx.fillRect(shift-w*.28,-h*.56,w*.08,h*.05);ctx.fillRect(shift+w*.20,-h*.56,w*.08,h*.05);}
 rivalPseudoSponsorBlocks(a.r.team,v,w,h,'front',1,lod);

}
function drawRivalSideSprite(a){

   ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-w*.22,-h*.40);ctx.lineTo(w*.18,-h*.48);ctx.lineTo(w*.42,-h*.30);ctx.lineTo(w*.06,-h*.12);ctx.lineTo(-w*.18,-h*.16);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();

 // floor and wings

 // cockpit opening and halo

// === v5.163.1 · RIVAL ATLAS INTERFACE (SAFE PREP) ===
// The next WebGL car pass can bind a transparent 4x4 / 16-angle texture here.
// It is intentionally disabled in this repair build; current vector rivals remain
// active so v5.162 visual stability is preserved until the atlas has correct
// transparent cut-outs, pivots and a real WebGL texture path.
const RIVAL_ATLAS16 = {
  enabled:false,
  columns:4,
  rows:4,
  angles:[0,22.5,45,67.5,90,112.5,135,157.5,180,202.5,225,247.5,270,292.5,315,337.5],
  pivotX:0.5,
  pivotY:0.88,
  source:null
};

function rivalAtlas16AngleIndex(facing,sideToCam){
  let angle=Math.atan2(-finite(sideToCam,0),finite(facing,0))*180/Math.PI;
  if(angle<0) angle+=360;
  return Math.round(angle/22.5)%16;
}

// v5.230.62.4 · LOD VISUAL DE RIVALES EXCLUSIVAMENTE 3D EN WEBGL (heredado de v62.3.1).
// La IA/física no cambia. Nivel 2 = 3D completo; 1 = 3D medio; 0 = 3D low-poly.
// Nunca existe impostor 2D como LOD lejano. Si se supera el presupuesto, sólo baja
// la complejidad de la malla 3D. Canvas sigue siendo únicamente un backend de emergencia.
let apexRivalLodFrameStats={visible:0,near:0,mid:0,far:0,gpu3d:0,gpuFull:0,gpuMid:0,gpuLow:0,canvasFallback:0};
function apexRivalLodSettings(){
 const mobile=mobilePerformanceMode(),pressure=mobile?(typeof mobileAdaptiveRenderPressure==='function'?mobileAdaptiveRenderPressure():1):(typeof desktopAdaptiveRenderPressure==='function'?desktopAdaptiveRenderPressure():1),q=clamp(Number.isFinite(pressure)?pressure:1,0,1);
 return mobile
   ?{fullZ:lerp(34,58,q),midZ:lerp(74,120,q),fullScale:10.2,midScale:5.7,fullBudget:Math.max(4,Math.round(lerp(4,7,q))),midBudget:Math.max(9,Math.round(lerp(10,15,q)))}
   :{fullZ:lerp(44,72,q),midZ:lerp(98,152,q),fullScale:10.0,midScale:5.5,fullBudget:Math.max(7,Math.round(lerp(8,11,q))),midBudget:Math.max(14,Math.round(lerp(15,19,q)))};
}
function apexRivalVisualLod(z,scale){
 const cfg=apexRivalLodSettings(),d=Math.max(0,Number(z)||0),sc=Math.max(0,Number(scale)||0);
 if(d<=cfg.fullZ||sc>=cfg.fullScale)return 2;
 if(d<=cfg.midZ||sc>=cfg.midScale)return 1;
 return 0; // low-poly 3D, nunca 2D
}
function apexRivalLodStatsSnapshot(){return{...apexRivalLodFrameStats,settings:apexRivalLodSettings(),mode:'3D_ONLY'};}

function rivalLikelyOccluded(q,lat,c,p,cam,pitch,visualLod=2){
 if(!trackData||!player||c.z<68)return false;
 const L=trackData.km*1000,ds=wrapDeltaS(q.s,player.s,L),dist=Math.abs(ds);
 if(dist<42)return false;
 const maxSteps=visualLod>=2?8:(visualLod===1?6:4),div=visualLod>=2?22:(visualLod===1?29:42);
 const steps=Math.max(3,Math.min(maxSteps,Math.round(dist/div)));
 let hits=0;
 for(let i=1;i<steps;i++){
   const t=i/steps,ss=mod(player.s+ds*t,L),qq=renderRibbonFrameAt(ss),sLat=lat*t;
   const P=ribbonWorldPoint(qq,Math.abs(sLat),sLat>=0?1:-1,.10),cc=worldToCamera(P.x,P.y,P.z,cam);
   if(!cc||cc.z<1.0||cc.z>c.z-2.2)continue;
   const pp=projectLocal(cc.x,cc.y,cc.z,pitch);if(!pp)continue;
   const dx=Math.abs(pp.x-p.x),dy=pp.y-p.y;
   if(dx<Math.max(24,p.scale*.58)&&dy<p.scale*.10&&dy>-p.scale*1.55){hits++;if(hits>=2)return true;}
 }
 return false;
}

// Canvas de emergencia no dispone de z-buffer. Para conservar la misma regla visual que WebGL,
// proyectamos las caras reales de edificios/boxes/gradas/puentes y ocultamos el coche si su
// punto central queda detrás de una de ellas. En WebGL NO se usa: el depth-buffer es exacto.
let apexRivalCanvasOccluderFrame=-1,apexRivalCanvasOccluders=[];
function apexPointInScreenPoly(x,y,poly){
 let inside=false;for(let i=0,j=poly.length-1;i<poly.length;j=i++){
  const a=poly[i],b=poly[j],yi=a[1],yj=b[1],xi=a[0],xj=b[0],cross=((yi>y)!==(yj>y))&&(x<(xj-xi)*(y-yi)/((yj-yi)||1e-9)+xi);if(cross)inside=!inside;
 }return inside;
}
function apexBuildRivalCanvasOccluders(cam,pitch){
 if(apexRivalCanvasOccluderFrame===renderFrameId)return apexRivalCanvasOccluders;
 apexRivalCanvasOccluderFrame=renderFrameId;const out=[],near=.52,allowed=new Set(['block','building','pit','grandstand','tower','bridge','tunnelportal','gantry']);
 const entries=typeof apexCompiledLandmarkEntries==='function'?apexCompiledLandmarkEntries(trackLandmarks()):trackLandmarks().map((it,i)=>({it,i}));
 for(const ent of entries){const it=ent.it||ent,t=String(it&&it.type||'').toLowerCase();if(!it||!allowed.has(t)||!Array.isArray(it.worldFootprint)||it.worldFootprint.length<3)continue;
  const fp=it.worldFootprint,a=it.anchor||{},wy=Array.isArray(it.worldY)?it.worldY:null,h=Math.max(.15,Number(it.height)||.15),bot=[],top=[];
  for(let i=0;i<fp.length;i++){const v=fp[i],gy=wy&&Number.isFinite(wy[i])?wy[i]:(Number.isFinite(a.y)?a.y:apexWorldGroundY(v[0],v[1]));bot.push(worldToCamera(v[0],gy,v[1],cam));top.push(worldToCamera(v[0],gy+h,v[1],cam));}
  const faces=[];for(let i=0;i<fp.length;i++){const k=(i+1)%fp.length;faces.push([bot[i],bot[k],top[k],top[i]])}faces.push(top);
  for(const face of faces){const clipped=clipPolyNear(face,near);if(clipped.length<3)continue;const poly=projectCameraPoly(clipped,pitch);if(!poly||poly.length<3)continue;let z=Infinity,minX=Infinity,maxX=-Infinity,minY=Infinity,maxY=-Infinity;for(const c of clipped)z=Math.min(z,c.z);for(const p of poly){minX=Math.min(minX,p[0]);maxX=Math.max(maxX,p[0]);minY=Math.min(minY,p[1]);maxY=Math.max(maxY,p[1]);}if(maxX<0||minX>W||maxY<0||minY>H)continue;out.push({poly,z,minX,maxX,minY,maxY});}
 }
 apexRivalCanvasOccluders=out;return out;
}
function apexRivalOccludedByWorldObjectCanvas(c,p,cam,pitch){
 if(rendererBackendId()!=='canvas2d'||!c||!p)return false;for(const o of apexBuildRivalCanvasOccluders(cam,pitch)){if(o.z>=c.z-.45||p.x<o.minX||p.x>o.maxX||p.y<o.minY||p.y>o.maxY)continue;if(apexPointInScreenPoly(p.x,p.y,o.poly))return true;}return false;
}

function collectRivalSprites(cam,pitch){
 const arr=[];apexRivalLodFrameStats={visible:0,near:0,mid:0,far:0,gpu3d:0,gpuFull:0,gpuMid:0,gpuLow:0,canvasFallback:0};
 for(const r of rivals){
   const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(r.s),surface=trackSurfacePoseAt(r.s,r.lat,.014),wx=surface.x,wz=surface.z,c=worldToCamera(wx,surface.y,wz,cam);
   if(c.z<2.2||c.z>mobileControlProfile().rivalMax)continue;
   const p=projectLocal(c.x,c.y,c.z,pitch);if(!p)continue;
   const visualLod=apexRivalVisualLod(c.z,p.scale);
   if(rivalLikelyOccluded(q,r.lat,c,p,cam,pitch,visualLod))continue;
   if(apexRivalOccludedByWorldObjectCanvas(c,p,cam,pitch))continue;
   const rh=Math.atan2(q.tx,q.tz)+(r.yawKick||0),rfx=Math.sin(rh),rfz=Math.cos(rh);
   const toCamX=cam.x-wx,toCamZ=cam.z-wz,dist=Math.hypot(toCamX,toCamZ)||1;
   const dirX=toCamX/dist,dirZ=toCamZ/dist;
   const facing=rfx*dirX+rfz*dirZ; // >0 nose towards camera
   const sideToCam=rfx*dirZ-rfz*dirX;
   const view=(Math.abs(sideToCam)>.50&&c.z<220)?'side':(facing>.24?'front':'rear');
   const side=rfx*cam.nx+rfz*cam.nz;
   const raw=Math.atan2(side,Math.max(.55,Math.abs(rfx*cam.tx+rfz*cam.tz)));
   const relYaw=clamp(raw,-.20,.20);
   arr.push({r,p,z:c.z,q,surface,relYaw,facing,sideToCam,view,visualLod});
   apexRivalLodFrameStats.visible++;if(visualLod>=2)apexRivalLodFrameStats.near++;else if(visualLod===1)apexRivalLodFrameStats.mid++;else apexRivalLodFrameStats.far++;
 }
 arr.sort((a,b)=>b.z-a.z);return arr;
}
