 ctx.strokeStyle=v.stripe;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,w*.014);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-ws*.94,-h*.825);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(0,-h*.792,ws*.94,-h*.825);ctx.stroke();
 for(const sg of [-1,1]){ctx.fillStyle=v.carbon;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(sg*ws,-h*.88);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(sg*w*.58,-h*.70,sg*w*.545,-h*.46);ctx.lineTo(sg*w*.505,-h*.30);ctx.lineTo(sg*w*.455,-h*.38);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(sg*w*.505,-h*.64,sg*(ws-w*.018),-h*.90);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();if(lod===2){ctx.strokeStyle=v.stripe;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,w*.009);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(sg*(ws-w*.006),-h*.84);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(sg*w*.55,-h*.61,sg*w*.50,-h*.38);ctx.stroke();}}
 ctx.fillStyle='#111418';ctx.fillRect(shift-w*.018,-h*.86,w*.036,h*.55);if(lod===2){ctx.fillStyle=light;ctx.globalAlpha=.30;ctx.fillRect(shift-w*.007,-h*.84,w*.014,h*.49);ctx.globalAlpha=1;}
 if(Math.abs(yaw)>.045&&lod===2){ctx.globalAlpha=.13;ctx.fillStyle='#fff';const sg=Math.sign(yaw);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(sg*w*.30,-h*.20);ctx.lineTo(shift+sg*w*.16,-h*.64);ctx.lineTo(shift+sg*w*.10,-h*.54);ctx.lineTo(sg*w*.20,-h*.08);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.globalAlpha=1;}
 if(wet&&a.z<180){ctx.fillStyle='rgba(215,230,240,.11)';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,h*.14,w*.57,h*.38,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();}
 ctx.restore();
}
function drawRivalSprite(a){
 // El mundo ya incorpora el roll del peralte del jugador. El rival añade su peralte local.
 const localBank=finite(a&&a.q&&a.q.bank,0);
 ctx.save();
 if(a&&a.p&&Math.abs(localBank)>1e-5){ctx.translate(a.p.x,a.p.y);ctx.rotate(localBank);ctx.translate(-a.p.x,-a.p.y);}
 if(a.view==='front') drawRivalFrontSprite(a);
 else if(a.view==='side') drawRivalSideSprite(a);
 else drawRivalRearSprite(a);
 ctx.restore();
}
function drawRivals(cam,pitch){
 for(const a of collectRivalSprites(cam,pitch)) drawRivalSprite(a);
}

function boardGeometry(board,cam,pitch){
 const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(board.s),env=envProfile(board.s),side=board.side||1;
 const latMag=board.authored&&Number.isFinite(board.authoredLat)?Math.max(env.half+.4,board.authoredLat):(env.half+env.curb+env.runoff+(board.latExtra||1.15));
 // v5.159: el cartel se apoya en el terreno peraltado del lado correcto.
 const G=ribbonWorldPoint(q,latMag,side,0),cx=G.x,cz=G.z,groundY=G.y,halfW=(board.width||2.65)*.5,h=Math.min(board.height||2.85,3.15),postH=1.08;
 const by=groundY+postH,topY=by+h;
 const lx=cx+q.nx*halfW,lz=cz+q.nz*halfW,rx=cx-q.nx*halfW,rz=cz-q.nz*halfW;
 const pts=[{x:lx,y:topY,z:lz},{x:rx,y:topY,z:rz},{x:rx,y:by,z:rz},{x:lx,y:by,z:lz}].map(P=>{const v=worldToCamera(P.x,P.y,P.z,cam);return {v,p:projectLocal(v.x,v.y,v.z,pitch)}});
 if(pts.some(o=>!o.p) || pts.every(o=>o.v.z<7)) return null;
 const poleInset=.34,plx=cx+q.nx*halfW*(1-poleInset),plz=cz+q.nz*halfW*(1-poleInset),prx=cx-q.nx*halfW*(1-poleInset),prz=cz-q.nz*halfW*(1-poleInset);
 const vL0=worldToCamera(plx,groundY,plz,cam),vL1=worldToCamera(plx,by,plz,cam),vR0=worldToCamera(prx,groundY,prz,cam),vR1=worldToCamera(prx,by,prz,cam);
 const poleL0=projectLocal(vL0.x,vL0.y,vL0.z,pitch),poleL1=projectLocal(vL1.x,vL1.y,vL1.z,pitch),poleR0=projectLocal(vR0.x,vR0.y,vR0.z,pitch),poleR1=projectLocal(vR1.x,vR1.y,vR1.z,pitch);
 const z=(pts[0].v.z+pts[1].v.z+pts[2].v.z+pts[3].v.z)*.25;
 const pp=pts.map(o=>o.p),screenW=Math.max(Math.hypot(pp[1].x-pp[0].x,pp[1].y-pp[0].y),Math.hypot(pp[2].x-pp[3].x,pp[2].y-pp[3].y)),screenH=Math.max(Math.hypot(pp[3].x-pp[0].x,pp[3].y-pp[0].y),Math.hypot(pp[2].x-pp[1].x,pp[2].y-pp[1].y));
 const inspectionFull=(typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene()),farAlpha=inspectionFull?1:clamp((470-z)/105,0,1),nearAlpha=clamp((z-2.0)/8,0,1),alpha=farAlpha*nearAlpha;
 return {board,q,z,alpha,screenW,screenH,pts:pp,poles:[poleL0,poleL1,poleR0,poleR1]};
}
const APEX_FIA_2026_CORNER_AUDIT_LOTE1=new Set(['01-australia','02-china','03-japan','04-miami','05-canada']);
const APEX_FIA_2026_CORNER_AUDIT_LOTE2=new Set(['06-monaco','07-barcelona-catalunya','08-austria','09-great-britain','10-belgium']);
const APEX_FIA_2026_CORNER_AUDIT_LOTE3=new Set(['11-hungary','12-netherlands','13-italy','14-spain','15-azerbaijan']);

function autoBrakeBoardDefinitions(){
 if(!trackData)return[];
 if(trackData.autoBrakeBoards)return trackData.autoBrakeBoards;
 const L=trackData.km*1000,markers=(Array.isArray(trackData.cornerMarkers)?trackData.cornerMarkers:[]).slice().filter(c=>c&&Number.isFinite(+c.s)).sort((a,b)=>a.s-b.s),defs=[];
 const integratedTurn=s=>{let k=0;for(let d=-48;d<=48;d+=12)k+=curvatureAt(mod(s+d,L));return k};
 const candidates=[];
 for(const c of markers){
   const s=mod(+c.s,L),safe=cornerSafeSpeed(s);let approach=0;
   for(const d of [70,100,130,160,190])approach=Math.max(approach,cornerSafeSpeed(mod(s-d,L)));
   const delta=approach-safe,fast=(safe>76&&delta<7.5);
   if(fast||delta<5.5)continue;
   candidates.push({s,safe,approach,delta,side:integratedTurn(s)>=0?1:-1,turn:+c.turn||0});
 }
 // Linked corners share one entry series only while there is no meaningful speed recovery.
 const selected=[];
 for(const c of candidates){const p=selected[selected.length-1];if(p){const gap=mod(c.s-p.s,L),mid=cornerSafeSpeed(mod(p.s+gap*.55,L));if(gap<145&&mid<Math.max(p.safe,c.safe)+7){p.covers=(p.covers||[p.turn]).concat(c.turn||[]);p.safe=Math.min(p.safe,c.safe);continue;}}selected.push({...c,covers:[c.turn]});}
 for(const c of selected){const marks=(c.delta>18||c.safe<54)?[200,150,100,50]:[150,100,50];for(const mark of marks)defs.push({s:mod(c.s-mark,L),cornerS:c.s,side:c.side,mark,width:2.7,height:3.0,latExtra:1.15,auto:true,turn:c.turn});}
 return trackData.autoBrakeBoards=defs;
}
function mergedBrakeBoardDefinitions(){
 const profile=circuitWorldProfile(),authored=authoredBrakeBoards()||[],L=trackData.km*1000,out=[];
 // v5.230.16: un SVG exportado con carteles materializados es autoritativo. Si el
 // usuario borra un cartel en Boxy y reimporta, APEX no lo vuelve a inventar.
 if(profile&&profile.brakeBoardsAuthoritative===true){for(const b of authored)out.push({...b,cornerS:Number.isFinite(b.cornerS)?mod(b.cornerS,L):mod(b.s+(Number(b.mark)||0),L)});return out}
 const auto=autoBrakeBoardDefinitions();
 const isRealBrakingBoard=b=>{const cornerS=Number.isFinite(b.cornerS)?b.cornerS:mod(b.s+(Number(b.mark)||0),L);return cornerSafeSpeed(cornerS)<=67;};
 // Los carteles específicos también desaparecen si su curva de referencia es rápida.
 for(const b of authored)if(isRealBrakingBoard(b))out.push({...b,cornerS:mod(b.s+(Number(b.mark)||0),L)});
 for(const b of auto){
   if(!isRealBrakingBoard(b))continue;
   const duplicate=out.some(a=>Number(a.mark)===Number(b.mark)&&Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(a.s,b.s,L))<34);
   if(!duplicate)out.push(b);
 }
 return out;
}
function collectBrakeBoardSprites(cam,pitch){
 const out=[];
 if((player.trackDirDot||1)<.15||!trackData||!player)return out;
 const L=trackData.km*1000,hasGradeCrossing=!!trackData.gradeCrossing,dirSign=player.reverse?-1:1;
 for(const b of mergedBrakeBoardDefinitions()){
   // No renderizamos señalización perteneciente a otra parte de un trazado con cruce a distinto nivel.
   // Esto evita ver carteles del otro brazo del circuito a través del paisaje.
   const ahead=wrapDeltaS(b.s,player.s,L)*dirSign;
   if(!((typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene()))&&hasGradeCrossing&&(ahead<-18||ahead>480))continue;
   const g=boardGeometry(b,cam,pitch),inspectionFull=(typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene());if(!g||g.z<=2||(!inspectionFull&&g.z>=485)||g.alpha<=.015)continue;
   const cx=(g.pts[0].x+g.pts[1].x+g.pts[2].x+g.pts[3].x)*.25,cy=(g.pts[0].y+g.pts[1].y+g.pts[2].y+g.pts[3].y)*.25;
   if(cx<-W*.10||cx>W*1.10||cy<-H*.18||cy>H*1.16)continue;
   if(!inspectionFull&&(g.screenW<4.5||g.screenH<5.5))continue;
   out.push(g);
 }
 out.sort((a,b)=>b.z-a.z);
 return out;
}
function quadMap(tl,tr,br,bl,u,v){
 const top=mixPt(tl,tr,u),bot=mixPt(bl,br,u);
 return mixPt(top,bot,v);
}
function drawQuadPoly(tl,tr,br,bl,pts,fill){
 const mapped=pts.map(p=>{ const q=quadMap(tl,tr,br,bl,p[0],p[1]); return [q.x,q.y]; });
 poly(mapped,fill);
}
const SEVEN_SEG_DIGITS={
 0:['a','b','c','d','e','f'],1:['b','c'],2:['a','b','g','e','d'],3:['a','b','g','c','d'],
 4:['f','g','b','c'],5:['a','f','g','c','d'],6:['a','f','g','e','c','d'],7:['a','b','c'],
 8:['a','b','c','d','e','f','g'],9:['a','b','c','d','f','g']
};
function sevenSegPolys(x,y,w,h,t){
 const l=x,r=x+w,top=y,bot=y+h,mx=x+w*.5,my=y+h*.5;
 const polys={
  a:[[l+t,top],[r-t,top],[r-2*t,top+t],[l+2*t,top+t]],
  b:[[r-t,top+t],[r,top+2*t],[r, my-t],[r-t,my],[r-2*t,my-t],[r-2*t,top+2*t]],
  c:[[r-t,my],[r, my+t],[r, bot-2*t],[r-t,bot-t],[r-2*t,bot-2*t],[r-2*t,my+t]],
  d:[[l+2*t,bot-t],[r-2*t,bot-t],[r-t,bot],[l+t,bot]],
  e:[[l,my+t],[l+t,my],[l+2*t,my+t],[l+2*t,bot-2*t],[l+t,bot-t],[l,bot-2*t]],
  f:[[l,top+2*t],[l+t,top+t],[l+2*t,top+2*t],[l+2*t,my-t],[l+t,my],[l,my-t]],
  g:[[l+2*t,my-t*.5],[r-2*t,my-t*.5],[r-t,my],[r-2*t,my+t*.5],[l+2*t,my+t*.5],[l+t,my]]
 };
 return polys;
}
function drawVectorDigitOnQuad(tl,tr,br,bl,digit,index,count,fill){
 const marginX=.16,marginY=.20,gap=.05,usable=1-marginX*2,cellW=(usable-gap*(count-1))/count;
 const u0=marginX+index*(cellW+gap),u1=u0+cellW,v0=marginY,v1=1-marginY;
 const w=u1-u0,h=v1-v0,t=Math.min(w,h)*.12;
 const polys=sevenSegPolys(u0,v0,w,h,t),segs=SEVEN_SEG_DIGITS[digit]||SEVEN_SEG_DIGITS[8];
 for(const key of segs) drawQuadPoly(tl,tr,br,bl,polys[key],fill);
}
function drawBrakeBoardSprite(item){
 const [tl,tr,br,bl]=item.pts; const [poleL0,poleL1,poleR0,poleR1]=item.poles; const alpha=item.alpha==null?1:item.alpha;
 if(alpha<=.001)return;
 ctx.save();
 ctx.globalAlpha*=alpha;
 if(poleL0&&poleL1){ctx.strokeStyle="#6c727a";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,(tl.scale||1)*.06);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(poleL0.x,poleL0.y);ctx.lineTo(poleL1.x,poleL1.y);ctx.stroke();}
 if(poleR0&&poleR1){ctx.strokeStyle="#6c727a";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,(tr.scale||1)*.06);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(poleR0.x,poleR0.y);ctx.lineTo(poleR1.x,poleR1.y);ctx.stroke();}
 poly([[tl.x,tl.y],[tr.x,tr.y],[br.x,br.y],[bl.x,bl.y]],"#ffffff");
 ctx.strokeStyle="#14181d";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,((tl.scale||1)+(tr.scale||1))*.03);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(tl.x,tl.y);ctx.lineTo(tr.x,tr.y);ctx.lineTo(br.x,br.y);ctx.lineTo(bl.x,bl.y);ctx.closePath();ctx.stroke();
 const rr0=mixPt(tl,bl,.15),rr1=mixPt(tr,br,.15),rr2=mixPt(tr,br,.02),rr3=mixPt(tl,bl,.02); poly([[rr3.x,rr3.y],[rr2.x,rr2.y],[rr1.x,rr1.y],[rr0.x,rr0.y]],"#dc3b4b");
 const shine0=mixPt(tl,bl,.18),shine1=mixPt(tr,br,.18),shine2=mixPt(tr,br,.10),shine3=mixPt(tl,bl,.10);
 poly([[shine0.x,shine0.y],[shine1.x,shine1.y],[shine2.x,shine2.y],[shine3.x,shine3.y]],"rgba(255,255,255,.10)");
 const digits=String(item.board.mark).split('').map(ch=>parseInt(ch,10)).filter(Number.isFinite);
 for(let i=0;i<digits.length;i++) drawVectorDigitOnQuad(tl,tr,br,bl,digits[i],i,digits.length,"#13171c");
 ctx.restore();
}
function drawBrakeBoards(cam,pitch){
 for(const item of collectBrakeBoardSprites(cam,pitch)) drawBrakeBoardSprite(item);
}


function mirrorCarsBehind(maxD=165){
 if(!player)return [];
 const a=carAxes(),list=[];
 for(const r of rivals){
   const w=rivalWorld(r),dx=w.x-player.x,dz=w.z-player.z,forward=dx*a.fx+dz*a.fz,side=dx*a.rx+dz*a.rz,dist=Math.hypot(dx,dz);
   if(forward<-2&&dist<maxD)list.push({r,d:dist,lat:side,w});
 }
 list.sort((a,b)=>a.d-b.d);return list.slice(0,12);
}
function rearTransform(wx,wy,wz,cam){
 const dx=wx-cam.x,dz=wz-cam.z;
 return {x:dx*cam.nx+dz*cam.nz,y:wy-cam.y,z:-(dx*cam.tx+dz*cam.tz)};
}
function mirrorProject(v,x,y,w,h){
 if(v.z<=.45)return null;
 const f=w*.72;
 return {x:x+w*.5+f*v.x/v.z,y:y+h*.54+f*(.98-v.y)/v.z,scale:f/v.z};
}
function clipMirrorPoly(poly3,near=.55){
 if(!poly3||poly3.length<3)return [];
 const out=[];
 for(let i=0;i<poly3.length;i++){
   const A=poly3[i],B=poly3[(i+1)%poly3.length],ain=A.z>=near,bin=B.z>=near;
   if(ain)out.push(A);
   if(ain!==bin){
     const den=B.z-A.z,t=Math.abs(den)<1e-9?0:(near-A.z)/den;
     out.push({x:lerp(A.x,B.x,t),y:lerp(A.y,B.y,t),z:near});
   }
 }
 return out;
}
function drawMirrorPoly(poly3,x,y,w,h,fill){
 const cl=clipMirrorPoly(poly3,.55);if(cl.length<3)return false;
 const pts=[];
 for(const v of cl){const p=mirrorProject(v,x,y,w,h);if(!p)return false;pts.push([p.x,p.y]);}
 ctx.fillStyle=fill;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(pts[0][0],pts[0][1]);for(let i=1;i<pts.length;i++)ctx.lineTo(pts[i][0],pts[i][1]);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();return true;
}
function mirrorCamera(side){
 const tr=player.track||syncPlayerTrack(),a=carAxes(),surface=trackSurfacePoseAt(player.s,player.lat),yaw=side*.09,c=Math.cos(yaw),sn=Math.sin(yaw);
 // Slightly outward but still looking mostly to the rear so the reflected track remains legible.
 const tx=a.fx*c+a.rx*sn,tz=a.fz*c+a.rz*sn;
 const nx=a.rx*c-a.fx*sn,nz=a.rz*c-a.fz*sn;
 return {...tr,tx,tz,nx,nz,x:player.x+a.rx*side*1.56-a.fx*.14,z:player.z+a.rz*side*1.56-a.fz*.14,y:surface.y+.16,bank:surface.bank};
}
function mirrorTrackSection(s,cam){
 const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s),env=envProfile(s),curb=curbInfo(s),half=env.half,outer=half+env.curb+env.runoff;
 const W3=(lat,yOff=0)=>{const P=ribbonWorldPoint(q,Math.abs(lat),lat>=0?1:-1,yOff);return rearTransform(P.x,P.y,P.z,cam)};
 return {s,q,env,curb,z:rearTransform(q.x,q.y,q.z,cam).z,
   l:W3(half),r:W3(-half),lo:W3(half+env.curb,.01),ro:W3(-(half+env.curb),.01),lx:W3(outer),rx:W3(-outer)};
}
// v5.230.62.4.1 · RETROVISORES ADAPTATIVOS + CICLO DE SESION SEGURO.
// La conducción manda: el espejo actualiza su escena con reloj propio y reutiliza la última
// proyección entre frames. Bajo presión baja primero frecuencia/detalle lejano del espejo;
// nunca se modifica la física ni el render principal.
const apexMirrorSceneCache=new Map();
let apexMirrorPerfFrame={requests:0,updates:0,reuses:0,landmarkFaces:0,carsVisible:0,carsOccluded:0,lastHz:30,lastTrackMax:0,lastBuildMs:0};
function apexMirrorPerfSettings(){
 const mobile=mobilePerformanceMode(),raw=mobile?(typeof mobileAdaptiveRenderPressure==='function'?mobileAdaptiveRenderPressure():1):(typeof desktopAdaptiveRenderPressure==='function'?desktopAdaptiveRenderPressure():1),q=clamp(Number.isFinite(raw)?raw:1,0,1),p=mobileControlProfile();
 // q=1 = holgado. Incluso holgado, 30 Hz es suficiente para un espejo pequeño y libera CPU.
 const hz=mobile?Math.round(lerp(12,24,q)):Math.round(lerp(18,28,q));
 const trackMax=Math.round(Math.min(mobile?225:280,Math.max(mobile?82:135,p.mirrorMax*(mobile?(.90+.12*q):(.94+.10*q)))));
 const carMax=Math.round(Math.max(trackMax,Math.min(mobile?235:320,p.mirrorMax*(mobile?(.96+.12*q):(1.02+.14*q)))));
 const carBudgetScale=.62+.38*q,carCount=Math.max(1,Math.min(9,Math.round((p.mirrorCars+(mobile?0:1))*carBudgetScale)));
 const nearStep=lerp(mobile?7.2:5.4,mobile?3.4:2.9,q),midStep=lerp(mobile?11.5:8.8,mobile?5.4:4.6,q),farStep=lerp(mobile?18:14,mobile?8.8:7.2,q);
 const maxLandmarkFaces=Math.max(mobile?24:38,Math.round((mobile?54:84)*(.58+.42*q)));
 return{q,mobile,hz,updateMs:1000/Math.max(1,hz),trackMax,carMax,carCount,nearStep,midStep,farStep,landmarkMax:Math.max(220,Math.min(500,trackMax+160)),maxLandmarkFaces};
}
function apexMirrorPerfStatsSnapshot(){const c=apexMirrorPerfFrame,tot=Math.max(1,c.requests);return{...c,reusePct:Number((100*c.reuses/tot).toFixed(1)),settings:apexMirrorPerfSettings()};}
function apexMirrorProjectedFace(poly3,w,h,fill){
 const cl=clipMirrorPoly(poly3,.55);if(cl.length<3)return null;const poly=[];let z=0,minZ=Infinity,minX=Infinity,maxX=-Infinity,minY=Infinity,maxY=-Infinity;
 for(const v of cl){const p=mirrorProject(v,0,0,w,h);if(!p)return null;poly.push([p.x,p.y]);z+=v.z;minZ=Math.min(minZ,v.z);minX=Math.min(minX,p.x);maxX=Math.max(maxX,p.x);minY=Math.min(minY,p.y);maxY=Math.max(maxY,p.y)}
 if(maxX<0||minX>w||maxY<0||minY>h)return null;return{poly,z:z/cl.length,minZ,fill,minX,maxX,minY,maxY};
}
function apexMirrorLandmarkFaces(cam,w,h,cfg){
 const allowed=new Set(['block','building','pit','grandstand','tower','bridge','gantry','tunnelportal']),faces=[];
 const entries=typeof apexCompiledLandmarkEntries==='function'?apexCompiledLandmarkEntries(trackLandmarks()):trackLandmarks().map((it,i)=>({it,i}));
 for(const ent of entries){const it=ent.it||ent,t=String(it&&it.type||'').toLowerCase();if(!it||!allowed.has(t)||!Array.isArray(it.worldFootprint)||it.worldFootprint.length<3)continue;
  const fp=it.worldFootprint,a=it.anchor||{},wy=Array.isArray(it.worldY)?it.worldY:null;
  let nearest=Infinity;for(const v of fp)nearest=Math.min(nearest,Math.hypot(v[0]-player.x,v[1]-player.z));if(nearest>cfg.landmarkMax)continue;
  const h3=Math.max(.3,Math.min(52,Number(it.height)||8)),bot=[],top=[];
  for(let i=0;i<fp.length;i++){const v=fp[i],gy=wy&&Number.isFinite(wy[i])?wy[i]:(Number.isFinite(a.y)?a.y:apexWorldGroundY(v[0],v[1]));bot.push(rearTransform(v[0],gy,v[1],cam));top.push(rearTransform(v[0],gy+h3,v[1],cam));}
  const col=it.color||(t==='grandstand'?'#747d84':t==='pit'?'#727b82':'#7f8990'),sideCol=shadeColor(col,-10),topCol=shadeColor(col,5);
  for(let i=0;i<fp.length;i++){const k=(i+1)%fp.length,f=apexMirrorProjectedFace([bot[i],bot[k],top[k],top[i]],w,h,sideCol);if(f)faces.push(f)}
  const roof=apexMirrorProjectedFace(top,w,h,topCol);if(roof)faces.push(roof);
 }
 // Conservamos primero caras cercanas, después las ordenamos de lejos a cerca para pintado.
 faces.sort((a,b)=>a.minZ-b.minZ);if(faces.length>cfg.maxLandmarkFaces)faces.length=cfg.maxLandmarkFaces;faces.sort((a,b)=>b.z-a.z);return faces;
}
function apexMirrorCarOccluded(p,z,faces){
 for(const f of faces){if(f.minZ>=z-.35||p.x<f.minX||p.x>f.maxX||p.y<f.minY||p.y>f.maxY)continue;if(apexPointInScreenPoly(p.x,p.y,f.poly))return true;}return false;
}
function apexBuildMirrorSnapshot(side,w,h,cfg){
 const t0=performance.now(),cam=mirrorCamera(side),secs=[],behindSign=(player.trackDirDot||1)>=0?-1:1;
 // Rama longitudinal principal detrás del coche con LOD creciente. Añadimos la rama opuesta
 // sólo a media distancia para conservar cruces/hairpins que realmente aparecen en el espejo.
 const signs=[behindSign,-behindSign];
 for(let si=0;si<signs.length;si++){
  const sign=signs[si],limit=si===0?cfg.trackMax:Math.min(cfg.trackMax,155);let prev=null;
  for(let d=3;d<=limit;){const cur=mirrorTrackSection(player.s+sign*d,cam);if(prev&&(prev.z>.38||cur.z>.38))secs.push({a:prev,b:cur,z:(Math.max(prev.z,.55)+Math.max(cur.z,.55))*.5});prev=cur;d+=d<70?cfg.nearStep:(d<145?cfg.midStep:cfg.farStep)}
 }
 secs.sort((A,B)=>B.z-A.z);
 const landmarkFaces=apexMirrorLandmarkFaces(cam,w,h,cfg),cars=[];
 for(const q of mirrorCarsBehind(cfg.carMax)){
  const v=rearTransform(q.w.x,q.w.y,q.w.z,cam);if(v.z<=.65)continue;const p=mirrorProject(v,0,0,w,h);if(!p)continue;
  if(apexMirrorCarOccluded(p,v.z,landmarkFaces)){apexMirrorPerfFrame.carsOccluded++;continue;}cars.push({q,p,z:v.z});
 }
 cars.sort((A,B)=>B.z-A.z);if(cars.length>cfg.carCount)cars.splice(cfg.carCount);
 apexMirrorPerfFrame.landmarkFaces+=landmarkFaces.length;apexMirrorPerfFrame.carsVisible+=cars.length;apexMirrorPerfFrame.lastBuildMs=performance.now()-t0;
 return{cam,secs,landmarkFaces,cars,s:player.s,at:performance.now(),w,h};
}
function apexMirrorSnapshot(side,w,h){
 const cfg=apexMirrorPerfSettings(),dir=trackData&&trackData.spec?trackData.spec.dir:'',key=`${dir}|${side}|${Math.round(w)}x${Math.round(h)}`,now=performance.now();apexMirrorPerfFrame.requests++;apexMirrorPerfFrame.lastHz=cfg.hz;apexMirrorPerfFrame.lastTrackMax=cfg.trackMax;
 let c=apexMirrorSceneCache.get(key),jump=!c||Math.abs(w-(c.w||0))>.5||Math.abs(h-(c.h||0))>.5||Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(player.s,c.s||player.s,trackData.km*1000))>16;
 if(!c||jump||now-c.at>=cfg.updateMs){c=apexBuildMirrorSnapshot(side,w,h,cfg);apexMirrorSceneCache.set(key,c);apexMirrorPerfFrame.updates++;if(apexMirrorSceneCache.size>8){const k=apexMirrorSceneCache.keys().next().value;apexMirrorSceneCache.delete(k)}}else apexMirrorPerfFrame.reuses++;
 return{snap:c,cfg};
}
function apexDrawMirrorProjectedPoly(poly,x,y,fill){if(!poly||poly.length<3)return;ctx.fillStyle=fill;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x+poly[0][0],y+poly[0][1]);for(let i=1;i<poly.length;i++)ctx.lineTo(x+poly[i][0],y+poly[i][1]);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();}
function drawMirrorSceneAdaptive(x,y,w,h,side,lite=false){
 ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();roundedPath(ctx,x,y,w,h,Math.max(6,w*.078));ctx.clip();
 const pal=palette(),{snap,cfg}=apexMirrorSnapshot(side,w,h),envNow=envProfile(player.s),tv=terrainVisualProfile({s:player.s,env:envNow}),groundCol=surfaceColor(tv&&tv.far?tv.far:'GRASS',false);
 const bg=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,y,0,y+h);bg.addColorStop(0,lite?pal.sky1:'#e8eff4');bg.addColorStop(lite?.48:.24,pal.sky2);bg.addColorStop(lite?.50:.555,shadeColor(groundCol,lite?5:-2));bg.addColorStop(1,shadeColor(groundCol,-12));ctx.fillStyle=bg;ctx.fillRect(x,y,w,h);ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.09)';ctx.fillRect(x,y,w,h*.12);
 let drawn=0;
 for(const seg of snap.secs){const a=seg.a,b=seg.b,seed=((a.q.i/6)|0),road=lite?pal.road:'#3a3e43',[mcA,mcB]=circuitCurbPair(seed);drawn+=drawMirrorPoly([a.lx,a.lo,b.lo,b.lx],x,y,w,h,surfaceColor(a.env.runoffSurface,false))?1:0;drawn+=drawMirrorPoly([a.ro,a.rx,b.rx,b.ro],x,y,w,h,surfaceColor(a.env.runoffSurface,true))?1:0;if(a.curb.left||b.curb.left)drawn+=drawMirrorPoly([a.lo,a.l,b.l,b.lo],x,y,w,h,mcA)?1:0;if(a.curb.right||b.curb.right)drawn+=drawMirrorPoly([a.r,a.ro,b.ro,b.r],x,y,w,h,mcB)?1:0;drawn+=drawMirrorPoly([a.l,a.r,b.r,b.l],x,y,w,h,road)?1:0;if(!lite&&seed%6===0)drawMirrorPoly([mixPt(a.l,a.r,.496),mixPt(a.l,a.r,.504),mixPt(b.l,b.r,.504),mixPt(b.l,b.r,.496)],x,y,w,h,'rgba(250,250,250,.14)');}
 if(!drawn){ctx.fillStyle='#4a4f55';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x+w*.20,y+h*.98);ctx.lineTo(x+w*.38,y+h*.56);ctx.lineTo(x+w*.62,y+h*.56);ctx.lineTo(x+w*.80,y+h*.98);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();}
 // Skyline/boxes/gradas: visibles a distancia y además actúan como oclusores de rivales.
 for(const f of snap.landmarkFaces)apexDrawMirrorProjectedPoly(f.poly,x,y,f.fill);
 ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.09)';ctx.fillRect(x,y+h*.54,w,Math.max(1,h*.012));
 for(const it of snap.cars){const {q,p}=it,team=teamInfo(q.r.team),col=team.color,accent=team.accent||'#fff',px=x+p.x,py=y+p.y,cw=clamp(p.scale*(lite?2.75:3.0),4,w*.34),ch=cw*.42;ctx.fillStyle='#05070b';ctx.fillRect(px-cw*.56,py-ch*.78,cw*.16,ch*.62);ctx.fillRect(px+cw*.40,py-ch*.78,cw*.16,ch*.62);ctx.fillRect(px-cw*.40,py-ch*.96,cw*.80,ch*.10);ctx.fillStyle=col;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(px-cw*.29,py-ch*.78);ctx.lineTo(px-cw*.20,py-ch*.20);ctx.lineTo(px+cw*.20,py-ch*.20);ctx.lineTo(px+cw*.29,py-ch*.78);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();ctx.fillStyle=accent;ctx.fillRect(px-cw*.055,py-ch*.69,cw*.11,ch*.22);const flash=apexWeatherIsWet()?(Math.sin(raceT*25+q.r.id*.47)>-.1):true;ctx.save();ctx.shadowColor='#ff203c';ctx.shadowBlur=flash?cw*.08:0;ctx.fillStyle=flash?'#ff3048':'#681722';ctx.fillRect(px-cw*.04,py-ch*.24,cw*.08,ch*.10);ctx.restore();}
 const gloss=ctx.createLinearGradient(x,y,x+w,y+h);gloss.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,255,255,.20)');gloss.addColorStop(.22,'rgba(255,255,255,.06)');gloss.addColorStop(.52,'rgba(255,255,255,0)');gloss.addColorStop(1,'rgba(26,34,40,.12)');ctx.fillStyle=gloss;ctx.fillRect(x,y,w,h);ctx.restore();
}
function drawMirrorSceneLite(x,y,w,h,side){return drawMirrorSceneAdaptive(x,y,w,h,side,true);}
function drawMirrorScene(x,y,w,h,side){return drawMirrorSceneAdaptive(x,y,w,h,side,mobilePerformanceMode());}

function drawRain(){
 const intensity=apexWeatherRainIntensity();if(intensity<.035||!player)return;
 ctx.save();const storm=apexWeatherSample().state==='STORM',alpha=.08+.24*intensity;ctx.strokeStyle=`rgba(205,225,255,${alpha.toFixed(3)})`;ctx.lineWidth=storm?1.25:1;
 const speed=player.speed||0,wind=apexWeatherSample().wind,slant=5+speed*.085+wind*11,rainCount=Math.max(4,Math.round(mobileControlProfile().rain*(.18+intensity*.95)));
 for(let i=0;i<rainCount;i++){
   const x=(i*173+raceT*(300+420*intensity+120*wind))%(W+100)-50,y=(i*97+raceT*(360+520*intensity))%(H*.86);
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,y);ctx.lineTo(x-slant,y+8+10*intensity+speed*.035);ctx.stroke();
 }
 let g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,H*.40,0,H);g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(220,235,245,0)');g.addColorStop(1,`rgba(220,235,245,${(.025+.13*intensity+Math.min(.08,speed/1000)).toFixed(3)})`);ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(0,H*.40,W,H*.60);
 ctx.restore();
}

function cockpitMotionState(){
 const roll=clamp((player.steer||0)*.006-(player.latG||0)*.0015,-.014,.014),dive=clamp((player.longG||0)*1.5,-4,4),aeroShake=clamp((finite(player.speed,0)-48)/58,0,1)*.55,shake=frameShake+aeroShake;
 const t=finite(raceT,0);
 return {x:(Math.sin(t*31.7)+Math.sin(t*17.3)*.45)*shake*.075,y:dive+(Math.sin(t*37.1)+Math.cos(t*21.4)*.35)*shake*.038,roll:roll-impactFX.roll*.18};
}
function applyCockpitMotion(m){ctx.translate(m.x,m.y);ctx.rotate(m.roll);}
function drawMirrorAssembly(x,y,w,h,side,body,bodyDark){
 const innerX=side<0?x+w*.93:x+w*.07;
 const shoulderX=side<0?W*.300:W*.700;
 const shoulderY=H*.612;
 const braceX=side<0?W*.245:W*.755;
 const braceY=H*.646;
 const mirrorBaseY=y+h*.78;

 ctx.save();ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';

 // main stalk: shorter and more vertical, closer to the reference
 ctx.strokeStyle='#05080c';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(7,w*.055);
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(shoulderX,shoulderY);ctx.lineTo(innerX,mirrorBaseY);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.strokeStyle=bodyDark;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,w*.015);
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(shoulderX+side*w*.006,shoulderY-1);ctx.lineTo(innerX+side*w*.004,mirrorBaseY-1);ctx.stroke();

 // visible triangular aerodynamic bracket
 ctx.fillStyle='#11161b';
 ctx.beginPath();
 ctx.moveTo(shoulderX,shoulderY+H*.006);
 ctx.lineTo(braceX,braceY);
 ctx.lineTo(innerX,mirrorBaseY+h*.025);
 ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
 ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.10)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,w*.007);
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(shoulderX,shoulderY+H*.006);ctx.lineTo(braceX,braceY);ctx.lineTo(innerX,mirrorBaseY+h*.025);ctx.stroke();

 // body-colour accent along the bracket root
 ctx.strokeStyle=body;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.3,w*.010);
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(shoulderX,shoulderY+H*.002);ctx.lineTo(braceX,braceY);ctx.stroke();

 // outer pod shell
 ctx.fillStyle='#05080c';
 ctx.beginPath();roundedPath(ctx,x-5,y-4,w+10,h+8,Math.max(10,w*.105));ctx.fill();

 const shell=ctx.createLinearGradient(x,y,x+w,y+h);
 shell.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,255,255,.09)');
 shell.addColorStop(.25,'rgba(255,255,255,.01)');
 shell.addColorStop(1,'rgba(0,0,0,.18)');
 ctx.fillStyle=shell;
 ctx.beginPath();roundedPath(ctx,x-5,y-4,w+10,h+8,Math.max(10,w*.105));ctx.fill();

 ctx.strokeStyle=body;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.7,w*.014);
 ctx.beginPath();roundedPath(ctx,x-3,y-2,w+6,h+4,Math.max(9,w*.10));ctx.stroke();

 const inset=Math.max(5.5,w*.041);
 drawMirrorScene(x+inset,y+inset,w-inset*2,h-inset*2,side);

 // thinner bezel so the glass looks larger
 ctx.strokeStyle='#14191e';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(4.0,w*.032);
 ctx.beginPath();roundedPath(ctx,x+inset*.40,y+inset*.40,w-inset*.80,h-inset*.80,Math.max(6,w*.080));ctx.stroke();

 ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.12)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,w*.007);
 ctx.beginPath();roundedPath(ctx,x+inset,y+inset,w-inset*2,h-inset*2,Math.max(5,w*.068));ctx.stroke();

 ctx.restore();
}

function drawMirrors(motion=null){
 const compact=compactMode(),mobileLand=mobileLandscapeMode(),shortLand=shortLandscapeMode(),
       body=player&&player.carColor?player.carColor:'#36dff1',
       bodyDark=shadeColor(body,-62);

 const mw=clamp(W*(compact?.124:.133),compact?88:110,compact?158:190),mh=mw*.39;
 const yy=H*(mobileLand?(shortLand?.300:.311):(compact?.328:.318));
 const edge=mobileLand?(shortLand?W*.024:W*.027):W*(compact?.030:.028);
 const lx=edge,rx=W-edge-mw;

 ctx.save();applyCockpitMotion(motion||cockpitMotionState());
 drawMirrorAssembly(lx,yy,mw,mh,-1,body,bodyDark);
 drawMirrorAssembly(rx,yy,mw,mh,1,body,bodyDark);
 ctx.restore();
}

function cockpitCarbonPanel(points,base="#0b0e12",highlight="rgba(255,255,255,.08)"){
 ctx.save();
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(points[0][0],points[0][1]);for(let i=1;i<points.length;i++)ctx.lineTo(points[i][0],points[i][1]);ctx.closePath();ctx.clip();
 const bb=points.reduce((a,p)=>({minX:Math.min(a.minX,p[0]),maxX:Math.max(a.maxX,p[0]),minY:Math.min(a.minY,p[1]),maxY:Math.max(a.maxY,p[1])}),{minX:1e9,maxX:-1e9,minY:1e9,maxY:-1e9});
 const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(bb.minX,bb.minY,bb.maxX,bb.maxY);g.addColorStop(0,"#151a20");g.addColorStop(.45,base);g.addColorStop(1,"#050709");ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(bb.minX,bb.minY,bb.maxX-bb.minX,bb.maxY-bb.minY);
 ctx.globalAlpha=.12;ctx.strokeStyle="#8f9aa5";ctx.lineWidth=1;
 const step=Math.max(9,Math.min(18,(bb.maxX-bb.minX)/26));
 for(let x=bb.minX-(bb.maxY-bb.minY);x<bb.maxX+(bb.maxY-bb.minY);x+=step){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,bb.maxY);ctx.lineTo(x+(bb.maxY-bb.minY),bb.minY);ctx.stroke();}
 ctx.globalAlpha=.07;for(let x=bb.minX;x<bb.maxX+(bb.maxY-bb.minY);x+=step){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,bb.minY);ctx.lineTo(x-(bb.maxY-bb.minY),bb.maxY);ctx.stroke();}
 ctx.globalAlpha=1;ctx.strokeStyle=highlight;ctx.lineWidth=1.2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(points[0][0],points[0][1]);for(let i=1;i<points.length;i++)ctx.lineTo(points[i][0],points[i][1]);ctx.closePath();ctx.stroke();
 ctx.restore();
}

function drawCockpitButton(x,y,r,fill,label,fs=r*.78){
 ctx.save();
 ctx.shadowColor="rgba(0,0,0,.65)";ctx.shadowBlur=r*.35;ctx.fillStyle="#07090c";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r*1.18,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 ctx.shadowBlur=0;const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(x-r*.3,y-r*.35,r*.1,x,y,r);g.addColorStop(0,"#ffffff88");g.addColorStop(.18,fill);g.addColorStop(1,shadeColor(fill,-70));ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 ctx.fillStyle="#f7f9fb";ctx.font=`900 ${Math.max(6,fs)}px system-ui`;ctx.textAlign="center";ctx.textBaseline="middle";ctx.fillText(label,x,y+.4);
 ctx.restore();
}

function drawRotaryDial(x,y,r,label,phase=0){
 ctx.save();
 ctx.fillStyle="#0a0d11";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r*1.15,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 const cols=["#2f7cff","#19c36a","#f3cc32","#f04a4f","#2f7cff","#19c36a"];
 ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,r*.16);ctx.lineCap="butt";
 for(let i=0;i<6;i++){ctx.strokeStyle=cols[(i+phase)%cols.length];ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r*.82,-Math.PI*.82+i*Math.PI*.273,-Math.PI*.82+(i+1)*Math.PI*.273-.03);ctx.stroke();}
 ctx.fillStyle="#242a31";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,r*.52,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 ctx.strokeStyle="#aeb7c1";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,r*.08);ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,y-r*.42);ctx.lineTo(x,y-r*.68);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.fillStyle="#dfe5ea";ctx.font=`800 ${Math.max(6,r*.32)}px system-ui`;ctx.textAlign="center";ctx.textBaseline="top";ctx.fillText(label,x,y+r*1.05);
 ctx.restore();
}

function drawModernSteeringWheel(cx,cy,scale,body,bodyLight){
 ctx.save();ctx.translate(cx,cy);ctx.rotate((player.steer||0)*.22);

 const w=scale,h=scale*.60,screenW=w*.52,screenH=h*.50;

 const wheelPath=()=>{
   ctx.beginPath();
   ctx.moveTo(-w*.27,-h*.46);
   ctx.lineTo(w*.27,-h*.46);
   ctx.lineTo(w*.43,-h*.34);
   ctx.quadraticCurveTo(w*.52,-h*.17,w*.48,h*.22);
   ctx.quadraticCurveTo(w*.46,h*.37,w*.36,h*.41);
   ctx.lineTo(w*.25,h*.30);
   ctx.lineTo(w*.15,h*.24);
   ctx.lineTo(-w*.15,h*.24);
   ctx.lineTo(-w*.25,h*.30);
   ctx.lineTo(-w*.36,h*.41);
   ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-w*.46,h*.37,-w*.48,h*.22);
   ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-w*.52,-h*.17,-w*.43,-h*.34);
   ctx.closePath();
 };

 // rear paddles
 ctx.fillStyle="#20262c";
 ctx.beginPath();roundedPath(ctx,-w*.49,-h*.12,w*.085,h*.38,w*.022);ctx.fill();
 ctx.beginPath();roundedPath(ctx,w*.405,-h*.12,w*.085,h*.38,w*.022);ctx.fill();

 // carbon body
 ctx.fillStyle="#090c10";wheelPath();ctx.fill();

 // clipped carbon texture
 ctx.save();wheelPath();ctx.clip();
 ctx.globalAlpha=.075;ctx.strokeStyle="#aab4bf";ctx.lineWidth=1;
 for(let i=-11;i<=11;i++){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-w*.44+i*w*.045,h*.42);ctx.lineTo(-w*.10+i*w*.045,-h*.54);ctx.stroke();}
 ctx.globalAlpha=.035;
 for(let i=-11;i<=11;i++){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-w*.44+i*w*.045,-h*.55);ctx.lineTo(-w*.08+i*w*.045,h*.40);ctx.stroke();}
 ctx.restore();

 ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.065)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,w*.0055);wheelPath();ctx.stroke();

 // thick grips, more like the reference
 const gripGrad=ctx.createLinearGradient(-w*.50,0,-w*.31,0);
 gripGrad.addColorStop(0,"#08090b");gripGrad.addColorStop(.45,"#262a2f");gripGrad.addColorStop(1,"#111417");
 ctx.fillStyle=gripGrad;

 ctx.beginPath();
 ctx.moveTo(-w*.49,-h*.29);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-w*.53,-h*.12,-w*.49,h*.24);
 ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-w*.46,h*.39,-w*.36,h*.34);
 ctx.lineTo(-w*.31,h*.18);ctx.lineTo(-w*.34,-h*.26);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();

 ctx.beginPath();
 ctx.moveTo(w*.49,-h*.29);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(w*.53,-h*.12,w*.49,h*.24);
 ctx.quadraticCurveTo(w*.46,h*.39,w*.36,h*.34);
 ctx.lineTo(w*.31,h*.18);ctx.lineTo(w*.34,-h*.26);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();

 ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.08)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1,w*.0045);
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-w*.455,-h*.24);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-w*.49,-h*.05,-w*.44,h*.28);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(w*.455,-h*.24);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(w*.49,-h*.05,w*.44,h*.28);ctx.stroke();

 // display housing
 ctx.fillStyle="#11151a";
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.roundRect(-screenW*.56,-screenH*.68,screenW*1.12,screenH*1.14,w*.025);ctx.fill();
 ctx.strokeStyle="#707a84";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.5,w*.006);
 ctx.strokeRect(-screenW*.50,-screenH*.59,screenW,screenH);
 ctx.fillStyle="#070a0d";ctx.fillRect(-screenW*.47,-screenH*.56,screenW*.94,screenH*.93);

 // shift LEDs
 const rpm=engineRPM(),rpmN=clamp((rpm-3500)/8500,0,1),ledN=10,ledY=-screenH*.76;
 for(let i=0;i<ledN;i++){
   const on=i<Math.round(rpmN*ledN);
   ctx.fillStyle=!on?"#252a30":i<3?"#29df63":i<7?"#ff334c":"#3e7bff";
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc((i-(ledN-1)/2)*w*.039,ledY,w*.0105,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 }

 // display
 const spd=Math.round(finite(player.speed,0)*3.6),g=gear();
 ctx.textBaseline="middle";ctx.textAlign="center";ctx.fillStyle="#f3f6f8";
 ctx.font=`950 ${Math.max(18,w*.105)}px system-ui`;ctx.fillText(String(g),0,-screenH*.06);

 ctx.textAlign="left";ctx.font=`900 ${Math.max(8,w*.034)}px system-ui`;
 ctx.fillText(String(spd),-screenW*.42,-screenH*.29);
 ctx.font=`800 ${Math.max(5,w*.018)}px system-ui`;ctx.fillStyle="#b9c3cc";ctx.fillText("KPH",-screenW*.42,-screenH*.15);

 ctx.textAlign="right";ctx.fillStyle="#f3f6f8";ctx.font=`900 ${Math.max(8,w*.032)}px system-ui`;
 ctx.fillText(String(Math.round(rpm)),screenW*.42,-screenH*.29);
 ctx.font=`800 ${Math.max(5,w*.018)}px system-ui`;ctx.fillStyle="#b9c3cc";ctx.fillText("RPM",screenW*.42,-screenH*.15);

 ctx.textAlign="left";ctx.fillStyle="#d9e1e7";ctx.font=`800 ${Math.max(5,w*.018)}px system-ui`;
 ctx.fillText("L"+(player.lap+1),-screenW*.42,screenH*.08);ctx.fillText("P"+player.pos,-screenW*.42,screenH*.25);

 ctx.textAlign="right";ctx.fillStyle=player.drs?"#63ff9c":"#6d7883";
 ctx.fillText(player.drs?"DRS":"DRS —",screenW*.42,screenH*.08);
 ctx.fillStyle="#69f5ff";ctx.fillText("ERS "+Math.round(player.ers)+"%",screenW*.42,screenH*.25);

 const barY=screenH*.40,bw=screenW*.58,bx=-bw*.5;
 ctx.fillStyle="#20262b";ctx.fillRect(bx,barY,bw,h*.018);
 ctx.fillStyle=bodyLight;ctx.fillRect(bx,barY,bw*clamp(player.ers/100,0,1),h*.018);

 // screen screws
 ctx.fillStyle="#9aa2aa";
 for(const sx of [-screenW*.58,screenW*.58])for(const sy of [-screenH*.62,screenH*.42]){
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(sx,sy,w*.0095,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
   ctx.fillStyle="#30363b";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(sx,sy,w*.0038,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
   ctx.fillStyle="#9aa2aa";
 }

 // buttons arranged in dense side clusters
 const r=w*.0325;
 drawCockpitButton(-w*.35,-h*.28,r,"#24d66f","N");
 drawCockpitButton(-w*.26,-h*.23,r,"#f2d329","DRS",r*.48);
 drawCockpitButton(-w*.31,-h*.07,r,"#d81f39","OT");
 drawCockpitButton(-w*.31,h*.13,r,"#2f8cff","BB");

 drawCockpitButton(w*.35,-h*.28,r,"#ff2944","PL");
 drawCockpitButton(w*.26,-h*.23,r,"#2f91ff","+1");
 drawCockpitButton(w*.31,-h*.07,r,"#f1ca27","PC");
 drawCockpitButton(w*.31,h*.13,r,"#f0c82f","BB+",r*.40);

 // thumb dials
 ctx.fillStyle="#20262d";
 for(const x of [-w*.355,w*.355]){
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,h*.00,w*.034,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
   ctx.strokeStyle="#606a74";ctx.lineWidth=1;
   for(let i=0;i<7;i++){
     const a=i*Math.PI*2/7;
     ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x+Math.cos(a)*w*.026,h*.00+Math.sin(a)*w*.026);
     ctx.lineTo(x+Math.cos(a)*w*.035,h*.00+Math.sin(a)*w*.035);ctx.stroke();
   }
 }

 // lower rotary trio
 drawRotaryDial(-w*.21,h*.31,w*.051,"TYRE",0);
 drawRotaryDial(0,h*.31,w*.061,"STRAT",2);
 drawRotaryDial(w*.21,h*.31,w*.051,"ENGINE",1);

 // top accent
 ctx.strokeStyle=body;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2,w*.010);
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-w*.24,-h*.445);ctx.lineTo(w*.24,-h*.445);ctx.stroke();

 ctx.restore();
}


// === v5.163.1 · CLEAN EDITABLE COCKPIT FOUNDATION ===
// We deliberately DO NOT bake the user reference PNG into the cockpit.
// The existing vector cockpit remains the runtime master so geometry,
// colours and instrumentation stay editable and dynamic.
const COCKPIT_EDITABLE_THEME = {
  enabled: true,
  // Visual direction taken from the provided modern black/orange cockpit reference,
  // but expressed as editable runtime parameters rather than a flat picture.
  bodySaturation: 1.00,
  carbonDarken: 0.0,
  haloAccent: 0.90,
  mirrorAccent: 0.82,
  shoulderAccent: 0.92,
  wheelAccent: 0.86
};

function cockpitEditablePalette(){
  const ti=player?teamInfo(player.teamKey||selectedTeamKey()):null;
  const primary=(player&&player.carColor)||(ti&&ti.color)||"#36dff1";
  const accent=(player&&player.carAccent)||(ti&&ti.accent)||shadeColor(primary,34);
  return {
    primary,
    accent,
    dark:shadeColor(primary,-72),
    mid:shadeColor(primary,-34),
    light:shadeColor(primary,42),
    carbon:"#090c10"
  };
}

function drawCockpit(motion=null){
 const compact=compactMode(),cp=cockpitEditablePalette(),body=cp.primary,
       bodyDark=cp.dark,bodyLight=cp.light,bodyMid=cp.mid,
       spec=(player&&player.carSpec)||carCatalog.A26;

 ctx.save();applyCockpitMotion(motion||cockpitMotionState());

 // front tyres
 const tyreY=H*(compact?.557:.546),tyreRX=W*.084,tyreRY=H*.128;
 ctx.fillStyle="#090a0c";
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(W*.198,tyreY,tyreRX,tyreRY,-.08,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(W*.802,tyreY,tyreRX,tyreRY,.08,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();

 // tyre shoulder highlights
 ctx.strokeStyle=body;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2.1,W*.0022);
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(W*.198,tyreY,W*.060,-1.95,-.16);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(W*.802,tyreY,W*.060,Math.PI+.16,Math.PI*1.91);ctx.stroke();

 // OUTER sidewall plane
 const leftOuter=[[0,H],[0,H*.708],[W*.086,H*.610],[W*.245,H*.572],[W*.370,H*.598],[W*.430,H],[0,H]];
 const rightOuter=[[W,H],[W,H*.708],[W*.914,H*.610],[W*.755,H*.572],[W*.630,H*.598],[W*.570,H],[W,H]];
 cockpitCarbonPanel(leftOuter,"#090c10");
 cockpitCarbonPanel(rightOuter,"#090c10");

 // MIDDLE sidewall plane for depth
 const sideGrad=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,H*.60,W*.43,H);
 sideGrad.addColorStop(0,"#1a2026");sideGrad.addColorStop(.38,"#101419");sideGrad.addColorStop(1,"#06080b");

 ctx.fillStyle=sideGrad;
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.095,H*.622);ctx.lineTo(W*.270,H*.580);ctx.lineTo(W*.402,H*.607);ctx.lineTo(W*.432,H);ctx.lineTo(W*.292,H);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.905,H*.622);ctx.lineTo(W*.730,H*.580);ctx.lineTo(W*.598,H*.607);ctx.lineTo(W*.568,H);ctx.lineTo(W*.708,H);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();

 // INNER sidewall plane / padding rail
 const innerGrad=ctx.createLinearGradient(W*.22,H*.64,W*.42,H);
 innerGrad.addColorStop(0,"#242b31");innerGrad.addColorStop(.28,"#151a20");innerGrad.addColorStop(1,"#080b0e");
 ctx.fillStyle=innerGrad;
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.155,H*.650);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.240,H*.612,W*.330,H*.612);ctx.lineTo(W*.390,H);ctx.lineTo(W*.275,H);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.845,H*.650);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.760,H*.612,W*.670,H*.612);ctx.lineTo(W*.610,H);ctx.lineTo(W*.725,H);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();

 // color-painted upper shoulders
 let shG=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,H*.55,W*.40,H*.76);
 shG.addColorStop(0,bodyDark);shG.addColorStop(.32,body);shG.addColorStop(1,"#0a0d11");
 poly([[0,H*.708],[W*.094,H*.613],[W*.270,H*.571],[W*.390,H*.594],[W*.338,H*.676],[W*.105,H*.735]],shG);
 poly([[W,H*.708],[W*.906,H*.613],[W*.730,H*.571],[W*.610,H*.594],[W*.662,H*.676],[W*.895,H*.735]],shG);

 ctx.strokeStyle=bodyLight;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2.2,W*.0021);
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,H*.684);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.176,H*.586,W*.400,H*.588);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W,H*.684);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.824,H*.586,W*.600,H*.588);ctx.stroke();

 // seam/volume line on the side panels
 ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.11)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.1,W*.0012);
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.120,H*.636);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.250,H*.594,W*.377,H*.620);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.880,H*.636);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.750,H*.594,W*.623,H*.620);ctx.stroke();

 // cockpit rim
 ctx.strokeStyle="#050608";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(13.5,W*.0112);ctx.lineCap="round";
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.082,H*.640);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.334,H*.541,W*.50,H*.571);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.666,H*.541,W*.918,H*.640);ctx.stroke();

 ctx.strokeStyle="#333a42";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(3.0,W*.0025);

 // cockpit cavity
 const cavity=ctx.createLinearGradient(W*.5,H*.50,W*.5,H*.96);
 cavity.addColorStop(0,"rgba(10,12,15,.10)");cavity.addColorStop(.24,"rgba(8,10,13,.46)");cavity.addColorStop(1,"rgba(4,5,7,.99)");
 ctx.fillStyle=cavity;
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.150,H*.650);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.305,H*.585,W*.50,H*.612);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.695,H*.585,W*.850,H*.650);ctx.lineTo(W*.775,H);ctx.lineTo(W*.225,H);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();

 // padded interior rails
 for(const s of [-1,1]){
   const x=W*.5+s*W*.171;
   ctx.strokeStyle="#151b20";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(8.2,W*.0060);
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,H*.646);ctx.bezierCurveTo(x+s*W*.012,H*.72,x+s*W*.020,H*.84,x+s*W*.020,H*.985);ctx.stroke();

   ctx.strokeStyle="#3c454d";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.2,W*.0014);
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x-s*W*.004,H*.658);ctx.bezierCurveTo(x+s*W*.009,H*.73,x+s*W*.014,H*.84,x+s*W*.014,H*.98);ctx.stroke();
 }

 // Visible nose / top bodywork
 const noseTop=ctx.createLinearGradient(W*.5,H*.35,W*.5,H*.63);
 noseTop.addColorStop(0,shadeColor(body,10));
 noseTop.addColorStop(.22,"#282f35");
 noseTop.addColorStop(.60,"#12171c");
 noseTop.addColorStop(1,"#06080b");

 ctx.fillStyle=noseTop;
 ctx.beginPath();
 ctx.moveTo(W*.382,H*.610);
 ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.402,H*.535,W*.438,H*.462);
 ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.463,H*.416,W*.486,H*.378);
 ctx.lineTo(W*.514,H*.378);
 ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.537,H*.416,W*.562,H*.462);
 ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.598,H*.535,W*.618,H*.610);
 ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.558,H*.630,W*.500,H*.630);
 ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.442,H*.630,W*.382,H*.610);
 ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();

 // nose accent + seam
 ctx.strokeStyle=bodyLight;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.7,W*.0017);
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.440,H*.466);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.500,H*.420,W*.560,H*.466);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.strokeStyle="#737d86";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.0,W*.0011);
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.425,H*.520);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.500,H*.484,W*.575,H*.520);ctx.stroke();

 // small hardware only; no floating golden nut
 ctx.fillStyle="#b8c1c9";
 for(const [sx,sy] of [[.402,.538],[.438,.520],[.468,.505],[.532,.505],[.562,.520],[.598,.538]]){
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(W*sx,H*sy,W*.0036,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
   ctx.fillStyle="#2c333a";ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(W*sx,H*sy,W*.0014,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
   ctx.fillStyle="#b8c1c9";
 }

 // antenna posts
 ctx.strokeStyle="#1d2329";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(2.9,W*.0029);ctx.lineCap="round";
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.462,H*.504);ctx.lineTo(W*.462,H*.454);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.538,H*.504);ctx.lineTo(W*.538,H*.454);ctx.stroke();

 // Steering bay / cowl
 const bayGrad=ctx.createLinearGradient(W*.5,H*.55,W*.5,H*.74);
 bayGrad.addColorStop(0,"#20262c");bayGrad.addColorStop(.42,"#10151a");bayGrad.addColorStop(1,"#06080b");
 ctx.fillStyle=bayGrad;
 ctx.beginPath();
 ctx.moveTo(W*.322,H*.658);
 ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.348,H*.600,W*.410,H*.572);
 ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.454,H*.555,W*.50,H*.555);
 ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.546,H*.555,W*.590,H*.572);
 ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.652,H*.600,W*.678,H*.658);
 ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.626,H*.682,W*.570,H*.686);
 ctx.lineTo(W*.430,H*.686);
 ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.374,H*.682,W*.322,H*.658);
 ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();

 ctx.strokeStyle="#73808b";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.25,W*.00135);
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.392,H*.582);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.50,H*.548,W*.608,H*.582);ctx.stroke();

 ctx.strokeStyle="#20262d";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(4.7,W*.0041);
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.344,H*.656);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.50,H*.694,W*.656,H*.656);ctx.stroke();

 // recessed side cheeks
 for(const s of [-1,1]){
   const x=W*.5+s*W*.120;
   ctx.fillStyle="#0a0e12";
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(x,H*.644);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x+s*W*.018,H*.616,x+s*W*.028,H*.584);
   ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x+s*W*.040,H*.606,x+s*W*.044,H*.648);
   ctx.quadraticCurveTo(x+s*W*.028,H*.671,x+s*W*.007,H*.678);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
 }

 // narrow connector/bridge, not a protruding nut
 ctx.fillStyle="#11161a";
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.roundRect(W*.430,H*.548,W*.140,H*.028,W*.010);ctx.fill();
 ctx.strokeStyle="#69737d";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(.9,W*.0010);
 ctx.strokeRect(W*.440,H*.554,W*.120,H*.015);

 // hoses
 ctx.strokeStyle="#0e1216";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(4.0,W*.0036);ctx.lineCap="round";
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.426,H*.598);ctx.bezierCurveTo(W*.390,H*.642,W*.386,H*.718,W*.405,H*.800);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.574,H*.598);ctx.bezierCurveTo(W*.610,H*.642,W*.614,H*.718,W*.595,H*.800);ctx.stroke();

 ctx.strokeStyle="#2a3138";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.25,W*.0015);
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.430,H*.598);ctx.bezierCurveTo(W*.398,H*.642,W*.396,H*.718,W*.413,H*.800);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.570,H*.598);ctx.bezierCurveTo(W*.602,H*.642,W*.604,H*.718,W*.587,H*.800);ctx.stroke();

 // central nose/spine under the wheel
 const noseG=ctx.createLinearGradient(W*.5,H*.43,W*.5,H);
 noseG.addColorStop(0,bodyMid);noseG.addColorStop(.12,"#1a2026");noseG.addColorStop(.48,"#0b0e12");noseG.addColorStop(1,"#05070a");
 ctx.fillStyle=noseG;
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.448,H);ctx.lineTo(W*.467,H*.600);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.500,H*.580,W*.533,H*.600);ctx.lineTo(W*.552,H);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();

 ctx.strokeStyle=body;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.5,W*.00165);
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.474,H*.608);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.500,H*.590,W*.526,H*.608);ctx.stroke();

 // wheel
 const wheelW=W*(compact?.322:.344),wheelY=H*(compact?.848:.846);
 drawModernSteeringWheel(W*.5,wheelY,wheelW,body,bodyLight);

 // halo
 const haloLift=spec.halo*H*.004,
       topY=H*(compact?.026:.021)+haloLift,
       joinY=H*(compact?.108:.098)+haloLift,
       baseY=H*(compact?.492:.478)+haloLift;

 ctx.lineCap="round";ctx.lineJoin="round";
 ctx.strokeStyle="#050608";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(20,W*(compact?.018:.020));
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-W*.03,topY);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.23,joinY*.15,W*.44,joinY);
 ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.50,joinY*1.33,W*.56,joinY);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.77,joinY*.15,W*1.03,topY);ctx.stroke();

 ctx.strokeStyle="#171b20";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(7,W*(compact?.0064:.0074));
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-W*.03,topY);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.23,joinY*.15,W*.44,joinY);
 ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.50,joinY*1.33,W*.56,joinY);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.77,joinY*.15,W*1.03,topY);ctx.stroke();

 ctx.strokeStyle="#050608";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(18,W*(compact?.015:.017));
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.5,joinY*.92);ctx.lineTo(W*.5,baseY);ctx.stroke();

 ctx.strokeStyle="#1b2025";ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(6,W*(compact?.0052:.006));
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.5,joinY*.92);ctx.lineTo(W*.5,baseY);ctx.stroke();

 ctx.strokeStyle=body;ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.35,W*.00135);
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(-W*.01,topY-H*.005);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.23,joinY*.10,W*.44,joinY-H*.006);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W*.56,joinY-H*.006);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.77,joinY*.10,W*1.01,topY-H*.005);ctx.stroke();

 ctx.restore();
}

function gear(){
 if(player&&apexControls.gearbox==="manual"&&!demoMode&&!assistMode&&!player.reverse&&!player.gyroNeutral)return player.manualGear||1;
 let k=finite(player&&player.speed,0)*3.6;
 if(player&&player.reverse)return"R";
 if(player&&player.gyroNeutral)return"N";
 if(k<8)return"N";
 return Math.min(8,Math.max(1,Math.floor((k+20)/43)));
}

const cockpitSvgAssetState={key:"",shell:null,tyres:null,tyreOverlay:null,wheel:null,shellReady:false,tyresReady:false,tyreOverlayReady:false,wheelReady:false};

function cockpitSvgSplitShell(svg){
 const tyreStart=svg.indexOf('<!-- Front tyres -->');
 const tyreEnd=svg.indexOf('<!-- mirrors -->',tyreStart);
 const defsEnd=svg.indexOf('</defs>')+7;
 if(tyreStart<0||tyreEnd<0||defsEnd<7)return {shell:svg,tyres:null};
 const prefix=svg.slice(0,defsEnd);
 const tyreFragment=svg.slice(tyreStart,tyreEnd);
 const tyreSvg=prefix+tyreFragment+'</svg>';
 const shellSvg=svg.slice(0,tyreStart)+svg.slice(tyreEnd);
 return {shell:shellSvg,tyres:tyreSvg};
}

function cockpitSvgStripProtection(svg){
 // Remove baked-in halo and old tyre sidewall decoration; both are rendered dynamically.
 return svg
  .replace(/<!-- Halo top -->[\s\S]*?<!-- rear fins \/ antennae -->/, '<!-- rear fins / antennae -->')
  .replace(/<!-- Tyre sidewall detail -->[\s\S]*?<\/g>/, '');
}

function cockpitSvgPrepareTyres(svg){
 // Keep the uploaded tyre/guard geometry, but remove the static yellow arc; branding rotates dynamically.
 return svg.replace(/<path d="M355 389 C339 457, 338 577, 370 675"[\s\S]*?\/>/g,'');
}

function cockpitSvgRecolor(svg,body,accent){
 const light=shadeColor(body,38),dark1=shadeColor(body,-62),dark2=shadeColor(body,-78),nose=shadeColor(body,-28),acc=accent||light;
 return svg
   .split('#ff2f45').join(body).split('#ef283d').join(body).split('#ff3047').join(body)
   .split('#ff6674').join(acc)
   .split('#484c50').join(dark1).split('#23272b').join(dark2).split('#393e42').join(nose);
}

function ensureCockpitSvgAssets(){
 if(!player)return cockpitSvgAssetState;
 const body=player.carColor||'#36dff1',
       ti=teamInfo(player.teamKey||selectedTeamKey()),
       accent=player.carAccent||(ti&&ti.accent)||shadeColor(body,32),
       spec=player.carSpec||carCatalog.A26;
 const key=[body,accent,spec.name||'',spec.nose||'',spec.wheel||0,'layerProtectionC'].join('|');
 if(cockpitSvgAssetState.key===key)return cockpitSvgAssetState;

 cockpitSvgAssetState.key=key;
 cockpitSvgAssetState.shellReady=false;
 cockpitSvgAssetState.tyresReady=false;
 cockpitSvgAssetState.tyreOverlayReady=false;
 cockpitSvgAssetState.wheelReady=false;

 const split=cockpitSvgSplitShell(cockpitSvgShellBase);
 const shell=new Image(),tyres=new Image(),tyreOverlay=new Image(),wheel=new Image();
 shell.onload=()=>cockpitSvgAssetState.shellReady=true;
 tyres.onload=()=>cockpitSvgAssetState.tyresReady=true;
 tyreOverlay.onload=()=>cockpitSvgAssetState.tyreOverlayReady=true;
 wheel.onload=()=>cockpitSvgAssetState.wheelReady=true;

 shell.src='data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,'+encodeURIComponent(cockpitSvgRecolor(cockpitSvgStripProtection(split.shell),body,accent));
 tyres.src='data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,'+encodeURIComponent(cockpitSvgPrepareTyres(cockpitSvgTyreReferenceBase));
 tyreOverlay.src='data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,'+encodeURIComponent(cockpitSvgTyreProtectorBase);
 wheel.src='data:image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8,'+encodeURIComponent(cockpitSvgRecolor(cockpitSvgWheelBase,body,accent));

 cockpitSvgAssetState.shell=shell;
 cockpitSvgAssetState.tyres=tyres;
 cockpitSvgAssetState.tyreOverlay=tyreOverlay;
 cockpitSvgAssetState.wheel=wheel;
 return cockpitSvgAssetState;
}

function cockpitSvgAssetsReady(){
 const a=cockpitSvgAssetState;
 return !!(a&&a.shellReady&&a.tyresReady&&a.tyreOverlayReady&&a.wheelReady);
}
function waitCockpitSvgAssetsReady(timeoutMs=2600){
 ensureCockpitSvgAssets();
 if(cockpitSvgAssetsReady())return Promise.resolve(true);
 return new Promise(resolve=>{
   const t0=performance.now();
   const poll=()=>{
     if(cockpitSvgAssetsReady())return resolve(true);
     if(performance.now()-t0>=timeoutMs)return resolve(false);
     requestAnimationFrame(poll);
   };
   poll();
 });
}

function driverViewCalibration(){
 return {
   eyeHeight:driverEyeHeight(),
   // Immediate ground is intentionally hidden by the nose/cockpit geometry.
   nearGroundOcclusionTarget:4.5,
   horizonRatio:mobileLandscapeMode()?.385:(compactMode()?.405:.425)
 };
}

function cockpitSvgModelParams(){
 const spec=(player&&player.carSpec)||carCatalog.A26,
       compact=compactMode(),mobileLand=mobileLandscapeMode(),shortLand=shortLandscapeMode();

 const eye=driverEyeHeight();
 // 0 = former ~0.835 m camera, 1 = current ~0.70 m driver-eye calibration.
 const lowEye=clamp((.835-eye)/.135,0,1.15);
 const modelSX=spec.nose==='wide'?1.020:1.000;
 const modelSY=spec.halo>0?1.006:(spec.halo<0?.995:1);

 // Keep the approved classic seating position, but give the low eye a slightly stronger cockpit perspective.
 const baseSY=(mobileLand?(shortLand?.865:.885):(compact?.905:.920));
 const shellSX=modelSX*(mobileLand?1.006:1.014)*(1+lowEye*.008);
 const shellSY=modelSY*baseSY*(1+lowEye*.026);
 const baseYOffset=(mobileLand?(shortLand?H*.106:H*.098):(compact?H*.090:H*.096));
 const shellYOffset=baseYOffset+H*lowEye*.010;

 const wheelScale=(1+(spec.wheel||0)*.020)*1.015*(1+lowEye*.012);
 const wheelY=(spec.wheel||0)*3.5+4+lowEye*2.0;

 return {shellSX,shellSY,shellYOffset,wheelScale,wheelY,lowEye};
}

function cockpitProtectionParams(){
 const mobileLand=mobileLandscapeMode(),compact=compactMode(),shortLand=shortLandscapeMode();
 const aspect=W/Math.max(1,H);
 const centerX=W*.5;
 const topBand=clamp(H*(mobileLand?.052:(compact?.048:.044)),20,40);
 const drop=clamp(H*(mobileLand?.030:(compact?.026:.022)),11,24);
 const wingHalf=W*(mobileLand?(shortLand?.350:.362):(compact?.372:.382));
 const throatHalf=W*(mobileLand?(shortLand?.062:.068):(compact?.073:.078));
 const pillarTopW=clamp(W*(mobileLand?.018:(compact?.019:.017)),12,25);
 const pillarBottomW=clamp(W*(mobileLand?.030:(compact?.031:.028)),19,40);
 const pillarTopY=topBand+drop*.18;
 const pillarBottomY=clamp(H*(mobileLand?(shortLand?.360:.376):(compact?.398:.386)),220,430);
 const pillarBelly=clamp((aspect-1.45)*12,-3,5);
 return {centerX,topBand,drop,wingHalf,throatHalf,pillarTopW,pillarBottomW,pillarTopY,pillarBottomY,pillarBelly};
}

function drawResponsiveProtectionOverlay(){
 const p=cockpitProtectionParams();
 ctx.save();
 ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.lineCap='round';

 // Horizontal upper halo bar remains pinned to the very top of the screen.
 const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,p.topBand+p.drop);
 g.addColorStop(0,'#0a0b0c');
 g.addColorStop(.35,'#3a3d41');
 g.addColorStop(.68,'#17191b');
 g.addColorStop(1,'#060708');
 ctx.fillStyle=g;
 ctx.beginPath();
 ctx.moveTo(0,0);
 ctx.lineTo(W,0);
 ctx.lineTo(W,p.topBand*.12);
 ctx.bezierCurveTo(W*.82,p.topBand*.48,p.centerX+p.wingHalf*.38,p.topBand*.74,p.centerX+p.throatHalf,p.topBand+p.drop);
 ctx.quadraticCurveTo(p.centerX+23,p.topBand+p.drop*.24,p.centerX,p.topBand+p.drop*.18);
 ctx.quadraticCurveTo(p.centerX-23,p.topBand+p.drop*.24,p.centerX-p.throatHalf,p.topBand+p.drop);
 ctx.bezierCurveTo(p.centerX-p.wingHalf*.38,p.topBand*.74,W*.18,p.topBand*.48,0,p.topBand*.12);
 ctx.closePath();
 ctx.fill();

 ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.15)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.2,W*.0011);
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(0,p.topBand*.08);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.25,p.topBand*.36,p.centerX-p.throatHalf,p.topBand+p.drop*.92);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(W,p.topBand*.08);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(W*.75,p.topBand*.36,p.centerX+p.throatHalf,p.topBand+p.drop*.92);ctx.stroke();

 // Original SVG centre-pillar silhouette, stretched ONLY vertically.
 // Original coordinates were centred on x=837, from y=31 to y=371 (height 340).
 const originalTop=31,originalBottom=371,originalHeight=originalBottom-originalTop;
 const targetTop=p.topBand+p.drop*.10;
 const targetBottom=p.pillarBottomY;
 const sx=clamp(W/1672,.58,1.35);
 const sy=Math.max(.45,(targetBottom-targetTop)/originalHeight);

 ctx.save();
 ctx.translate(p.centerX,targetTop);
 ctx.scale(sx,sy);
 ctx.translate(-837,-originalTop);

 // gradient defined in original local coordinates so the material stretches with the pillar
 const pg=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,31,0,371);
 pg.addColorStop(0,'#101112');
 pg.addColorStop(.28,'#3c3f42');
 pg.addColorStop(.58,'#25282b');
 pg.addColorStop(1,'#0a0b0c');
 ctx.fillStyle=pg;
 ctx.strokeStyle='#4b4d4f';
 ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(1.2,2/sx);

 ctx.beginPath();
 ctx.moveTo(792,66);
 ctx.bezierCurveTo(808,95,818,135,820,190);
 ctx.lineTo(821,346);
 ctx.bezierCurveTo(821,362,831,371,837,371);
 ctx.bezierCurveTo(843,371,853,362,853,346);
 ctx.lineTo(854,190);
 ctx.bezierCurveTo(856,135,866,95,882,66);
 ctx.bezierCurveTo(865,42,851,31,837,31);
 ctx.bezierCurveTo(823,31,809,42,792,66);
 ctx.closePath();
 ctx.fill();
 ctx.stroke();

 // Preserve the original centre highlight and slight side depth.
 ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.13)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(.9,1.35/sx);
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(837,42);ctx.bezierCurveTo(838,104,837,210,837,356);ctx.stroke();

 ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(0,0,0,.38)';ctx.lineWidth=Math.max(.9,1.25/sx);
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(811,69);ctx.bezierCurveTo(824,126,825,230,826,347);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(863,69);ctx.bezierCurveTo(850,126,849,230,848,347);ctx.stroke();

 ctx.restore();
 ctx.restore();
}

let cockpitTyreSpin=0,cockpitTyreSpinT=null;
function updateCockpitTyreSpin(){
 const t=finite(raceT,0);
 if(cockpitTyreSpinT===null||t<cockpitTyreSpinT||t-cockpitTyreSpinT>.25){cockpitTyreSpinT=t;return cockpitTyreSpin;}
 const dt=clamp(t-cockpitTyreSpinT,0,.055);cockpitTyreSpinT=t;
 const speed=Math.abs(finite(player&&player.speed,0)),dir=(player&&player.reverse)?-1:1,wheelRadius=.36;
 cockpitTyreSpin=mod(cockpitTyreSpin+dir*(speed/wheelRadius)*dt,Math.PI*2);
 return cockpitTyreSpin;
}
function drawPseudoTyreGlyphMark(cx,cy,rx,ry,angle,side,alpha=1,glyphScale=.62,color='#ffd400'){
 const px=cx+Math.cos(angle)*rx,py=cy+Math.sin(angle)*ry;
 const tangent=Math.atan2(ry*Math.cos(angle),-rx*Math.sin(angle));
 const edge=Math.abs(Math.sin(angle)),stretch=.86-edge*.18,squash=.78+edge*.16,skew=(side<0?1:-1)*Math.sin(angle)*.22;
 ctx.save();ctx.translate(px,py);ctx.rotate(tangent);ctx.transform(1,0,skew,1,0,0);ctx.scale((side<0?1:-1)*glyphScale*stretch,glyphScale*squash);ctx.globalAlpha*=alpha;
 ctx.fillStyle=color;
 const gw=9,gh=18,gap=4,start=-((gw+gap)*5-gap)*.5;
 const seg=(x,y,w,h)=>ctx.fillRect(x,y,w,h);
 for(let i=0;i<5;i++){
   const x=start+i*(gw+gap);
   if(i===0){seg(x,-gh*.5,2.8,gh);seg(x+2.2,-gh*.5,5.5,2.6);seg(x+6.3,-gh*.45,2.2,7.0);seg(x+2.2,-2.0,5.2,2.4);}
   else if(i===1){seg(x,-gh*.5,gw,2.5);seg(x,gh*.5-2.5,gw,2.5);ctx.save();ctx.translate(x+gw*.5,0);ctx.rotate(-.52);seg(-1.3,-gh*.46,2.6,gh*.92);ctx.restore();}
   else if(i===2){seg(x,-gh*.5,2.7,gh);seg(x,-gh*.5,gw,2.4);seg(x,-1.2,gw*.78,2.4);seg(x,gh*.5-2.4,gw,2.4);}
   else if(i===3){seg(x,-gh*.5,2.7,gh);seg(x+2,-gh*.5,5.4,2.4);seg(x+6.1,-gh*.44,2.3,6.7);seg(x+2,-2.0,5.2,2.3);ctx.save();ctx.translate(x+5.1,4.0);ctx.rotate(.50);seg(-1.3,-1,2.6,9);ctx.restore();}
   else{seg(x,-gh*.5,gw,2.5);seg(x,gh*.5-2.5,gw,2.5);seg(x,-gh*.5,2.5,gh);seg(x+gw-2.5,-gh*.5,2.5,gh);}
 }
 ctx.restore();
}
function frontTyreAssetLayout(){
 const p=cockpitSvgModelParams(),low=p.lowEye||0;
 // Calibrated against the 1672x941 reference: inner faces at about x396/x1276, y435.
 const dw=1434*(1+low*.004),dh=262*(1+low*.006);
 const dx=(1672-dw)*.5,dy=260-low*1.5;
 return {dx,dy,dw,dh,sx:dw/2098,sy:dh/749};
}
function frontTyrePerspectiveGeometry(){
 const L=frontTyreAssetLayout();
 const faceY=L.dy+501*L.sy,faceRx=60*L.sx,faceRy=132*L.sy;
 return [
  {side:-1,nearCx:L.dx+394*L.sx,cy:faceY+2*L.sy,faceRx,faceRy,rot:-.026},
  {side: 1,nearCx:L.dx+(2098-394)*L.sx,cy:faceY+2*L.sy,faceRx,faceRy,rot:.026}
 ];
}
function drawSvgFrontTyreFallback(t){
 ctx.save();ctx.translate(t.nearCx,t.cy);ctx.rotate(t.rot);
 const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(-t.faceRx*.16,-t.faceRy*.18,t.faceRy*.10,0,0,t.faceRy*1.2);
 g.addColorStop(0,'#35383c');g.addColorStop(.28,'#222529');g.addColorStop(.70,'#111315');g.addColorStop(1,'#050607');
 ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,t.faceRx*1.35,t.faceRy,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 ctx.fillStyle='#080a0c';ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,t.faceRx*.62,t.faceRy*.70,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();
 ctx.restore();
}
function drawSvgFrontTyres3D(){
 const assets=ensureCockpitSvgAssets(),L=frontTyreAssetLayout();
 if(assets.tyresReady){
   ctx.save();ctx.globalAlpha=.998;
   ctx.drawImage(assets.tyres,0,0,2098,749,L.dx,L.dy,L.dw,L.dh);
   ctx.restore();return;
 }
 for(const t of frontTyrePerspectiveGeometry())drawSvgFrontTyreFallback(t);
}
function drawSvgTyreMarkings(){
 const spin=updateCockpitTyreSpin(),speed=Math.abs(finite(player&&player.speed,0));
 const blurAmt=clamp((speed-10)/78,0,1),ghost=blurAmt*.12;
 const brandCol=teamBranding((player&&player.teamKey)||selectedTeamKey()).tyreColor;
 for(const t of frontTyrePerspectiveGeometry()){
   ctx.save();ctx.translate(t.nearCx,t.cy);ctx.rotate(t.rot);
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.ellipse(0,0,t.faceRx*.84,t.faceRy*.84,0,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.clip();
   const dir=t.side<0?1:-1,base=dir*spin-.84;
   for(const off of [0,Math.PI]){
     if(ghost>0){
       drawPseudoTyreGlyphMark(0,0,t.faceRx*.44,t.faceRy*.66,base+off-dir*.16,t.side,ghost,.58,brandCol);
       drawPseudoTyreGlyphMark(0,0,t.faceRx*.44,t.faceRy*.66,base+off+dir*.16,t.side,ghost,.58,brandCol);
     }
     drawPseudoTyreGlyphMark(0,0,t.faceRx*.44,t.faceRy*.66,base+off,t.side,.92,.58,brandCol);
   }
   ctx.restore();
 }
}
function drawSvgTyreProtectorOverlay(){
 const assets=ensureCockpitSvgAssets(),L=frontTyreAssetLayout();
 if(!assets.tyreOverlayReady)return;
 ctx.save();ctx.drawImage(assets.tyreOverlay,0,0,2098,749,L.dx,L.dy,L.dw,L.dh);ctx.restore();
}

function drawSvgMirrorMountRefine(){
 const body=(player&&player.carColor)||'#36dff1',bodyDark=shadeColor(body,-60),edge=shadeColor(body,24);
 const sets=[
   {side:-1,podX:248,podY:376,anchorX:318,anchorY:432,braceX:282,braceY:418},
   {side:1,podX:1424,podY:376,anchorX:1354,anchorY:432,braceX:1390,braceY:418}
 ];
 for(const m of sets){
   ctx.save();ctx.lineCap='round';ctx.lineJoin='round';
   ctx.strokeStyle='#05080c';ctx.lineWidth=10;
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(m.anchorX,m.anchorY);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(m.braceX,m.braceY,m.podX,m.podY);ctx.stroke();
   ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.12)';ctx.lineWidth=2.2;
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(m.anchorX+2*m.side,m.anchorY-2);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(m.braceX,m.braceY-1,m.podX+3*m.side,m.podY-2);ctx.stroke();
   ctx.fillStyle=bodyDark;
   ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(m.anchorX,m.anchorY);ctx.lineTo(m.braceX,m.braceY);ctx.lineTo(m.anchorX-24*m.side,m.anchorY-8);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
   ctx.strokeStyle=edge;ctx.lineWidth=1.8;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(m.anchorX,m.anchorY);ctx.lineTo(m.braceX,m.braceY);ctx.stroke();
   ctx.restore();
 }
}
function drawSvgTyreProtectorRefine(){
 const body=(player&&player.carColor)||'#36dff1',teamKey=(player&&player.teamKey)||selectedTeamKey(),brand=teamBranding(teamKey),accent=(player&&player.carAccent)||teamInfo(teamKey).accent||shadeColor(body,28);
 const edge=brand.guard==='clean'?shadeColor(body,10):brand.guard==='nose'?accent:accent;
 for(const t of frontTyrePerspectiveGeometry()){
   const sg=t.side,guardTopX=t.nearCx+sg*t.faceRx*.64,guardTopY=t.cy-t.faceRy*1.14;
   const outerX=t.nearCx+sg*t.faceRx*1.15,outerY=t.cy-t.faceRy*.38;
   const lowerX=t.nearCx+sg*t.faceRx*.94,lowerY=t.cy+t.faceRy*.58;
   const braceX=t.nearCx+sg*t.faceRx*.79,braceY=t.cy+t.faceRy*.18;
   ctx.save();
   const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(guardTopX,guardTopY,outerX,lowerY);
   g.addColorStop(0,'#2e3338');g.addColorStop(.34,'#111418');g.addColorStop(1,'#06080b');
   ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.beginPath();
   ctx.moveTo(guardTopX,guardTopY);
   ctx.quadraticCurveTo(t.nearCx+sg*t.faceRx*.98,t.cy-t.faceRy*.93,outerX,outerY);
   ctx.lineTo(braceX,t.cy+t.faceRy*.05);
   ctx.quadraticCurveTo(braceX-sg*2,t.cy+t.faceRy*.34,lowerX,lowerY);
   ctx.lineTo(t.nearCx+sg*t.faceRx*.66,t.cy+t.faceRy*.42);
   ctx.quadraticCurveTo(t.nearCx+sg*t.faceRx*.73,t.cy+t.faceRy*.03,guardTopX,guardTopY);
   ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
   ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,255,255,.16)';ctx.lineWidth=1.5;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(guardTopX+sg*2,guardTopY+2);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(t.nearCx+sg*t.faceRx*.98,t.cy-t.faceRy*.90,outerX-2*sg,outerY+3);ctx.stroke();
   if(brand.guard!=='clean'){ctx.strokeStyle=edge;ctx.lineWidth=2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(guardTopX+sg*5,guardTopY+3);ctx.quadraticCurveTo(t.nearCx+sg*t.faceRx*.92,t.cy-t.faceRy*.94,outerX-sg*10,outerY+4);ctx.stroke();}
   ctx.fillStyle='#20252a';ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(t.nearCx+sg*t.faceRx*.72,t.cy-t.faceRy*.01);ctx.lineTo(braceX,t.cy+t.faceRy*.16);ctx.lineTo(lowerX,t.cy+t.faceRy*.56);ctx.lineTo(t.nearCx+sg*t.faceRx*.67,t.cy+t.faceRy*.40);ctx.closePath();ctx.fill();
   ctx.restore();
 }
}
function drawSvgMirrorGlass(){
 const _mirrorT0=performance.now();
 // SVG native coordinates. The housings/supports come from the user's SVG; only the glass is live.
 drawMirrorScene(24,310,171,68,-1);
 drawMirrorScene(1477,310,171,68,1);

 ctx.beginPath();roundedPath(ctx,24,310,171,68,10);ctx.stroke();
 ctx.beginPath();roundedPath(ctx,1477,310,171,68,10);ctx.stroke();

}
