 ctx.restore();
}
function drawSvgWheelOverlay(){
 const spd=Math.round(finite(player.speed,0)*3.6),rpm=engineRPM(),g=gear(),boost=!!(apexControls.ersActive||keys.ers)&&player.ers>0;
 // screen refresh
 ctx.fillStyle='#070a0d';ctx.fillRect(708,520,258,133);
 ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(105,114,125,.52)';ctx.lineWidth=1.2;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(770,522);ctx.lineTo(770,650);ctx.moveTo(870,522);ctx.lineTo(870,650);ctx.moveTo(708,575);ctx.lineTo(966,575);ctx.stroke();
 // rev LEDs
 const rpmN=clamp((rpm-3500)/8500,0,1),ledXs=[712,735,758,781,804,827,850,873,896,919];
 for(let i=0;i<ledXs.length;i++){const on=i<Math.round(rpmN*ledXs.length);ctx.fillStyle=!on?'#263036':i<3?'#00fb68':i<7?'#ff172a':'#2176ff';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(ledXs[i],490,6,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();}
 // screen data
 ctx.textBaseline='alphabetic';ctx.font='700 25px Arial';ctx.textAlign='left';ctx.fillStyle='#f5f5f5';ctx.fillText(String(spd),720,548);
 ctx.font='12px Arial';ctx.fillStyle='#c5cbd0';ctx.fillText('KPH',724,566);
 ctx.font='700 21px Arial';ctx.fillStyle='#f5f5f5';ctx.fillText('L'+(player.lap+1),716,595);
 ctx.font='15px Arial';ctx.fillText('P'+player.pos,716,620);
 ctx.textAlign='right';ctx.font='700 25px Arial';ctx.fillText(String(Math.round(rpm)),954,548);
 ctx.font='12px Arial';ctx.fillStyle='#c5cbd0';ctx.fillText('RPM',950,566);
 ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.font='700 57px Arial';ctx.fillStyle='#f5f5f5';ctx.fillText(String(g),837,594);
 ctx.textAlign='right';ctx.font='700 16px Arial';ctx.fillStyle=player.drs?'#5cff74':'#6f7b82';ctx.fillText(player.drs?'DRS':'DRS —',950,596);
 // ERS bar
 ctx.fillStyle='#253028';ctx.fillRect(777,609,122,12);ctx.fillStyle=boost?'#5cff86':'#39b54a';ctx.fillRect(777,609,122*clamp(player.ers/100,0,1),12);
 ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.font='700 9px Arial';ctx.fillStyle='#e8f0f4';ctx.fillText('ERS '+Math.round(player.ers)+'%',838,619);
 ctx.font='14px Arial';ctx.fillStyle='#dce2e6';ctx.textAlign='left';ctx.fillText('92°C',718,644);ctx.fillText('94°C',777,644);ctx.fillText('90°C',836,644);ctx.fillText('88°C',902,644);
 // buttons/alerts
 svgWheelButton(566,521,20,g==='N'?'#34f23a':'#246b2a','N',g==='N');
 svgWheelButton(619,550,19,player.drs?'#54ff70':'#ffdc00','DRS',player.drs,10);
 svgWheelButton(656,585,18,boost?'#d600ff':'#6e2d78','+10',boost,10);
 svgWheelButton(655,631,19,boost?'#ff2538':'#f10d17','OT',boost,11);
 svgWheelButton(1108,521,20,'#f10d17','PL',false,12);
 svgWheelButton(1055,550,19,'#0a81ff','+1',false,11);
 svgWheelButton(1018,585,18,'#ffc400','PC',false,11);
 svgWheelButton(1019,631,19,'#ffd200','BB+',false,10);
 // extra +/- controls
 for(const [x,y] of [[641,692],[641,775],[1033,692],[1033,775]]){ctx.fillStyle='#555f67';ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,18,0,Math.PI*2);ctx.fill();ctx.strokeStyle='#151719';ctx.lineWidth=4;ctx.stroke();ctx.fillStyle='#fff';ctx.font='700 24px Arial';ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.fillText('+',x,y+8);}
 // rotaries
 drawRotaryDial(729,749,42,'TYRE',0);drawRotaryDial(837,754,50,'STRAT',2);drawRotaryDial(945,749,42,'ENGINE',1);
 // warning line
 ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.font='700 11px Arial';
 if(player.ers<15){ctx.fillStyle='#ffb24a';ctx.fillText('ERS LOW',837,670);}
 else if(player.drs){ctx.fillStyle='#5cff84';ctx.fillText('DRS ACTIVE',837,670);}
}
function drawSvgCockpitLayer(){
 const assets=ensureCockpitSvgAssets(),motion=cockpitMotionState(),p=cockpitSvgModelParams();

 if(!assets.shellReady||!assets.wheelReady){
   frameShake*=.82;return;
 }

 // Pass 1: tyres, tyre marks, chassis and live mirror glass in the SVG coordinate space.
 ctx.save();
 applyCockpitMotion(motion);
 ctx.translate(W*.5,H + p.shellYOffset);
 ctx.scale(p.shellSX,p.shellSY);
 ctx.translate(-W*.5,-H);
 ctx.scale(W/1672,H/941);
 drawSvgFrontTyres3D();
 if(!mobilePerformanceMode()||mobileRenderQuality>=82)drawSvgTyreMarkings();
 drawSvgTyreProtectorOverlay();
 ctx.drawImage(assets.shell,0,0,1672,941);
 drawSvgMirrorMountRefine();
 drawSvgTyreProtectorRefine();
 drawSvgMirrorGlass();
 ctx.restore();

 // Pass 2: responsive halo/protection overlay in screen space.
 ctx.save();
 applyCockpitMotion(motion);
 drawResponsiveProtectionOverlay();
 ctx.restore();

 // Pass 3: steering wheel as its own dynamic assembly.
 ctx.save();
 applyCockpitMotion(motion);
 ctx.translate(W*.5,H + p.shellYOffset);
 ctx.scale(p.shellSX,p.shellSY);
 ctx.translate(-W*.5,-H);
 ctx.scale(W/1672,H/941);
 const pivotX=837,pivotY=682+p.wheelY,ang=(player.steer||0)*.22;
 ctx.translate(pivotX,pivotY);
 ctx.rotate(ang);
 ctx.scale(p.wheelScale,p.wheelScale);
 ctx.translate(-837,-682);
 ctx.drawImage(assets.wheel,0,0,1672,941);
 drawSvgWheelOverlay();
 ctx.restore();

 frameShake*=.82;
}

function drawResponsiveCockpitLayer(){
 drawSvgCockpitLayer();
}

function drawMini(){
 if(!trackData||!player)return;
 updateHudMapLayout();
 const rect=mini.getBoundingClientRect();
 const cw=Math.max(72,Math.round(rect.width)||mini.clientWidth||176),ch=Math.max(52,Math.round(rect.height)||mini.clientHeight||134);
 const mw=Math.max(1,Math.round(cw*DPR)),mh=Math.max(1,Math.round(ch*DPR));
 if(mini.width!==mw||mini.height!==mh){mini.width=mw;mini.height=mh;}
 mctx.setTransform(DPR,0,0,DPR,0,0);mctx.clearRect(0,0,cw,ch);

 const map=apexMapPresentationCache();if(!map)return;const P=map.pts;
 const rangeX=Math.max(1,map.maxX-map.minX),rangeY=Math.max(1,map.maxY-map.minY);
 const sc=Math.min((cw-18)/rangeX,(ch-16)/rangeY);
 const ox=(cw-rangeX*sc)/2-map.minX*sc,oy=(ch-rangeY*sc)/2-map.minY*sc;

 mctx.strokeStyle="#ffffffd8";mctx.lineWidth=2.5;mctx.lineJoin="round";mctx.beginPath();
 P.forEach((p,i)=>{const x=p[0]*sc+ox,y=p[1]*sc+oy;i?mctx.lineTo(x,y):mctx.moveTo(x,y);});
 mctx.closePath();mctx.stroke();

 const pp=apexMapWorldXY(player.x,player.z,map.p),a=carAxes(),pf=apexMapWorldXY(player.x+a.fx,player.z+a.fz,map.p),pr=apexMapWorldXY(player.x+a.rx,player.z+a.rz,map.p),x=pp[0]*sc+ox,y=pp[1]*sc+oy,fx=pf[0]-pp[0],fy=pf[1]-pp[1],rx=pr[0]-pp[0],ry=pr[1]-pp[1],sz=6;
 mctx.fillStyle="#59f3ff";mctx.beginPath();mctx.moveTo(x+fx*sz,y+fy*sz);mctx.lineTo(x-fx*3-rx*3,y-fy*3-ry*3);mctx.lineTo(x-fx*3+rx*3,y-fy*3+ry*3);mctx.closePath();mctx.fill();
}


// ---------- v5.152 · CANVAS2D RENDER BACKEND ----------
// Composición visual aislada del loop de simulación. Este archivo es el backend que
// v5.153 podrá acompañar con WebGL sin modificar física/IA.
function canvas2dRenderFrame(){
 renderFrameId++;
 renderProfileReset();
 if(!trackData||!player){
   ctx.fillStyle="#070a10";ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
   let g=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,W,H);g.addColorStop(0,"#071522");g.addColorStop(1,"#0c0e15");
   ctx.fillStyle=g;ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);renderProfileCommit();return;
 }
 const cam=driverCamera(),pitch=cam.pitch,viewRoll=impactFX.roll+finite(cam.roadRoll,0);
 renderProfileMeasure('environmentMs',()=>drawBackground(cam));
 ctx.save();
 ctx.translate(W*.5,H*.5);ctx.rotate(viewRoll);ctx.translate(-W*.5,-H*.5);
 renderProfileMeasure('environmentMs',()=>drawWorldLandmarks(cam,pitch));
 renderProfileMeasure('environmentMs',()=>drawGradeCrossingEmbankments(cam,pitch));
 drawRoad(cam);
 canvasPushExactWallFences(cam,pitch);
 renderProfileMeasure('environmentMs',()=>drawGradeCrossingOverpass(cam,pitch));
 renderProfileMeasure('effectsMs',()=>{if(!mobilePerformanceMode()||impactParticles.length<18)drawImpactParticles(cam,pitch);drawRain();});
 ctx.restore();
 
 if(apexInspectionActive())renderProfileMeasure('cockpitGrossMs',()=>apexDrawInspectionOverlays(cam,pitch));else renderProfileMeasure('cockpitGrossMs',()=>drawResponsiveCockpitLayer());
 renderProfileCommit();
}
function canvas2dDrawMiniMap(){
 const _miniT0=performance.now();drawMini();
 telemetryPerf.miniMapRenderMs=lerp(telemetryPerf.miniMapRenderMs||0,performance.now()-_miniT0,.14);
}
// ---------- v5.154 · RENDERER CONTRACT + SAFE BACKEND SWITCH ----------
// Hotfix de seguridad: Canvas2D SIEMPRE arranca primero. WebGL2 no se inicializa
// durante la carga del menú ni al poblar circuitos; sólo se prueba al activarlo.
const RENDERER_BACKEND_KEY="apex26.renderer.backend.v1";
let preferredRendererBackend="webgl2";
function preferredRendererBackendId(){
 try{const saved=appStorage.getItem(RENDERER_BACKEND_KEY);if(saved==="webgl2"||saved==="canvas2d")preferredRendererBackend=saved;}catch(e){}
 return preferredRendererBackend;
}
const rendererRegistry={};
let rendererWebglSupport=null; // null = sin probar, true/false = resultado del último intento
function registerRenderer(backend){if(!backend||!backend.id||typeof backend.renderFrame!=="function")throw new Error("Renderer inválido");rendererRegistry[backend.id]=Object.freeze(backend);return rendererRegistry[backend.id];}
registerRenderer({id:"canvas2d",label:"Canvas 2D",renderFrame:()=>canvas2dRenderFrame(),drawMiniMap:()=>canvas2dDrawMiniMap(),available:()=>true});
let activeRenderer=rendererRegistry.canvas2d;
function rendererBackendId(){return activeRenderer.id}
function rendererBackendAvailable(id,{probe=true}={}){
 const r=rendererRegistry[id];if(!r)return false;if(id==="canvas2d")return true;
 if(!probe&&id==="webgl2")return rendererWebglSupport!==false;
 try{const ok=typeof r.available!=="function"||!!r.available();if(id==="webgl2")rendererWebglSupport=ok;return ok}catch(e){if(id==="webgl2")rendererWebglSupport=false;console.warn("Renderer availability check failed",e);return false}
}
function updateRendererBackendUi(){
 const actualId=rendererBackendId(),id=mode==="menu"?preferredRendererBackendId():actualId,sel=$("rendererSelect"),support=$("rendererSupport"),hint=$("rendererHint"),summary=$("rendererSummary");
 document.body.classList.toggle("renderer-webgl",mode!=="menu"&&actualId==="webgl2");
 if(typeof webglCv!=="undefined"&&webglCv)webglCv.classList.toggle("active",mode!=="menu"&&actualId==="webgl2");
 if(sel){sel.value=id;const opt=sel.querySelector('option[value="webgl2"]');if(opt)opt.disabled=mode!=="menu"&&rendererWebglSupport===false;}
 const menuRenderer=$("menuRendererBadge");if(menuRenderer)menuRenderer.textContent=id==="webgl2"?"WebGL2 · 3D":"Canvas 2D";
 if(support){support.textContent=rendererWebglSupport===true?"WEBGL2 DISPONIBLE":rendererWebglSupport===false?"WEBGL2 NO DISPONIBLE":"WEBGL2 · AL ACTIVAR";support.className=rendererWebglSupport===true?"ok":rendererWebglSupport===false?"warn":"";}
 if(summary){const authored=webglUsesAuthoredWorldBackdrop();summary.textContent=id==="webgl2"?(authored?"WebGL2 global · entorno autorado":"WebGL2 global · coches 3D + entorno"):"Canvas 2.5D · estable";}
 if(hint&&mode==="menu"){hint.textContent=id==="webgl2"?"Coches 3D en todos los circuitos. Se activará al entrar en pista; si WebGL2 no está disponible, se usará Canvas 2D.":"Coches dibujados como imágenes. Elige WebGL2 para ver los coches en 3D.";}
 else if(hint){const authored=webglUsesAuthoredWorldBackdrop();hint.textContent=id==="webgl2"?(authored?"El circuito mantiene su perfil de entorno autorado y comparte coches 3D, barreras, landmarks y renderer WebGL universal.":"El renderer WebGL completo se usa en todos los circuitos: coches rivales 3D procedurales, pista/terreno, barreras y landmarks globales."):"Canvas 2D conserva el renderer completo y sirve como referencia/fallback.";}
}
function setRendererBackend(id,opts={}){
 const next=rendererRegistry[id];
 if(!next)return false;
 if(id!=="canvas2d"&&!rendererBackendAvailable(id,{probe:true})){
   activeRenderer=rendererRegistry.canvas2d;updateRendererBackendUi();return false;
 }
 const prev=activeRenderer;
 if(prev&&prev!==next&&typeof prev.deactivate==="function")try{prev.deactivate()}catch(e){console.warn("Renderer deactivate failed",e)}
 if(typeof next.activate==="function"){
   try{if(next.activate()===false){activeRenderer=rendererRegistry.canvas2d;updateRendererBackendUi();return false}}
   catch(e){console.warn("Renderer activation failed",e);if(id==="webgl2")rendererWebglSupport=false;activeRenderer=rendererRegistry.canvas2d;updateRendererBackendUi();return false}
 }
 activeRenderer=next;
 if(opts.persist!==false){preferredRendererBackend=id;try{appStorage.setItem(RENDERER_BACKEND_KEY,id)}catch(e){}}
 updateRendererBackendUi();
 if(opts.notify&&player){const a=$("raceAlert");if(a){a.textContent=id==="webgl2"?"RENDER · WEBGL2 VISUAL":"RENDER · CANVAS2D";a.style.color=id==="webgl2"?"#90d8ff":"#dce7f2";setTimeout(()=>{if(a.textContent.startsWith("RENDER ·"))a.textContent=""},1200)}}
 if(sessionTelemetry){sessionTelemetry.renderer=sessionTelemetry.renderer||{};sessionTelemetry.renderer.backend=rendererBackendId();sessionTelemetry.renderer.changes=sessionTelemetry.renderer.changes||[];sessionTelemetry.renderer.changes.push({t:telemetryRound(raceT||0,3),backend:rendererBackendId()});}
 return true;
}
function cycleRendererBackend(notify=true){const target=rendererBackendId()==="canvas2d"?"webgl2":"canvas2d";if(!setRendererBackend(target,{notify,persist:true})&&target==="webgl2")setRendererBackend("canvas2d",{notify,persist:false});}
function initRendererBackendSelection(){
 // v5.230.36: no se toca WebGL mientras está abierta la portada.
 activeRenderer=rendererRegistry.canvas2d;document.body.classList.remove("renderer-webgl");if(typeof webglCv!=="undefined"&&webglCv)webglCv.classList.remove("active");
 const sel=$("rendererSelect");
 if(sel&&!sel.dataset.rendererBound){
   sel.dataset.rendererBound="1";
   sel.addEventListener("change",e=>{
     if(mode==="menu"){preferredRendererBackend=e.target.value;try{appStorage.setItem(RENDERER_BACKEND_KEY,e.target.value)}catch(_e){};updateRendererBackendUi();return;}
     const wanted=e.target.value;if(!setRendererBackend(wanted,{notify:false,persist:true})){e.target.value=rendererBackendId();updateRendererBackendUi();}
   });
 }
 if(mode==="menu"){updateRendererBackendUi();return;}
 const wanted=preferredRendererBackendId();
 if(!setRendererBackend(wanted,{notify:false,persist:false}))setRendererBackend("canvas2d",{notify:false,persist:false});
 updateRendererBackendUi();
}
// ---------- v5.154 · WEBGL2 VISUAL PASS (SUZUKA) ----------
// Primer salto visual real y deliberadamente acotado: el renderer universal puede mover a GPU
// cielo, horizonte, terreno inmediato, escapatorias, pianos, asfalto y bordes.
// El resto de circuitos conserva el modo híbrido seguro de v5.153.2.
let webglState={gl:null,program:null,buffer:null,aPos:-1,aColor:-1,ready:false,lost:false,error:"",triangles:0,depth:true};
let webglVertexData=new Float32Array(8192),webglFloatCount=0,webglScreenRoll=0;
const webglColorCache=new Map();

function webglCompile(gl,type,src){
 const sh=gl.createShader(type);gl.shaderSource(sh,src);gl.compileShader(sh);
 if(!gl.getShaderParameter(sh,gl.COMPILE_STATUS)){const msg=gl.getShaderInfoLog(sh)||"shader error";gl.deleteShader(sh);throw Error(msg)}
 return sh;
}
function webglInit(){
 if(webglState.ready&&webglState.gl&&!webglState.lost)return true;
 if(typeof webglCv==="undefined"||!webglCv){webglState.error="canvas WebGL ausente";rendererWebglSupport=false;return false}
 try{
   const gl=webglCv.getContext("webgl2",{alpha:false,antialias:true,depth:true,stencil:false,preserveDrawingBuffer:false,powerPreference:"high-performance"});
   if(!gl){webglState.error="WebGL2 no disponible";rendererWebglSupport=false;return false}
   const vs=webglCompile(gl,gl.VERTEX_SHADER,`#version 300 es\nin vec3 aPos;\nin vec4 aColor;\nout vec4 vColor;\nvoid main(){gl_Position=vec4(aPos,1.0);vColor=aColor;}`);
   const fs=webglCompile(gl,gl.FRAGMENT_SHADER,`#version 300 es\nprecision mediump float;\nin vec4 vColor;\nout vec4 outColor;\nvoid main(){outColor=vColor;}`);
   const program=gl.createProgram();gl.attachShader(program,vs);gl.attachShader(program,fs);gl.linkProgram(program);gl.deleteShader(vs);gl.deleteShader(fs);
   if(!gl.getProgramParameter(program,gl.LINK_STATUS))throw Error(gl.getProgramInfoLog(program)||"link error");
   const buffer=gl.createBuffer(),aPos=gl.getAttribLocation(program,"aPos"),aColor=gl.getAttribLocation(program,"aColor");
   webglState={gl,program,buffer,aPos,aColor,ready:true,lost:false,error:"",triangles:0,depth:true};rendererWebglSupport=true;
   gl.enable(gl.DEPTH_TEST);gl.depthFunc(gl.LEQUAL);gl.depthMask(true);gl.clearDepth(1.0);gl.disable(gl.CULL_FACE);gl.enable(gl.BLEND);gl.blendFunc(gl.SRC_ALPHA,gl.ONE_MINUS_SRC_ALPHA);webglResizeViewport();
   if(!webglCv.dataset.lossHook){
     webglCv.dataset.lossHook="1";
     webglCv.addEventListener("webglcontextlost",e=>{e.preventDefault();webglState.lost=true;webglState.ready=false;rendererWebglSupport=false;if(rendererBackendId()==="webgl2")setRendererBackend("canvas2d",{notify:true,persist:false})});
     webglCv.addEventListener("webglcontextrestored",()=>{webglState={gl:null,program:null,buffer:null,aPos:-1,aColor:-1,ready:false,lost:false,error:"",triangles:0,depth:true};rendererWebglSupport=null;updateRendererBackendUi()});
   }
   return true;
 }catch(e){webglState.error=String(e&&e.message||e);webglState.ready=false;rendererWebglSupport=false;console.warn("WebGL2 init failed",e);return false}
}
function webgl2Available(){return webglInit()}
function webglActivate(){const ok=webglInit();if(ok&&webglCv)webglCv.classList.add("active");return ok}
function webglDeactivate(){if(typeof webglCv!=="undefined"&&webglCv)webglCv.classList.remove("active")}
function webglResizeViewport(){const gl=webglState.gl;if(gl&&webglCv)gl.viewport(0,0,webglCv.width,webglCv.height)}
function webglClearCanvasOverlay(){ctx.save();ctx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);ctx.clearRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);ctx.restore();ctx.setTransform(DPR,0,0,DPR,0,0)}
function webglEnsureFloats(n){if(webglFloatCount+n<=webglVertexData.length)return;let size=webglVertexData.length;while(size<webglFloatCount+n)size*=2;const next=new Float32Array(size);next.set(webglVertexData);webglVertexData=next}
function webglCssColor(css){
 css=String(css||"#ffffff").trim().toLowerCase();if(webglColorCache.has(css))return webglColorCache.get(css);
 let r=255,g=255,b=255,a=1,m;if(css[0]==="#"){let h=css.slice(1);if(h.length===3||h.length===4)h=h.split("").map(c=>c+c).join("");if(h.length>=6){r=parseInt(h.slice(0,2),16);g=parseInt(h.slice(2,4),16);b=parseInt(h.slice(4,6),16);if(h.length>=8)a=parseInt(h.slice(6,8),16)/255}}
 else if((m=css.match(/^rgba?\(([^)]+)\)$/))){const p=m[1].split(",").map(v=>v.trim());r=parseFloat(p[0]);g=parseFloat(p[1]);b=parseFloat(p[2]);if(p.length>3)a=parseFloat(p[3])}
 const out=[clamp(r/255,0,1),clamp(g/255,0,1),clamp(b/255,0,1),clamp(Number.isFinite(a)?a:1,0,1)];webglColorCache.set(css,out);return out;
}
const WEBGL_DEPTH_NEAR=.45,WEBGL_DEPTH_FAR=2200,WEBGL_BG_NDC=1;
function webglDepthNdc(camZ){
 const n=WEBGL_DEPTH_NEAR,f=apexInspectionActive()?apexInspectionFarDistance():WEBGL_DEPTH_FAR,z=clamp(finite(camZ,f),n,f);
 return (f+n)/(f-n)-2*f*n/((f-n)*z);
}
function webglVertex(x,y,z,c){webglEnsureFloats(7);const i=webglFloatCount;webglVertexData[i]=x;webglVertexData[i+1]=y;webglVertexData[i+2]=z;webglVertexData[i+3]=c[0];webglVertexData[i+4]=c[1];webglVertexData[i+5]=c[2];webglVertexData[i+6]=c[3];webglFloatCount+=7}
function webglVertexPx(x,y,c,camZ=null){
 if(webglScreenRoll){const cx=W*.5,cy=H*.5,dx=x-cx,dy=y-cy,cs=Math.cos(webglScreenRoll),sn=Math.sin(webglScreenRoll);x=cx+dx*cs-dy*sn;y=cy+dx*sn+dy*cs}
 webglVertex(x/W*2-1,1-y/H*2,camZ==null?WEBGL_BG_NDC:webglDepthNdc(camZ),c);
}
function webglGradientRect(y0,y1,cTop,cBottom){const bg=apexInspectionActive()?1:WEBGL_BG_NDC;const a=webglCssColor(cTop),b=webglCssColor(cBottom),ny0=1-y0/H*2,ny1=1-y1/H*2;webglVertex(-1,ny0,bg,a);webglVertex(1,ny0,bg,a);webglVertex(1,ny1,bg,b);webglVertex(-1,ny0,bg,a);webglVertex(1,ny1,bg,b);webglVertex(-1,ny1,bg,b)}
function webglScreenTri(a,b,c,color,camZ=null){const col=webglCssColor(color);webglVertexPx(a[0],a[1],col,camZ);webglVertexPx(b[0],b[1],col,camZ);webglVertexPx(c[0],c[1],col,camZ)}
function webglScreenQuad(a,b,c,d,color,camZ=null){webglScreenTri(a,b,c,color,camZ);webglScreenTri(a,c,d,color,camZ)}
function webglPushCamPoly(poly3,pitch,color,near=.58,depthBias=0){
 const clipped=clipPolyNear(poly3,near);if(clipped.length<3)return 0;const pp=projectCameraPoly(clipped,pitch);if(!pp||pp.length<3)return 0;
 const col=webglCssColor(color);let tris=0;for(let i=1;i<pp.length-1;i++){
   webglVertexPx(pp[0][0],pp[0][1],col,clipped[0].z+depthBias);
   webglVertexPx(pp[i][0],pp[i][1],col,clipped[i].z+depthBias);
   webglVertexPx(pp[i+1][0],pp[i+1][1],col,clipped[i+1].z+depthBias);tris++
 }return tris;
}
function webglPushCamQuad(a,b,c,d,pitch,color,near=.58,depthBias=0){return webglPushCamPoly([a,b,c,d],pitch,color,near,depthBias)}

function webglTrackPointCamera(s,lat,cam,yOff=.014){
 const q=renderRibbonFrameAt(s),p=ribbonWorldPoint(q,Math.abs(lat),lat>=0?1:-1,yOff);
 return worldToCamera(p.x,p.y,p.z,cam);
}
function webglFillTrackQuad(points,cam,pitch,color,yOff=.014,depthBias=-.020){
 if(!points||points.length!==4)return 0;
 const v=points.map(([s,lat])=>webglTrackPointCamera(s,lat,cam,yOff));
 return webglPushCamQuad(v[0],v[1],v[2],v[3],pitch,color,.58,depthBias);
}

// v5.164 · Pit lane, grid boxes and start/finish now share the WebGL depth buffer.
function webglPushStartFinish(cam,pitch,baseS=null){
 if(!trackData)return;apexDrawSectorSurface({s:Number.isFinite(baseS)?baseS:apexCircuitStartFinishS(trackData.spec).finish},cam,pitch,true);
}
function webglPushRaceStartLine(cam,pitch,baseS=null){
 if(!trackData)return;
 const L=trackData.km*1000,sf=apexCircuitStartFinishS(trackData.spec),sBase=mod(Number.isFinite(baseS)?baseS:sf.start,L),env=envProfile(sBase),half=env.half-.06,th=.18;
 webglFillTrackQuad([[sBase-th*.5,half],[sBase-th*.5,-half],[sBase+th*.5,-half],[sBase+th*.5,half]],cam,pitch,'#f4f5f6',.020,-.034);
}
function webglPushGridOnSegment(a,b,cam,pitch,baseS=null){
 if(!trackData)return;
 const L=trackData.km*1000;
 baseS=mod(Number.isFinite(baseS)?baseS:apexCircuitStartFinishS(trackData.spec).start,L);
 const midS=a.s+wrapDeltaS(b.s,a.s,L)*.5,rel=wrapDeltaS(midS,baseS,L);
 if(rel>8||rel<-118)return;
 const line="#f3f4f5",boxHalfL=1.42,boxHalfW=.78,sideTh=.085,crossTh=.09;
 for(let slot=1;slot<=22;slot++){
   const off=-8-(slot-1)*4.9,center=mod(baseS+off,L),lane=(slot%2)?-1.95:1.95;
   const da=wrapDeltaS(a.s,center,L),db=wrapDeltaS(b.s,center,L),dm=(da+db)*.5;
   if(Math.abs(dm)<=boxHalfL+1.35){
     const seg0=center+clamp(Math.min(da,db),-boxHalfL,boxHalfL);
     const seg1=center+clamp(Math.max(da,db),-boxHalfL,boxHalfL);
     if(seg1-seg0>.06){
       for(const side of [-1,1]){
         const lc=lane+side*boxHalfW;
         webglFillTrackQuad(
           [[seg0,lc-sideTh*.5],[seg0,lc+sideTh*.5],[seg1,lc+sideTh*.5],[seg1,lc-sideTh*.5]],
           cam,pitch,line,.020,-.030
         );
       }
     }
   }
   const front=center+boxHalfL,xa=da-boxHalfL,xb=db-boxHalfL;
   if((xa===0)||(xb===0)||(xa*xb<0&&Math.abs(da-db)<6.5)){
     webglFillTrackQuad(
       [[front-crossTh*.5,lane-boxHalfW],[front-crossTh*.5,lane+boxHalfW],[front+crossTh*.5,lane+boxHalfW],[front+crossTh*.5,lane-boxHalfW]],
       cam,pitch,line,.020,-.030
     );
   }
 }
}
function webglPushPitLaneOnSegment(a,b,cam,pitch){
 if(!trackData)return;
 if(trackData.customWorld&&trackData.customWorld.pitNetwork&&trackData.customWorld.pitNetwork.runs&&trackData.customWorld.pitNetwork.runs.length)return;
 const pa=pitLaneAtS(a.s),pb=pitLaneAtS(b.s);if(!pa||!pb)return;
 const wA=pa.width*.5,wB=pb.width*.5;
 const aOuter=webglTrackPointCamera(a.s,pa.centerLat+pa.side*wA,cam,.014),
       aInner=webglTrackPointCamera(a.s,pa.centerLat-pa.side*wA,cam,.014),
       bOuter=webglTrackPointCamera(b.s,pb.centerLat+pb.side*wB,cam,.014),
       bInner=webglTrackPointCamera(b.s,pb.centerLat-pb.side*wB,cam,.014);
 webglPushCamQuad(aInner,aOuter,bOuter,bInner,pitch,'#555b61',.58,-.022);

 const edge=.055;
 const ai2=webglTrackPointCamera(a.s,pa.centerLat-pa.side*(wA-edge),cam,.020),
       bi2=webglTrackPointCamera(b.s,pb.centerLat-pb.side*(wB-edge),cam,.020),
       ao2=webglTrackPointCamera(a.s,pa.centerLat+pa.side*(wA-edge),cam,.020),
       bo2=webglTrackPointCamera(b.s,pb.centerLat+pb.side*(wB-edge),cam,.020);
 webglPushCamQuad(aInner,ai2,bi2,bInner,pitch,'rgba(248,249,250,.92)',.58,-.034);
 webglPushCamQuad(ao2,aOuter,bOuter,bo2,pitch,'rgba(248,249,250,.78)',.58,-.034);

 const mid=(pa.rel+pb.rel)*.5,pit=pa.pit,centralA=pit.entry+95,centralB=pit.exit-65;
 if(mid>centralA&&mid<centralB){
   const boxEvery=17.5,n0=Math.floor((pa.rel-centralA)/boxEvery),n1=Math.floor((pb.rel-centralA)/boxEvery);
   if(n0!==n1||Math.abs(pb.rel-pa.rel)>boxEvery*.75){
     const smid=(a.s+b.s)*.5,pc=pitLaneAtS(smid);
     if(pc){
       const outer=pc.centerLat+pc.side*(pc.width*.5-.35),inner=outer-pc.side*1.65;
       webglFillTrackQuad([[smid-.55,inner],[smid-.55,outer],[smid+.55,outer],[smid+.55,inner]],cam,pitch,'rgba(235,238,240,.82)',.021,-.035);
     }
   }
 }
 for(const mark of [pit.entry,pit.exit]){
   const da=pa.rel-mark,db=pb.rel-mark;
   if(da===0||db===0||da*db<0){
     const ss=mod(mark,trackData.km*1000),pp=pitLaneAtS(ss);if(!pp)continue;
     const l0=pp.centerLat-pp.side*pp.width*.5,l1=pp.centerLat+pp.side*pp.width*.5;
     webglFillTrackQuad([[ss-.11,l0],[ss-.11,l1],[ss+.11,l1],[ss+.11,l0]],cam,pitch,'#f7f8f9',.022,-.038);
   }
 }
}

function webglPushExactPitLane(cam,pitch){
 for(const it of collectExactPitRenderItems(cam)){const d=it.data,A=d.A0,B=d.A1,C=d.B1,D=d.B0;webglPushCamQuad(A,B,C,D,pitch,'#555b61',.58,-.022);const e0=camMix(A,B,.018),e1=camMix(D,C,.018),f0=camMix(B,A,.018),f1=camMix(C,D,.018);webglPushCamQuad(A,e0,e1,D,pitch,'rgba(248,249,250,.92)',.58,-.034);webglPushCamQuad(f0,B,C,f1,pitch,'rgba(248,249,250,.82)',.58,-.034)}
}

function webglPushSurfaceMarkings(segments,cam,pitch){
 const mp=mobileControlProfile(),L=trackData.km*1000,sf=apexCircuitStartFinishS(trackData.spec),gridBase=sf.start,finishBase=sf.finish;
 webglPushExactPitLane(cam,pitch);
 let finishDone=false,startLineDone=false;const sectorDone=new Set();
 for(const seg of segments){
   const a=seg.a,b=seg.b;
   if(seg.z<mp.pitMax)webglPushPitLaneOnSegment(a,b,cam,pitch);
   if(seg.z<=3)continue;
   const relMid=wrapDeltaS((a.s+b.s)*.5,gridBase,L);
   if(relMid<10&&relMid>-125)webglPushGridOnSegment(a,b,cam,pitch,gridBase);
   for(const c of trackData.sectorControls||[])if(!sectorDone.has(c.s)&&apexSegmentContainsTiming(a,b,c.s,L)){apexDrawSectorSurface(c,cam,pitch,true);sectorDone.add(c.s);}
   if(!finishDone){
     const da=wrapDeltaS(a.s,finishBase,L),db=wrapDeltaS(b.s,finishBase,L);
     if((da===0)||(db===0)||(da*db<0&&Math.abs(da-db)<8)){webglPushStartFinish(cam,pitch,finishBase);finishDone=true;}
   }
   if(!startLineDone&&Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(gridBase,finishBase,L))>.5){
     const da=wrapDeltaS(a.s,gridBase,L),db=wrapDeltaS(b.s,gridBase,L);
     if((da===0)||(db===0)||(da*db<0&&Math.abs(da-db)<8)){webglPushRaceStartLine(cam,pitch,gridBase);startLineDone=true;}
   }
 }
}

function webglPushScreenLineDepth(a,b,width,color,camZ){
 if(!a||!b)return;const dx=b[0]-a[0],dy=b[1]-a[1],len=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1,nx=-dy/len*width*.5,ny=dx/len*width*.5;
 webglScreenQuad([a[0]+nx,a[1]+ny],[b[0]+nx,b[1]+ny],[b[0]-nx,b[1]-ny],[a[0]-nx,a[1]-ny],color,camZ);
}
function webglGroundedY(anchor,along=0,localLat=0){
 if(!trackData||!Number.isFinite(anchor.s))return anchor.y;
 const q0=anchor.groundQ||anchor.q||poseAt(anchor.s||0),dx=anchor.x-q0.x,dz=anchor.z-q0.z,baseLat=dx*q0.nx+dz*q0.nz;
 const L=trackData.km*1000,ss=mod(anchor.s+along,L),q=renderRibbonFrameAt(ss),absLat=baseLat+localLat;
 const st={s:ss,q,env:envProfile(ss)},edge=terrainOuterWidth(st),spread=terrainSpread(st),baseGround=terrainGroundY(st),wx=q.x+q.nx*absLat,wz=q.z+q.nz*absLat,ground=apexTerrainHeightAtWorld(wx,wz,baseGround),bankY=Math.tan(Number.isFinite(q.bank)?q.bank:0)*absLat;
 const t=terrainEase(clamp((Math.abs(absLat)-edge)/Math.max(8,spread.far*.72),0,1));return lerp(q.y+bankY,ground,t);
}
function webglLandmarkCam(anchor,along,lat,y,cam){
 const q=anchor.q||poseAt(anchor.s||0),wx=anchor.x+q.tx*along+q.nx*lat,wz=anchor.z+q.tz*along+q.nz*lat,wy=webglGroundedY(anchor,along,lat)+y;
 return worldToCamera(wx,wy,wz,cam);
}
function webglSectorIndexAtS(s){
 if(!trackData)return 0;const L=trackData.km*1000,f=mod(s,L)/L;
 if(f<.115||f>.955)return 0;if(f<.285)return 1;if(f<.355)return 2;if(f<.515)return 3;if(f<.755)return 4;if(f<.875)return 5;return 6;
}
function webglSectorGap(a,b){const d=Math.abs(a-b);return Math.min(d,7-d)}
function webglCamPointVisible(c,{near=2.5,far=1200,radius=0,margin=1.10,minPx=0,pitch=0}={}){
 if(!c||!Number.isFinite(c.z)||c.z<near||c.z>far)return false;
 const halfW=c.z*Math.tan(sceneHFov()*.5)*margin+Math.max(0,radius);
 if(Math.abs(c.x)>halfW)return false;
 const p=projectRoadLocal(c.x,c.y,c.z,pitch);if(!p||p.x<-W*.20||p.x>W*1.20||p.y<-H*.45||p.y>H*1.35)return false;
 if(minPx>0&&radius>0){const f=(W*.5)/Math.tan(sceneHFov()*.5),px=f*radius/Math.max(c.z,.5);if(px<minPx)return false;}
 return true;
}

// v5.171 · Global LOD manager for the current WebGL circuit world.
// 0 = full, 1 = medium, 2 = coarse, 3 = culled.
const WEBGL_CIRCUIT_LOD_PROFILES={
 // v5.173: gradas, noria y cruce conservan detalle con culling estable y mayor persistencia.
 terrain:[460,900,1550],barrier:[190,450,800],grandstand:[250,620,1200],pit:[360,760,1260],block:[330,700,1150],gantry:[210,470,730],ferris:[760,1500,2400],tunnel:[430,820,1250],overpass:[390,760,1180],backdrop:[480,1120,1950],default:[240,560,1020]
};
const webglCircuitLodCache=new Map();
function webglCircuitProjectedPx(z,radius){const f=(W*.5)/Math.tan(sceneHFov()*.5);return f*Math.max(.1,radius||1)/Math.max(.5,z||1)}
function webglCircuitLod(kind,z,radius=10,key=null){
 const p=WEBGL_CIRCUIT_LOD_PROFILES[kind]||WEBGL_CIRCUIT_LOD_PROFILES.default,mobile=mobilePerformanceMode(),scale=mobile?.78:1;
 const t0=p[0]*scale,t1=p[1]*scale,t2=p[2]*scale,px=webglCircuitProjectedPx(z,radius);
 let raw=(z<t0&&px>10)?0:((z<t1&&px>4.0)?1:((z<t2&&px>1.25)?2:3));
 if(key!=null){
   const old=webglCircuitLodCache.get(key);
   if(Number.isFinite(old)&&old!==raw){
     // Hysteresis: avoid flicker/popping when hovering around a distance boundary.
     if(raw>old){const b=[t0,t1,t2][Math.min(old,2)]||t2;if(z<b*1.10)raw=old;}
     else {const b=[t0,t1,t2][Math.min(raw,2)]||t2;if(z>b*.88)raw=old;}
   }
   webglCircuitLodCache.set(key,raw);
 }
 return raw;
}
function webglLandmarkRadius(it){
 if(!it)return 12;if(it.type==='barrier')return Math.max(10,(it.width||1)*3);if(it.type==='surfaceZone'||it.type==='terrainZone'||it.type==='water'||it.type==='marking'||it.type==='forestZone')return 24;if(it.type==='grandstand')return Math.min(54,(it.length||60)*.48);if(it.type==='pit')return Math.min(62,(it.length||100)*.46);if(it.type==='block')return Math.max(8,(it.width||20)*.52);if(it.type==='gantry')return (it.span||19)*.55;if(it.type==='ferris')return (it.radius||22)*1.15;if(it.type==='tree')return Math.max(10,(it.width||5)*1.6);if(it.type==='tower')return Math.max(12,(it.width||7)*1.6);if(it.type==='billboard'||it.type==='advertising')return Math.max(9,(it.width||14)*.62);if(it.type==='tunnelPortal')return Math.max(10,(it.span||18)*.62);return 12;
}
function webglLandmarkVisible(it,base,pitch,cam){
 if(!it||!base||!player||!trackData)return false;
 const L=trackData.km*1000,a=it.anchor||{},ds=Number.isFinite(a.s)?Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(a.s,player.s,L)):0,skyline=apexIsSkylineLandmark(it);

 let far=1020,radius=16,minPx=2,maxDs=1020,margin=1.18;
 if(it.type==="grandstand"){far=1650;radius=Math.min(72,(it.length||60)*.62);minPx=.35;maxDs=it.maxDs||1500;margin=1.98}
 else if(it.type==="pit"){far=1500;radius=Math.min(84,(it.length||100)*.56);minPx=.30;maxDs=it.maxDs||1450;margin=1.82}
 else if(it.type==="block"){far=it.proposalId?4200:3600;radius=Math.max(11,(it.width||20)*.64);minPx=it.proposalId?.12:.24;maxDs=(it.maxDs||(it.proposalId?1250:1030))*3.20;margin=1.90}
 else if(it.type==="gantry"){far=760;radius=(it.span||19)*.68;minPx=2.4;maxDs=740;margin=1.30}
 else if(it.type==="ferris"){far=2320;radius=(it.radius||25)*1.58;minPx=.55;maxDs=it.maxDs||2480;margin=1.70}
 else if(it.type==="tree"){far=1180;radius=Math.max(12,(it.width||5)*2.0);minPx=.8;maxDs=1100;margin=1.34}
 else if(it.type==="tower"){far=it.farGeo?9000:1500;radius=Math.max(14,(it.width||7)*2.0);minPx=it.farGeo?.025:.9;maxDs=it.farGeo?1e9:1450;margin=it.farGeo?2.15:1.45}
 else if(it.type==="billboard"||it.type==="advertising"){far=980;radius=Math.max(10,(it.width||14)*.75);minPx=1.2;maxDs=it.maxDs||930;margin=1.30}
 else if(it.type==="tunnelPortal"){far=900;radius=Math.max(12,(it.span||18)*.72);minPx=1.5;maxDs=850;margin=1.28}
 else if(it.type==="flagPole"){far=1700;radius=24;minPx=.45;maxDs=1600;margin=1.30}
 if(Number.isFinite(a.s)&&ds>maxDs&&!skyline)return false;
 if(it.type==="grandstand"&&cam){
   const len=Math.min(it.length||60,118),h=Math.min(it.height||12,16),samples=[
     base,
     webglLandmarkCam(a,-len*.56,0,h*.40,cam),webglLandmarkCam(a,len*.56,0,h*.40,cam),
     webglLandmarkCam(a,-len*.30,0,h*.82,cam),webglLandmarkCam(a,len*.30,0,h*.82,cam)
   ];
   // Close grandstands are never dropped just because their centre crossed a frustum edge.
   // Near-plane clipping handles the pieces that are actually behind the camera.
   if(ds<250)return samples.some(c=>c&&Number.isFinite(c.z)&&c.z>-.5&&Math.abs(c.x)<Math.max(220,c.z*Math.tan(sceneHFov()*.5)*2.25+radius));
   for(const c of samples)if(webglCamPointVisible(c,{near:.7,far,radius,margin:ds<520?1.85:margin,minPx:ds<700?0:minPx,pitch}))return true;
   return false;
 }
 if(it.type==="ferris"&&cam){
   const r=it.radius||22,samples=[
     base,
     webglLandmarkCam(a,0,0,r*1.25,cam),
     webglLandmarkCam(a,0,0,r*2.25,cam),
     webglLandmarkCam(a,0, r*.85,r*1.25,cam),
     webglLandmarkCam(a,0,-r*.85,r*1.25,cam)
   ];
   if(ds<1400)return samples.some(c=>c&&Number.isFinite(c.z)&&c.z>0&&Math.abs(c.x)<Math.max(260,c.z*Math.tan(sceneHFov()*.5)*2.7+radius));
   for(const c of samples)if(webglCamPointVisible(c,{near:1.0,far,radius,margin:1.85,minPx,pitch}))return true;
   return false;
 }
 // v62.2.2: cualquier masa de skyline queda exenta del culling longitudinal. Si se mira hacia
 // un edificio lejano, se conserva como silueta LOD2 aunque pertenezca a otro sector.
 if(trackData&&trackData.gradeCrossing&&!skyline&&Number.isFinite(a.s)){
   const curSec=webglSectorIndexAtS(player.s),objSec=webglSectorIndexAtS(a.s),gap=webglSectorGap(curSec,objSec);
   if(gap>1&&ds>460)return false;
 }
 if(skyline){
   const ext=apexLandmarkExtentCamSamples(it,cam),opts={near:.55,far:Math.max(6000,far),radius:Math.max(3,radius*.38),margin:2.20,minPx:.015,pitch};
   // Cualquier parte visible mantiene el edificio. Con muestreo a <=55 m también funciona
   // cuando el centro ya está detrás del coche pero todavía recorremos el resto de los boxes.
   for(const c of ext)if(webglCamPointVisible(c,opts))return true;
   return false;
 }
 return webglCamPointVisible(base,{near:1.6,far,radius,margin,minPx,pitch});
}
function webglPushMappedBoardPoly(tl,tr,br,bl,uvPts,color,z){
 const pts=uvPts.map(p=>{const q=quadMap(tl,tr,br,bl,p[0],p[1]);return[q.x,q.y]});if(pts.length<3)return;
 for(let i=1;i<pts.length-1;i++)webglScreenTri(pts[0],pts[i],pts[i+1],color,z-.010);
}
function webglPushBrakeBoards(cam,pitch){
 if(!webglCircuitVisualAvailable())return;for(const item of collectBrakeBoardSprites(cam,pitch)){
   const [tl,tr,br,bl]=item.pts,[poleL0,poleL1,poleR0,poleR1]=item.poles,z=item.z;
   // Panel y postes participan del Z-buffer: ya no pueden verse a través de taludes/pista.
   if(poleL0&&poleL1)webglPushScreenLineDepth([poleL0.x,poleL0.y],[poleL1.x,poleL1.y],Math.max(1,tl.scale*.055),"#6c727a",z+.015);
   if(poleR0&&poleR1)webglPushScreenLineDepth([poleR0.x,poleR0.y],[poleR1.x,poleR1.y],Math.max(1,tr.scale*.055),"#6c727a",z+.015);
   webglScreenQuad([tl.x,tl.y],[tr.x,tr.y],[br.x,br.y],[bl.x,bl.y],"#f7f7f5",z);
   const rr0=mixPt(tl,bl,.15),rr1=mixPt(tr,br,.15),rr2=mixPt(tr,br,.02),rr3=mixPt(tl,bl,.02);webglScreenQuad([rr3.x,rr3.y],[rr2.x,rr2.y],[rr1.x,rr1.y],[rr0.x,rr0.y],"#dc3b4b",z-.008);
   webglPushScreenLineDepth([tl.x,tl.y],[tr.x,tr.y],1.15,"#15191d",z-.012);webglPushScreenLineDepth([tr.x,tr.y],[br.x,br.y],1.15,"#15191d",z-.012);webglPushScreenLineDepth([br.x,br.y],[bl.x,bl.y],1.15,"#15191d",z-.012);webglPushScreenLineDepth([bl.x,bl.y],[tl.x,tl.y],1.15,"#15191d",z-.012);
   const digits=String(item.board.mark).split('').map(ch=>parseInt(ch,10)).filter(Number.isFinite),marginX=.16,marginY=.20,gap=.05,usable=1-marginX*2,cellW=(usable-gap*(digits.length-1))/Math.max(1,digits.length);
   for(let di=0;di<digits.length;di++){
     const u0=marginX+di*(cellW+gap),u1=u0+cellW,v0=marginY,v1=1-marginY,w=u1-u0,h=v1-v0,t=Math.min(w,h)*.12,polys=sevenSegPolys(u0,v0,w,h,t),segs=SEVEN_SEG_DIGITS[digits[di]]||SEVEN_SEG_DIGITS[8];
     for(const key of segs)webglPushMappedBoardPoly(tl,tr,br,bl,polys[key],"#13171c",z);
   }
 }
}
function webglPushCircuitWalls(segments,cam,pitch){
 if(!webglCircuitVisualAvailable())return;const mp=mobileControlProfile(),cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld,exact=!!(cw&&cw.authoritative&&Array.isArray(cw.wallSegments)&&cw.wallSegments.length),items=[];
 if(exact)items.push(...collectExactWallRenderItems(cam).map(x=>x.data));else if(segments)for(const seg of segments){if(seg.z>mp.barrierMax)continue;for(const side of [1,-1]){const item=buildWallRenderItem(seg,side,cam);if(item)items.push(item)}}
 for(const item of items){
  if(!item||item.z>620)continue;const lod=webglCircuitLod('barrier',item.z,2.2);if(lod>=3||item.poly3.length<4)continue;
  const barrier=item.barrier||wallBarrierStyle(item.env||{},item.s||0,item.side||1),kind=barrier.kind||'WALL_F1',adStyle=item.advertising===false?null:circuitAdvertisingStyle(item.s,item.env,item.side),col=adStyle?adStyle.bg:(barrier.base||'#bfc4c8'),A=item.poly3[0],T0=item.poly3[1],T1=item.poly3[2],B=item.poly3[3];
  webglPushCamPoly(item.poly3,pitch,col,.62);
  const top0=camMix(A,T0,.86),top1=camMix(B,T1,.86),band0=camMix(A,T0,.42),band1=camMix(B,T1,.42),band2=camMix(A,T0,.78),band3=camMix(B,T1,.78);
  if(adStyle){const fg=adStyle.fg||'#ffffff';webglPushCamQuad(band0,band1,band3,band2,pitch,adStyle.bg,.62,-.008);if(lod<=1)webglPushCamQuad(camMix(band0,band2,.42),camMix(band1,band3,.42),camMix(band1,band3,.58),camMix(band0,band2,.58),pitch,fg,.62,-.010)}
  else if(lod<=1)webglPushCamQuad(top0,T0,T1,top1,pitch,barrier.stripe||'#eceff1',.62,-.008);
  const fp=item.fencePoly3,fg=item.fenceGeom;
  if(barrier.fence&&fp&&fp.length>=3&&lod<=2){
   // v62.8.0.3: la base de la catch-fence se ancla EXPLÍCITAMENTE a T0/T1,
   // la coronación del muro. El clipping de cámara ya no puede hacerla arrancar del suelo.
   const F0=fg&&fg.base0?fg.base0:fp[0],FT0=fg&&fg.top0?fg.top0:fp[Math.min(1,fp.length-1)],FT1=fg&&fg.top1?fg.top1:fp[Math.max(0,fp.length-2)],F1=fg&&fg.base1?fg.base1:fp[fp.length-1];
   // v62.8.0.4: NO hay un panel relleno detrás de la catch-fence.
   // La valla F1 es geometría abierta: postes + hilos + diagonales. Esto evita que
   // varios segmentos transparentes acumulados parezcan un segundo muro sólido.
   const projFencePoint=v=>{if(!v||v.z<.60)return null;const p=projectRoadLocal(v.x,v.y,v.z,pitch);return p&&Number.isFinite(p.x)&&Number.isFinite(p.y)?p:null};
   const fenceLine=(a,b,w,col,bias=-.020)=>{const pa=projFencePoint(a),pb=projFencePoint(b);if(!pa||!pb)return;const zLine=Math.max(.61,((a.z+b.z)*.5)+bias);webglPushScreenLineDepth([pa.x,pa.y],[pb.x,pb.y],w,col,zLine)};
   const zNear=Math.max(.7,item.z||((F0.z+F1.z)*.5)),pxScale=clamp(170/Math.max(22,zNear),.72,2.15),postW=(lod===0?2.35:1.75)*pxScale,meshW=(lod===0?1.05:.82)*pxScale;
   // Rail inferior: marca exactamente la unión HORMIGÓN -> VALLA. Nunca baja al suelo.
   fenceLine(F0,F1,Math.max(1.15,postW*.58),'rgba(228,234,238,.92)',-.027);
   // Postes físicos: los segmentos SVG de muro ya están muestreados aprox. cada 2,5 m,
   // por lo que sus extremos son una separación muy próxima a una valla F1 real.
   if(lod<=1){fenceLine(F0,FT0,postW,'rgba(224,231,236,.98)');fenceLine(F1,FT1,postW,'rgba(224,231,236,.98)');}
   // Hilos horizontales de malla.
   const rows=lod===0?7:(lod===1?5:3);
   for(let r=1;r<rows;r++){const u=r/rows;fenceLine(camMix(F0,FT0,u),camMix(F1,FT1,u),meshW,lod===0?'rgba(186,199,208,.78)':'rgba(188,201,210,.60)',-.023)}
   // Malla diagonal sólo cerca del coche: visible y mucho más barata que una textura densa.
   if(lod===0){
    fenceLine(F0,FT1,Math.max(.78,meshW*.82),'rgba(168,182,192,.56)',-.026);
    fenceLine(FT0,F1,Math.max(.78,meshW*.82),'rgba(168,182,192,.56)',-.026);
   }
   // Tubo/coronación superior inclinada hacia el interior del circuito.
   fenceLine(FT0,FT1,Math.max(1.55,postW*.82),'rgba(236,240,243,.98)',-.030);
  }
 }
}


// v5.230.62.8.2 · CATCH-FENCE F1 VISUAL REALISTA EN PASADA INDEPENDIENTE.
// No depende del polígono del muro ni de su clipping. WALL_F1 y su valla son
// geometrías distintas: hormigón en webglPushCircuitWalls, malla aquí.
function webglPushExactWallFences(cam,pitch){
 const cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld,segs=cw&&(cw._wallRenderSegments||cw.wallSegments);if(!cw||!Array.isArray(segs)||!segs.length)return 0;
 let drawn=0;
 const proj=v=>{if(!v||v.z<.58||v.z>820)return null;const p=projectRoadLocal(v.x,v.y,v.z,pitch);return p&&Number.isFinite(p.x)&&Number.isFinite(p.y)?p:null};
 const line=(a,b,w,col,bias=-.055)=>{const pa=proj(a),pb=proj(b);if(!pa||!pb)return false;const z=Math.max(.59,Math.min(a.z,b.z)+bias);webglPushScreenLineDepth([pa.x,pa.y],[pb.x,pb.y],w,col,z);return true};
 for(const sg of segs){
  if(!sg)continue;const env=(sg._apexStaticWall&&sg._apexStaticWall.env)||envProfile(sg.s||0),base=(sg._apexStaticWall&&sg._apexStaticWall.style)||wallBarrierStyle(env,sg.s||0,sg.side||1);
  const style={...base};if(sg.fence!==null&&sg.fence!==undefined)style.fence=sg.fence;if(style.fence===false)continue;
  const P=apexWallFenceProfileWorld(sg,style,cam);if(!P)continue;const {B0,B1,J0,J1,T0,T1,fh}=P;
  if((B0.z<.58&&B1.z<.58)||(B0.z>820&&B1.z>820))continue;
  const z=(Math.max(.6,B0.z)+Math.max(.6,B1.z))*.5,lod=webglCircuitLod('barrier',z,fh+1.2);if(lod>=3)continue;
  const px=clamp(188/Math.max(24,z),.72,2.22),postW=(lod===0?2.35:1.85)*px,railW=(lod===0?1.55:1.15)*px,wireW=(lod===0?.88:.68)*px;
  // Rail en la coronación del hormigón.
  line(B0,B1,Math.max(1.05,railW*.82),'rgba(198,205,210,.96)',-.071);
  // Postes verticales robustos.
  line(B0,J0,postW,'rgba(176,184,190,.98)',-.076);line(B1,J1,postW,'rgba(176,184,190,.98)',-.076);
  // Brazo superior inclinado hacia la pista.
  line(J0,T0,Math.max(1.22,postW*.86),'rgba(184,191,197,.99)',-.078);line(J1,T1,Math.max(1.22,postW*.86),'rgba(184,191,197,.99)',-.078);
  // Rail del codo y tubo superior.
  line(J0,J1,Math.max(1.0,railW*.72),'rgba(176,185,191,.90)',-.075);
  line(T0,T1,Math.max(1.22,railW),'rgba(214,220,224,.98)',-.081);
  // Un travesaño intermedio ayuda a leer la estructura metálica.
  if(lod<=1){const u=.55;line(camMix(B0,J0,u),camMix(B1,J1,u),Math.max(.72,wireW*.95),'rgba(174,184,190,.72)',-.073)}
  // Malla vertical tipo chain-link: varias diagonales pequeñas, no una X gigante.
  if(lod<=1){
   const panels=lod===0?3:2;
   for(let i=0;i<panels;i++){
    const a=i/panels,b=(i+1)/panels;
    line(camMix(B0,B1,a),camMix(J0,J1,b),wireW,'rgba(166,178,186,.54)',-.070);
    line(camMix(J0,J1,a),camMix(B0,B1,b),wireW,'rgba(166,178,186,.54)',-.070);
   }
  }
  // Malla del brazo superior.
  if(lod===0){
   const panels=2;
   for(let i=0;i<panels;i++){
    const a=i/panels,b=(i+1)/panels;
    line(camMix(J0,J1,a),camMix(T0,T1,b),Math.max(.62,wireW*.86),'rgba(170,181,188,.46)',-.078);
    line(camMix(T0,T1,a),camMix(J0,J1,b),Math.max(.62,wireW*.86),'rgba(170,181,188,.46)',-.078);
   }
  }
  drawn++;
 }
 return drawn;
}
function canvasPushExactWallFences(cam,pitch){
 const cw=trackData&&trackData.customWorld,segs=cw&&(cw._wallRenderSegments||cw.wallSegments);if(!cw||!Array.isArray(segs)||!segs.length)return;
 const proj=v=>{if(!v||v.z<.58||v.z>820)return null;const p=projectRoadLocal(v.x,v.y,v.z,pitch);return p&&Number.isFinite(p.x)&&Number.isFinite(p.y)?p:null};
 const line=(a,b,w,col)=>{const A=proj(a),B=proj(b);if(!A||!B)return false;ctx.strokeStyle=col;ctx.lineWidth=w;ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(A.x,A.y);ctx.lineTo(B.x,B.y);ctx.stroke();return true};
 ctx.save();ctx.lineCap='round';
 for(const sg of segs){if(!sg)continue;const env=(sg._apexStaticWall&&sg._apexStaticWall.env)||envProfile(sg.s||0),base=(sg._apexStaticWall&&sg._apexStaticWall.style)||wallBarrierStyle(env,sg.s||0,sg.side||1),style={...base};if(sg.fence!==null&&sg.fence!==undefined)style.fence=sg.fence;if(style.fence===false)continue;
  const P=apexWallFenceProfileWorld(sg,style,cam);if(!P)continue;const {B0,B1,J0,J1,T0,T1,fh}=P;if((B0.z<.58&&B1.z<.58)||(B0.z>820&&B1.z>820))continue;const z=(Math.max(.6,B0.z)+Math.max(.6,B1.z))*.5,lod=webglCircuitLod('barrier',z,fh+1.2);if(lod>=3)continue;
  const px=clamp(188/Math.max(24,z),.72,2.22),postW=(lod===0?2.35:1.85)*px,railW=(lod===0?1.55:1.15)*px,wireW=(lod===0?.88:.68)*px;
  line(B0,B1,Math.max(1.05,railW*.82),'rgba(198,205,210,.96)');
  line(B0,J0,postW,'rgba(176,184,190,.98)');line(B1,J1,postW,'rgba(176,184,190,.98)');
  line(J0,T0,Math.max(1.22,postW*.86),'rgba(184,191,197,.99)');line(J1,T1,Math.max(1.22,postW*.86),'rgba(184,191,197,.99)');
  line(J0,J1,Math.max(1.0,railW*.72),'rgba(176,185,191,.90)');
  line(T0,T1,Math.max(1.22,railW),'rgba(214,220,224,.98)');
  if(lod<=1){const u=.55;line(camMix(B0,J0,u),camMix(B1,J1,u),Math.max(.72,wireW*.95),'rgba(174,184,190,.72)')}
  if(lod<=1){const panels=lod===0?3:2;for(let i=0;i<panels;i++){const a=i/panels,b=(i+1)/panels;line(camMix(B0,B1,a),camMix(J0,J1,b),wireW,'rgba(166,178,186,.54)');line(camMix(J0,J1,a),camMix(B0,B1,b),wireW,'rgba(166,178,186,.54)')}}
  if(lod===0){const panels=2;for(let i=0;i<panels;i++){const a=i/panels,b=(i+1)/panels;line(camMix(J0,J1,a),camMix(T0,T1,b),Math.max(.62,wireW*.86),'rgba(170,181,188,.46)');line(camMix(T0,T1,a),camMix(J0,J1,b),Math.max(.62,wireW*.86),'rgba(170,181,188,.46)')}}
 }
 ctx.restore();
}

function webglPushTunnelDetails(segments,cam,pitch){
 if(!webglCircuitVisualAvailable()||!segments)return;
 const _tunnelT0=performance.now();
 for(const seg of segments){const a=seg.a,b=seg.b,env=a.env;if(!env.tunnel)continue;const zone=authoredStructureZoneAtS((a.s+b.s)*.5),visual=apexTunnelVisualProfile(zone),sc=String(env.scenery||''),isProceduralCrossing=sc==='GRADE_CROSSING'&&!(zone&&zone.kind==='UNDER'),isSvg=!!(zone&&zone.kind==='UNDER')||sc==='SVG_TUNNEL';const lod=webglCircuitLod('tunnel',seg.z,14,'tunnelSeg:'+Math.round(a.s/8));if(lod>=3)continue;
   const roofH=tunnelRoofHeightForState(a);let aRL=tunnelRoofPoint(a,1,cam,roofH),bRL=tunnelRoofPoint(b,1,cam,roofH),aRR=tunnelRoofPoint(a,-1,cam,roofH),bRR=tunnelRoofPoint(b,-1,cam,roofH);const aWL=tunnelBasePoint(a,1,cam),bWL=tunnelBasePoint(b,1,cam),aWR=tunnelBasePoint(a,-1,cam),bWR=tunnelBasePoint(b,-1,cam);
   if(!isProceduralCrossing)webglPushCamQuad(aRL,aRR,bRR,bRL,pitch,visual.roof,.60,-.006);
   if(lod<=2){webglPushCamQuad(aWL,aRL,bRL,bWL,pitch,visual.wallL,.60);webglPushCamQuad(aRR,aWR,bWR,bRR,pitch,visual.wallR,.60);const l0=camMix(aWL,aRL,.16),l1=camMix(bWL,bRL,.16),r0=camMix(aWR,aRR,.16),r1=camMix(bWR,bRR,.16);webglPushCamQuad(aWL,l0,l1,bWL,pitch,visual.lower,.60,-.005);webglPushCamQuad(r0,aWR,bWR,r1,pitch,visual.lower,.60,-.005);}
   webglPushCamQuad(a.v.l,a.v.r,b.v.r,b.v.l,pitch,visual.road,.60,-.020);
   if(isSvg){const rise=Math.max(roofH+.45,Number(zone&&zone.roofRiseM)||0);if(rise>roofH+.015){const c0=tunnelRoofPoint(a,1,cam,rise),c1=tunnelRoofPoint(a,-1,cam,rise),d0=tunnelRoofPoint(b,1,cam,rise),d1=tunnelRoofPoint(b,-1,cam,rise);webglPushCamQuad(aRL,c0,d0,bRL,pitch,visual.portalSide,.60,-.01);webglPushCamQuad(c1,aRR,bRR,d1,pitch,visual.portalSide,.60,-.01);webglPushCamQuad(c0,c1,d1,d0,pitch,visual.roof,.60,-.014);}}
   if(visual.hasPanels&&lod<=1&&seg.z<360&&Math.floor(a.s/visual.panelSpacingM)!==Math.floor(b.s/visual.panelSpacingM)){const f=.965,L0=camMix(aWL,bWL,f),L1=camMix(aRL,bRL,f),R0=camMix(aWR,bWR,f),R1=camMix(aRR,bRR,f),g=.995,LL0=camMix(aWL,bWL,g),LL1=camMix(aRL,bRL,g),RR0=camMix(aWR,bWR,g),RR1=camMix(aRR,bRR,g);webglPushCamQuad(L0,LL0,LL1,L1,pitch,visual.joint,.60,-.012);webglPushCamQuad(RR0,R0,R1,RR1,pitch,visual.joint,.60,-.012);}
   if(visual.hasServiceWalk&&lod<=1&&seg.z<330){const sw=Math.max(.45,visual.serviceWalkWidthM),aI=tunnelFloorInsetPoint(a,1,cam,sw,.12),bI=tunnelFloorInsetPoint(b,1,cam,sw,.12),aW=tunnelFloorInsetPoint(a,1,cam,.06,.12),bW=tunnelFloorInsetPoint(b,1,cam,.06,.12);webglPushCamQuad(aW,aI,bI,bW,pitch,'#a7adb0',.60,-.013);}
   if(visual.hasLights&&isSvg&&lod<=1&&seg.z<360&&Math.floor(a.s/visual.lightSpacingM)!==Math.floor(b.s/visual.lightSpacingM)){const g0=.74,g1=.96,A0L=camMix(aRL,bRL,g0),A0R=camMix(aRR,bRR,g0),A1L=camMix(aRL,bRL,g1),A1R=camMix(aRR,bRR,g1),cut=(L,R,t)=>camMix(L,R,t);webglPushCamQuad(cut(A0L,A0R,.11),cut(A0L,A0R,.89),cut(A1L,A1R,.89),cut(A1L,A1R,.11),pitch,visual.lightGlow,.60,-.014);webglPushCamQuad(cut(A0L,A0R,.17),cut(A0L,A0R,.83),cut(A1L,A1R,.83),cut(A1L,A1R,.17),pitch,visual.light,.60,-.017);}
 }
 renderProfileAdd('tunnelMs',performance.now()-_tunnelT0);
}
function webglPushCircuitLandmarks(cam,pitch){
 if(!webglCircuitVisualAvailable())return;const allItems=trackLandmarks(),L=trackData.km*1000;if(!allItems||!allItems.length)return;const entries=apexCompiledLandmarkEntries(allItems),staticOcclusion=apexBuildStaticOcclusionContext(entries,cam,pitch);
 for(const ent of entries){
   const li=ent.i,it=ent.it,a=it.anchor,base=webglLandmarkCam(a,0,0,0,cam),skyline=apexIsSkylineLandmark(it),exactClip=skyline&&Array.isArray(it.worldFootprint)&&it.worldFootprint.length>=3?apexLandmarkFootprintClipInfo(it,cam,pitch,Math.max(6000,it.maxDs||0),.30):null;
   if(exactClip?!exactClip.visible:!webglLandmarkVisible(it,base,pitch,cam))continue;
   if(apexStaticLandmarkOccluded(it,base,cam,pitch,staticOcclusion))continue;
   const radius=webglLandmarkRadius(it),dsLm=Number.isFinite(a.s)?Math.abs(wrapDeltaS(a.s,player.s,L)):1e9,lodZ=exactClip&&exactClip.visible?exactClip.depth:(skyline?apexLandmarkEffectiveCamDepth(it,cam,base):base.z);
   if(it.type==='surfaceZone'&&!(typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene())){const gb=apexSceneDetailBudget(),surfaceFar=(gb.mobile?620:1050)*gb.farScale*(gb.pressure>=2?.82:1);if(dsLm>surfaceFar&&lodZ>220)continue;}
   let lod=it.farGeo?2:webglCircuitLod(it.type,lodZ,radius,'lm:'+li+':'+(it.type||''));if(it.type==='grandstand'){const q=renderQualityT(),m=mobilePerformanceMode(),near=(m?115:150)+90*q,mid=(m?280:360)+160*q,far=(m?720:920)+220*q;if(dsLm<near)lod=Math.min(lod,0);else if(dsLm<mid)lod=Math.min(lod,1);else if(dsLm<far)lod=Math.min(lod,2);else lod=Math.max(lod,2);}if(!apexSceneryShouldRender(it,li,dsLm,lodZ))continue;lod=apexObjectLodBudget(it.type,lod,dsLm);if(skyline&&lodZ>1)lod=Math.min(lod,2);if(lod>=3)continue;
   const P=(along=0,lat=0,y=0)=>webglLandmarkCam(a,along,lat,y,cam);
   if(it.type==='surfaceZone'||it.type==='terrainZone'||it.type==='water'||it.type==='marking'||it.type==='forestZone'){
     if(it.type==='terrainZone')continue;
     if(it.worldFootprint&&it.worldFootprint.length>=3){const col=it.color||(it.type==='water'?'#347ca5':it.type==='forestZone'?'#4b7b50':'#777'),sb=it._apexStaticBatch,bp=sb&&Array.isArray(sb.surfacePoly3)?sb.surfacePoly3.map(p=>apexStaticWorldCam(p,cam)):it.worldFootprint.map((v,j)=>worldToCamera(v[0],(Array.isArray(it.worldY)&&Number.isFinite(it.worldY[j])?it.worldY[j]:apexWorldGroundY(v[0],v[1]))+apexGroundPatchYOffset(it),v[1],cam));webglPushCamPoly(bp,pitch,col,.60,apexGroundPatchDepthBias(it));if(it.type==='forestZone'){if(sb&&Array.isArray(sb.forest)){for(const tree of sb.forest){webglPushCamQuad(...tree.trunk.map(p=>apexStaticWorldCam(p,cam)),pitch,'#57452f',.60,-.004);webglPushCamPoly(tree.crown.map(p=>apexStaticWorldCam(p,cam)),pitch,'#285f38',.60,-.005)}}else if(Array.isArray(it.forestPoints)){for(const fp of it.forestPoints){const yy=fp[2]||0,C=worldToCamera(fp[0],yy+8.2,fp[1],cam),L0=worldToCamera(fp[0]-2,yy+1.8,fp[1],cam),R0=worldToCamera(fp[0]+2,yy+1.8,fp[1],cam);webglPushCamQuad(worldToCamera(fp[0]-.12,yy,fp[1],cam),worldToCamera(fp[0]+.12,yy,fp[1],cam),worldToCamera(fp[0]+.12,yy+3.1,fp[1],cam),worldToCamera(fp[0]-.12,yy+3.1,fp[1],cam),pitch,'#57452f',.60,-.004);webglPushCamPoly([L0,R0,C],pitch,'#285f38',.60,-.005)}}}continue;}
   }else if(it.type==='barrier'){
     if(Array.isArray(it.worldPath)&&it.worldPath.length>=2){const h=Math.max(.55,it.height||1.05),kind=it.barrierKind||'CONCRETE',ad=circuitAdvertisingStyle(a.s,{},it.side||1),col=(ad&&kind!=='TYRE')?ad.bg:apexBarrierColor(kind),sb=it._apexStaticBatch,segs=sb&&Array.isArray(sb.barrierSegments)?sb.barrierSegments:null;if(segs){for(const sg of segs){if(sg.bound&&!apexStaticBoundInView(sg.bound,cam,1250,1.28))continue;const q=sg.q,b0=apexStaticWorldCam(q[0],cam),b1=apexStaticWorldCam(q[1],cam),t1=apexStaticWorldCam(q[2],cam),t0=apexStaticWorldCam(q[3],cam);webglPushCamQuad(b0,b1,t1,t0,pitch,col,.60,-.004);if(it.fence&&lod<=1){const fh=Number(it.fenceH)||sg.fenceH||1.3,f0=worldToCamera(q[3][0],q[3][1]+fh,q[3][2],cam),f1=worldToCamera(q[2][0],q[2][1]+fh,q[2][2],cam);webglPushCamQuad(t0,t1,f1,f0,pitch,'rgba(202,215,226,.20)',.60,-.011);webglPushCamQuad(t0,camMix(t0,t1,.025),camMix(f0,f1,.025),f0,pitch,'rgba(207,217,224,.70)',.60,-.012);webglPushCamQuad(t1,camMix(t1,t0,.025),camMix(f1,f0,.025),f1,pitch,'rgba(207,217,224,.70)',.60,-.012);}}}else for(let j=0;j<it.worldPath.length-1;j++){const p0=it.worldPath[j],p1=it.worldPath[j+1],g0=Array.isArray(it.worldPathY)&&Number.isFinite(it.worldPathY[j])?it.worldPathY[j]:apexWorldGroundY(p0[0],p0[1]),g1=Array.isArray(it.worldPathY)&&Number.isFinite(it.worldPathY[j+1])?it.worldPathY[j+1]:apexWorldGroundY(p1[0],p1[1]),b0=worldToCamera(p0[0],g0,p0[1],cam),b1=worldToCamera(p1[0],g1,p1[1],cam),t0=worldToCamera(p0[0],g0+h,p0[1],cam),t1=worldToCamera(p1[0],g1+h,p1[1],cam);webglPushCamQuad(b0,b1,t1,t0,pitch,col,.60,-.004);}
     }continue;
   }else if(it.type==='tunnel'){
     // SVG tunnel = hueco transitable. webglPushTunnelDetails genera paredes/techo.
     continue;
   }else if(it.type==='bridge'&&it.pedestrianBridge){
     // v5.230.27 · Pasarela peatonal: volumen elevado y fino, no bloque sólido desde el suelo.
     if(it.worldFootprint&&it.worldFootprint.length>=3){
       const totalH=Math.max(6.1,Math.min(8.6,Number(it.height)||7)),deckT=clamp(totalH*.16,.88,1.24),deckBase=totalH-deckT,gy=a.y,col=it.color||'#343b42',bp=it.worldFootprint.map(v=>worldToCamera(v[0],gy+deckBase,v[1],cam)),tp=it.worldFootprint.map(v=>worldToCamera(v[0],gy+totalH,v[1],cam));
       webglPushCamPoly(bp,pitch,'#1c2227',.60,-.006);webglPushCamPoly(tp,pitch,shadeColor(col,16),.60,-.010);
       for(let j=0;j<bp.length;j++){const k=(j+1)%bp.length;webglPushCamQuad(bp[j],bp[k],tp[k],tp[j],pitch,j%2?shadeColor(col,-8):col,.60,-.008);}
       if(Array.isArray(it.worldPath)&&it.worldPath.length>=2){
         const p0=it.worldPath[0],p1=it.worldPath[it.worldPath.length-1],dx=p1[0]-p0[0],dz=p1[1]-p0[1],ln=Math.hypot(dx,dz)||1,nx=-dz/ln,nz=dx/ln,colHalf=.30;
         for(const ep of [p0,p1]){const ey=apexWorldGroundY(ep[0],ep[1]),B0=worldToCamera(ep[0]-nx*colHalf,ey,ep[1]-nz*colHalf,cam),B1=worldToCamera(ep[0]+nx*colHalf,ey,ep[1]+nz*colHalf,cam),T0=worldToCamera(ep[0]-nx*colHalf,gy+deckBase,ep[1]-nz*colHalf,cam),T1=worldToCamera(ep[0]+nx*colHalf,gy+deckBase,ep[1]+nz*colHalf,cam);webglPushCamQuad(B0,B1,T1,T0,pitch,'#aeb7be',.60,-.010);}
         if(lod<=1){
           const railY=gy+totalH+.42,rail=it.worldPath.map(v=>worldToCamera(v[0],railY,v[1],cam));
           for(let j=1;j<rail.length;j++){const A=rail[j-1],B=rail[j];webglPushCamQuad(camMix(A,B,.01),camMix(A,B,.03),camMix(A,B,.99),camMix(A,B,.97),pitch,'rgba(205,215,222,.72)',.60,-.014);}
         }
       }
       continue;
     }
   }else if(it.type==='road'&&Array.isArray(it.worldPath)&&it.worldPath.length>=2){
     // v5.230.62.5 · La cinta 3D ya viene batcheada desde la compilación del circuito.
     const col=it.color||'#747c83',sb=it._apexStaticBatch;if(sb&&Array.isArray(sb.roadQuads)){for(const q of sb.roadQuads){if(q._apexBound&&!apexStaticBoundInView(q._apexBound,cam,2100,1.32))continue;const c=q.map(p=>apexStaticWorldCam(p,cam));webglPushCamQuad(c[0],c[1],c[2],c[3],pitch,shadeColor(col,-5),.60,-.010)}}else{
       const P=it.worldPath,Y=Array.isArray(it.worldPathY)?it.worldPathY:[],hw=Math.max(1.8,(Number(it.width)||8)*.5),lift=.045;for(let j=0;j<P.length-1;j++){const p0=P[j],p1=P[j+1],dx=p1[0]-p0[0],dz=p1[1]-p0[1],ln=Math.hypot(dx,dz);if(ln<.08)continue;const nx=-dz/ln,nz=dx/ln,y0=Number.isFinite(Y[j])?Y[j]:apexTerrainHeightAtWorld(p0[0],p0[1],0),y1=Number.isFinite(Y[j+1])?Y[j+1]:apexTerrainHeightAtWorld(p1[0],p1[1],0),A=worldToCamera(p0[0]+nx*hw,y0+lift,p0[1]+nz*hw,cam),B=worldToCamera(p0[0]-nx*hw,y0+lift,p0[1]-nz*hw,cam),C=worldToCamera(p1[0]-nx*hw,y1+lift,p1[1]-nz*hw,cam),D=worldToCamera(p1[0]+nx*hw,y1+lift,p1[1]+nz*hw,cam);webglPushCamQuad(A,B,C,D,pitch,shadeColor(col,-5),.60,-.010)}}
     continue;
   }else if(it.type==='pit'||it.type==='block'||it.type==='parking'||it.type==='bridge'){
     if(it.worldFootprint&&it.worldFootprint.length>=3){
       if(it.type==='pit'){apexWebglRenderPitExact(it,cam,pitch,lod);continue;}
       if(it.type==='block'){apexWebglRenderBuildingExact(it,cam,pitch,lod);continue;}
       const h=Math.min(it.height||12,48),clear=Math.max(0,it.clearance||0),gy=a.y+clear,col=it.color||'#aab2b9',bp=it.worldFootprint.map(v=>worldToCamera(v[0],gy,v[1],cam)),tp=it.worldFootprint.map(v=>worldToCamera(v[0],gy+h,v[1],cam));
       webglPushCamPoly(tp,pitch,'#d9dee2',.60,-.008);
       for(let j=0;j<bp.length;j++){const k=(j+1)%bp.length;webglPushCamQuad(bp[j],bp[k],tp[k],tp[j],pitch,j%2?shadeColor(col,-7):col,.60);}
       continue;     }
     const len=Math.min(it.length||it.width||24,it.proposalId?340:132),side=it.side||-1,specialPit=false,col=it.color||'#89929a';
    let dep=Math.min(it.depth||((it.type==='pit')?20:16),it.proposalId?145:18),h=Math.min(it.height||12,it.proposalId?20:12.5);
    const wallAtObj=wallGeometryAtS(a.s,side),wallAbs=wallAtObj&&wallAtObj.enabled?Math.abs(wallAtObj.edge):null;
    const gap=Number.isFinite(wallAbs)?wallAbs+(it.type==='pit'?3.2:2.6):2.5,frontLat=side*gap,backLat=side*(gap+dep);
     const f1=P(-len*.5,frontLat,0),f2=P(len*.5,frontLat,0),f3=P(len*.5,frontLat,h),f4=P(-len*.5,frontLat,h),b1=P(-len*.5,backLat,0),b2=P(len*.5,backLat,0),b3=P(len*.5,backLat,h),b4=P(-len*.5,backLat,h);
     webglPushCamQuad(f4,f3,f2,f1,pitch,shadeColor(col,-10),.60);webglPushCamQuad(b4,b3,f3,f4,pitch,col,.60);
     if(lod<=1){webglPushCamQuad(b4,f4,f1,b1,pitch,shadeColor(col,-18),.60);webglPushCamQuad(f3,b3,b2,f2,pitch,shadeColor(col,-15),.60);}
     if(lod===0)webglPushCamQuad(b4,b3,b2,b1,pitch,shadeColor(col,-22),.60);
     // LOD2: silueta sólida y barata. El edificio permanece visible hasta salir de cámara,
     // pero no reconstruimos ventanas, juntas y HVAC a cientos/metres de distancia.
     if(lod>=2){
       webglPushCamQuad(P(-len*.5,frontLat,h+.20),P(len*.5,frontLat,h+.20),P(len*.5,backLat,h+.48),P(-len*.5,backLat,h+.48),pitch,specialPit?'#b8454d':'#c7cdd1',.60,-.006);
       continue;
     }
     // Roof/parapet to avoid boxy flat look.
     webglPushCamQuad(P(-len*.5,frontLat,h+.20),P(len*.5,frontLat,h+.20),P(len*.5,backLat,h+.56),P(-len*.5,backLat,h+.56),pitch,specialPit?'#b8454d':'#d2d8dd',.60,-.006);
     webglPushCamQuad(P(-len*.5,frontLat,h),P(len*.5,frontLat,h),P(len*.5,frontLat,h+.18),P(-len*.5,frontLat,h+.18),pitch,specialPit?'#91353d':'#c6cdd2',.60,-.008);
     if(specialPit)webglPushCamQuad(P(-len*.49,frontLat,h*.41),P(len*.49,frontLat,h*.41),P(len*.49,frontLat,h*.50),P(-len*.49,frontLat,h*.50),pitch,'#f0c622',.60,-.010);
     // Front glazing / bay rhythm.
     const bays=it.type==='pit'?8:5;
     for(let k=0;k<bays;k++){
       const u0=lerp(-len*.46,len*.46,k/bays),u1=lerp(-len*.46,len*.46,(k+.78)/bays),glass=it.type==='pit'?(specialPit?((k&1)?'#59636b':'#6a747d'):((k&1)?'#85b4c6':'#97c8db')):((k&1)?'#8d99a1':'#a1acb4');
       webglPushCamQuad(P(u0,frontLat,h*.28),P(u1,frontLat,h*.28),P(u1,frontLat,h*.70),P(u0,frontLat,h*.70),pitch,glass,.60,-.010);
     }
     // Vertical joints and a modest rooftop equipment strip when close.
     if(lod<=1){
       for(let k=1;k<bays;k++){const x=lerp(-len*.5,len*.5,k/bays);webglPushCamQuad(P(x-.04,frontLat,0),P(x+.04,frontLat,0),P(x+.04,frontLat,h),P(x-.04,frontLat,h),pitch,'#737c83',.60,-.011);} 
     }
     if(lod===0){
       const hvacCount=it.type==='pit'?5:3;
       for(let k=0;k<hvacCount;k++){const x=lerp(-len*.34,len*.34,(k+.5)/hvacCount),w=len*(it.type==='pit'?0.055:0.07);webglPushCamQuad(P(x-w,backLat-.8*side,h+.58),P(x+w,backLat-.8*side,h+.58),P(x+w,backLat-.2*side,h+1.02),P(x-w,backLat-.2*side,h+1.02),pitch,'#9ca5ab',.60,-.010);} 
     }
   }else if(it.type==='grandstand'){
     // v5.230.32 · Misma regla en WebGL: huella SVG exacta + graderío por bandas.
     if(Array.isArray(it.worldFootprint)&&it.worldFootprint.length>=3){
       const prof=apexGrandstandStyleProfile(it),fp=it.worldFootprint,h=Math.max(3,Number(it.height)||14),col=it.color||prof.base||'#667078',gb=apexGrandstandRenderBudget(lod,dsLm),rows=gb.tier===2?Math.min(10,Math.max(8,prof.rows)):(gb.tier===1?Math.min(6,Math.max(5,Math.round(prof.rows*.62))):3),side=it.side||1,att=apexGrandstandAttendanceProfile(),frame=apexGrandstandFrameStats(it);it._grandstandFrontEdge=it._grandstandFrontEdge||apexGrandstandFrontEdge(it);
       const bp=fp.map(v=>worldToCamera(v[0],apexWorldGroundY(v[0],v[1])+.02,v[1],cam)),bt=fp.map(v=>worldToCamera(v[0],apexWorldGroundY(v[0],v[1])+h*.18,v[1],cam));
       webglPushCamPoly(bp,pitch,shadeColor(col,-30),.60,-.004);
       for(let j=0;j<fp.length;j++){const k=(j+1)%fp.length;webglPushCamQuad(bp[j],bp[k],bt[k],bt[j],pitch,j&1?shadeColor(col,-24):shadeColor(col,-19),.60,-.005);}
       for(let ri=0;ri<rows;ri++){
         const t0=ri/rows,t1=(ri+1)/rows,band=apexGrandstandBand(fp,a,side,t0,t1,it);if(band.length<3)continue;
         const yTop=h*(.20+.60*t1),yLow=h*(.20+.60*t0),top=band.map(v=>worldToCamera(v[0],apexWorldGroundY(v[0],v[1])+yTop,v[1],cam)),low=band.map(v=>worldToCamera(v[0],apexWorldGroundY(v[0],v[1])+yLow,v[1],cam));
