       for(const sg of [-1,1]){
         const iLat=sg*bankInner,oLat=sg*bankOuter,cLat=sg*bankCrest;
         const I0=P(-bankAlong,iLat,0),I1=P(bankAlong,iLat,0),O0=P(-bankAlong,oLat,0),O1=P(bankAlong,oLat,0),C0=P(-bankAlong,cLat,deckH-.08),C1=P(bankAlong,cLat,deckH-.08);
         webglPushCamQuad(I0,O0,C0,C1,pitch,'#597a49',.60,-.006);
         webglPushCamQuad(I0,C0,C1,I1,pitch,'#4e7042',.60,-.007);
         webglPushCamPoly([O0,O1,C1,C0],pitch,'#6f9157',.60,-.008);
       }
       // parapets / crash barriers on deck
       webglPushCamQuad(P(-2.1,-deckW*.5,deckH),P(2.1,-deckW*.5,deckH),P(2.1,-deckW*.5,deckH+1.0),P(-2.1,-deckW*.5,deckH+1.0),pitch,'#c3c8cc',.60,-.007);
       webglPushCamQuad(P(-2.1,deckW*.5,deckH),P(2.1,deckW*.5,deckH),P(2.1,deckW*.5,deckH+1.0),P(-2.1,deckW*.5,deckH+1.0),pitch,'#c3c8cc',.60,-.007);
       // motorway sign above, deliberately modest rather than a floating billboard.
       webglPushCamQuad(P(-2.18,-span*.18,deckH+1.35),P(-2.18,span*.18,deckH+1.35),P(-2.18,span*.18,deckH+2.25),P(-2.18,-span*.18,deckH+2.25),pitch,'#1f7f45',.60,-.010);
     }else{
       const fw=clamp(span*.075,.72,1.35),lh=clamp(h*.13,.55,.95),iL=-span*.5+fw,iR=span*.5-fw,iT=h-lh,dep=.72;
       webglPushCamQuad(P(-dep,-span*.5,0),P(-dep,iL,0),P(-dep,iL,iT),P(-dep,-span*.5,h),pitch,'#9ba2a8',.60,-.006);
       webglPushCamQuad(P(-dep,iR,0),P(-dep,span*.5,0),P(-dep,span*.5,h),P(-dep,iR,iT),pitch,'#8f979d',.60,-.006);
       webglPushCamQuad(P(-dep,-span*.5,h),P(-dep,span*.5,h),P(-dep,iR,iT),P(-dep,iL,iT),pitch,'#a8afb4',.60,-.007);
       webglPushCamQuad(P(-dep,iL,0),P(dep,iL,0),P(dep,iL,iT),P(-dep,iL,iT),pitch,'#6c7379',.60,-.008);
       webglPushCamQuad(P(dep,iR,0),P(-dep,iR,0),P(-dep,iR,iT),P(dep,iR,iT),pitch,'#656c72',.60,-.008);
       webglPushCamQuad(P(-dep,iL,iT),P(-dep,iR,iT),P(dep,iR,iT),P(dep,iL,iT),pitch,'#5a6066',.60,-.009);
     }
   }else if(it.type==='flagPole'){
     const h=it.height||30;
     webglPushCamQuad(P(-.07,0,0),P(.07,0,0),P(.07,0,h),P(-.07,0,h),pitch,'#e6eaee',.60,-.004);
     webglPushCamPoly([P(0,.45,h-4.8),P(0,5.0,h-4.2),P(0,5.8,h-2.7),P(0,4.8,h-1.0),P(0,.4,h-1.4)],pitch,'#f4c724',.60,-.006);
     webglPushCamPoly([P(0,.45,h-4.9),P(0,4.7,h-4.35),P(0,5.5,h-3.55),P(0,.42,h-3.75)],pitch,'#c61f28',.60,-.007);
     webglPushCamPoly([P(0,.40,h-2.05),P(0,5.2,h-1.55),P(0,4.6,h-.90),P(0,.36,h-1.35)],pitch,'#c61f28',.60,-.007);
     webglPushCamQuad(P(-.10,2.12,h-3.20),P(.10,2.12,h-3.20),P(.10,2.40,h-2.90),P(-.10,2.40,h-2.90),pitch,'#9a3a25',.60,-.008);
   }else if(it.type==='ferris'){
     const center=P(0,0,it.radius*1.25),rim=P(0,0,it.radius*2.25),baseP=P(0,0,0);if(center.z<3||rim.z<3)continue;
     const pc=projectRoadLocal(center.x,center.y,center.z,pitch),pr=projectRoadLocal(rim.x,rim.y,rim.z,pitch),pb=projectRoadLocal(baseP.x,baseP.y,baseP.z,pitch),rr=Math.max(6,Math.abs(pr.y-pc.y)),segments=lod===0?28:(lod===1?18:12),spokes=lod===0?10:(lod===1?8:6),col=it.color||'#eef3f6',rot=(Number.isFinite(raceT)?raceT:0)*.055;
     for(let i=0;i<segments;i++){const a0=rot+Math.PI*2*i/segments,a1=rot+Math.PI*2*(i+1)/segments,A=[pc.x+Math.cos(a0)*rr,pc.y+Math.sin(a0)*rr],B=[pc.x+Math.cos(a1)*rr,pc.y+Math.sin(a1)*rr];webglPushScreenLineDepth(A,B,lod===0?2.0:1.55,col,center.z)}
     for(let i=0;i<spokes;i++){const a0=rot+Math.PI*2*i/spokes;webglPushScreenLineDepth([pc.x,pc.y],[pc.x+Math.cos(a0)*rr,pc.y+Math.sin(a0)*rr],1.05,col,center.z)}
     if(lod<=1){const gond=lod===0?10:6;for(let i=0;i<gond;i++){const a0=rot+Math.PI*2*i/gond,x=pc.x+Math.cos(a0)*rr,y=pc.y+Math.sin(a0)*rr,w=Math.max(2.2,rr*.065),h=Math.max(2.0,rr*.052);webglScreenQuad([x-w,y-h*.45],[x+w,y-h*.45],[x+w,y+h*.55],[x-w,y+h*.55],i%2?'#d94343':'#f1c84a',center.z-.01)}}
     webglPushScreenLineDepth([pc.x-rr*.62,pb.y],[pc.x-rr*.19,pc.y+rr*.82],1.8,col,center.z);webglPushScreenLineDepth([pc.x+rr*.62,pb.y],[pc.x+rr*.19,pc.y+rr*.82],1.8,col,center.z);

   }else if(it.type==='tree'){
     const v=it.treeVariant||it.variant||'deciduous',seed=apexStringHash((it.sourceId||it.name||'tree')+'|'+v),r0=((seed>>>0)&1023)/1023,r1=((seed>>>10)&1023)/1023,r2=((seed>>>20)&1023)/1023,wMul=.88+r0*.34,hMul=.92+r1*.22,crossTilt=(r2-.5)*.22,treeW=it.width*wMul,treeH=it.height*hMul,th=treeH*(v==='palm'?(0.48+r0*.07):(v==='fir'?(0.24+r0*.05):(0.37+r0*.06)));
     webglPushCamQuad(P(-.20,0,0),P(.20,0,0),P(.20,0,th),P(-.20,0,th),pitch,v==='palm'?'#6b5737':'#5a432d',.60,-.004);
     if(v==='fir'){
       const fc=it.foliage||'#285f38';
       webglPushCamPoly([P(0,-treeW*.62,treeH*(.24+.03*r1)),P(0,treeW*.62,treeH*(.24+.03*r1)),P(0,0,treeH)],pitch,fc,.60,-.005);
       webglPushCamPoly([P(0,-treeW*.50,treeH*(.46+.05*r2)),P(0,treeW*.50,treeH*(.46+.05*r2)),P(0,0,treeH*.92)],pitch,shadeColor(fc,6),.60,-.006);
       webglPushCamPoly([P(0,-treeW*.36,treeH*(.67+.03*r0)),P(0,treeW*.36,treeH*(.67+.03*r0)),P(0,crossTilt*treeW*.10,treeH*1.01)],pitch,shadeColor(fc,12),.60,-.007);
       if(lod<=1)webglPushCamPoly([P(0,-treeW*.20,treeH*.56),P(0,treeW*.20,treeH*.56),P(0,crossTilt*treeW*.18,treeH*.76)],pitch,shadeColor(fc,18),.60,-.008);
     }
     else if(v==='palm'){
       const fc=it.foliage||'#4e8b45',C=P(0,0,treeH*(th/treeH+.06));
       for(let k=0;k<8;k++){const aa=-1.26+k*(2.52/7),lat=Math.sin(aa)*treeW*(.88-.08*(k%2)),tip=P(0,lat,treeH*(.80-.06*(Math.abs(k-3.5)/3.5))),mid=P(0,lat*.58,treeH*.86);webglPushCamPoly([C,tip,mid],pitch,shadeColor(fc,k%2?8:-5),.60,-.006);}
       if(lod<=1){webglPushCamPoly([P(0,-treeW*.38,treeH*.73),P(0,treeW*.42,treeH*.75),P(0,crossTilt*treeW*.18,treeH*.86)],pitch,shadeColor(fc,12),.60,-.008);webglPushCamPoly([P(0,-treeW*.18,treeH*.66),P(0,treeW*.16,treeH*.68),P(0,crossTilt*treeW*.12,treeH*.80)],pitch,shadeColor(fc,5),.60,-.009);}
     }
     else{
       const fc=it.foliage||'#4b8447';
       webglPushCamPoly([P(0,-treeW*(.62+.04*r0),treeH*.34),P(0,treeW*(.64-.02*r1),treeH*.35),P(0,treeW*.52,treeH*.74),P(0,crossTilt*treeW*.14,treeH*1.01),P(0,-treeW*.56,treeH*.72)],pitch,fc,.60,-.005);
       webglPushCamPoly([P(0,-treeW*.48,treeH*.46),P(0,treeW*.46,treeH*.48),P(0,treeW*.32,treeH*.86),P(0,crossTilt*treeW*.10,treeH*.96),P(0,-treeW*.34,treeH*.84)],pitch,shadeColor(fc,8),.60,-.007);
       if(lod<=1)webglPushCamPoly([P(0,-treeW*.24,treeH*.54),P(0,treeW*.22,treeH*.56),P(0,crossTilt*treeW*.18,treeH*.74)],pitch,shadeColor(fc,16),.60,-.008);
     }
   }
 }
}
function webglCrossingEase(x){x=clamp(x,0,1);return x*x*(3-2*x)}
function webglPushGradeCrossingEmbankments(cam,pitch){return;
 if(!apexGradeCrossingNearUnder(380))return;
 const c=trackData.gradeCrossing,L=trackData.km*1000,underEnv=envProfile(c.underS),overEnv=envProfile(c.overS),half=underEnv.half,uq=renderRibbonFrameAt(c.underS),oq=renderRibbonFrameAt(c.overS),center=worldToCamera(uq.x,uq.y,uq.z,cam);
 const lod=webglCircuitLod('tunnel',Math.max(1,center.z),40,'grade-crossing:tunnel-terrain175');if(lod>=3)return;
 const roadRise=clamp(oq.y-uq.y,5.8,7.8),portalRoof=clamp(Math.min(4.65,roadRise-.78),4.15,4.65),portalD=Math.max(14,Math.min(18,c.underHalf*.92)),approach=lod===0?78:(lod===1?68:58),wc=P=>worldToCamera(P.x,P.y,P.z,cam);
 const underPoint=(d,lat,y)=>crossingWorldCam(mod(c.underS+d,L),lat,y,cam);

 // 1) Lower road is a cutting: retaining wall + earth bank grow progressively toward the underpass.
 const step=lod===0?5:(lod===1?8:12);
 for(const side of [1,-1]){
   for(let d=-approach;d<approach;d+=step){
     const d1=Math.min(approach,d+step),ad0=Math.abs(d),ad1=Math.abs(d1),cl0=1-webglCrossingEase((ad0-portalD)/(approach-portalD)),cl1=1-webglCrossingEase((ad1-portalD)/(approach-portalD));
     const inTunnel0=ad0<=portalD,inTunnel1=ad1<=portalD;
     const wallH0=inTunnel0?portalRoof:lerp(.42,2.35,cl0),wallH1=inTunnel1?portalRoof:lerp(.42,2.35,cl1),wallLat=side*(half+.78);
     const wb0=underPoint(d,wallLat,0),wb1=underPoint(d1,wallLat,0),wt0=underPoint(d,wallLat,wallH0),wt1=underPoint(d1,wallLat,wallH1);
     webglPushCamQuad(wb0,wt0,wt1,wb1,pitch,inTunnel0&&inTunnel1?'#777e84':'#969da2',.60,-.007);

     // Earth immediately outside the wall: at the crossing it climbs to the upper-road level,
     // farther away it returns smoothly to ordinary ground. No giant horizontal cap is used.
     const rise0=Math.max(wallH0+.18,lerp(.30,roadRise-.10,Math.pow(cl0,.78))),rise1=Math.max(wallH1+.18,lerp(.30,roadRise-.10,Math.pow(cl1,.78)));
     const bankW0=lerp(7.0,18.0,cl0),bankW1=lerp(7.0,18.0,cl1),outerLat0=side*(half+.78+bankW0),outerLat1=side*(half+.78+bankW1);
     const top0=underPoint(d,outerLat0,rise0),top1=underPoint(d1,outerLat1,rise1);
     webglPushCamQuad(wt0,top0,top1,wt1,pitch,side>0?'#527649':'#4b7044',.60,.004);
     if(lod<=1){
       const farLat0=side*(Math.abs(outerLat0)+10.5),farLat1=side*(Math.abs(outerLat1)+10.5),farH0=Math.max(.08,rise0-lerp(.65,1.35,cl0)),farH1=Math.max(.08,rise1-lerp(.65,1.35,cl1));
       const far0=underPoint(d,farLat0,farH0),far1=underPoint(d1,farLat1,farH1);
       webglPushCamQuad(top0,far0,far1,top1,pitch,side>0?'#3e623b':'#385c38',.60,.006);
     }
   }
 }

 // 2) Proper portal faces at the real ends of the underpass zone.
 const openLat=half+.62,pierOuter=openLat+2.35,th=.72;
 for(const dir of [-1,1]){
   const d=dir*portalD;
   for(const side of [1,-1]){
     const inner=side*openLat,outer=side*pierOuter;
     const A=underPoint(d-th,inner,0),B=underPoint(d+th,inner,0),C=underPoint(d+th,inner,portalRoof),D=underPoint(d-th,inner,portalRoof),E=underPoint(d-th,outer,0),F=underPoint(d+th,outer,0),G=underPoint(d+th,outer,roadRise*.72),Hh=underPoint(d-th,outer,roadRise*.72);
     webglPushCamQuad(E,F,G,Hh,pitch,'#8f969c',.60,-.010);webglPushCamQuad(A,E,Hh,D,pitch,'#aab0b5',.60,-.012);webglPushCamQuad(B,C,G,F,pitch,'#838a90',.60,-.012);
   }
   const L0=underPoint(d-th,-openLat,portalRoof),R0=underPoint(d-th,openLat,portalRoof),R1=underPoint(d+th,openLat,portalRoof),L1=underPoint(d+th,-openLat,portalRoof);
   const LT=underPoint(d-th,-openLat,portalRoof+.52),RT=underPoint(d-th,openLat,portalRoof+.52),RT1=underPoint(d+th,openLat,portalRoof+.52),LT1=underPoint(d+th,-openLat,portalRoof+.52);
   webglPushCamQuad(L0,R0,RT,LT,pitch,'#a3a9ae',.60,-.014);webglPushCamQuad(L1,LT1,RT1,R1,pitch,'#777e84',.60,-.014);
 }

 // 3) Terrain shoulders follow the recalibrated UPPER ROAD profile so they actually
 // meet the road above instead of forming detached slabs in the sky.
 const overSpan=lod===0?58:(lod===1?50:42),overStep=lod===0?4.5:(lod===1?7:10);
 for(const side of [1,-1]){
   for(let d=-overSpan;d<overSpan;d+=overStep){
     const d1=Math.min(overSpan,d+overStep),s0=mod(c.overS+d,L),s1=mod(c.overS+d1,L),q0=renderRibbonFrameAt(s0),q1=renderRibbonFrameAt(s1),e0=envProfile(s0),e1=envProfile(s1),cl0=1-webglCrossingEase(Math.abs(d)/overSpan),cl1=1-webglCrossingEase(Math.abs(d1)/overSpan);
     const in0=e0.half+e0.curb+e0.runoff+.18,in1=e1.half+e1.curb+e1.runoff+.18,mid0=in0+lerp(8,15,cl0),mid1=in1+lerp(8,15,cl1),out0=mid0+lerp(9,16,cl0),out1=mid1+lerp(9,16,cl1);
     const A=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q0,in0,side,.00)),B=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q0,mid0,side,-.28)),C=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q1,mid1,side,-.28)),D=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q1,in1,side,.00));
     webglPushCamQuad(A,B,C,D,pitch,side>0?'#527649':'#4b7044',.60,.010);
     if(lod<=1){const B2=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q0,out0,side,-lerp(.65,1.15,cl0))),C2=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q1,out1,side,-lerp(.65,1.15,cl1)));webglPushCamQuad(B,B2,C2,C,pitch,side>0?'#3c6039':'#365a37',.60,.012);}
   }
 }
}
function webglPushGradeCrossingOverpass(cam,pitch){return;
 if(!apexGradeCrossingNearUnder(310))return;const c=trackData.gradeCrossing,L=trackData.km*1000,qc=renderRibbonFrameAt(c.overS),center=worldToCamera(qc.x,qc.y,qc.z,cam),lod=webglCircuitLod('overpass',Math.max(1,center.z),28,'grade-crossing:overpass175');if(lod>=3)return;
 const span=27,step=lod===0?3.5:(lod===1?6:9),wc=P=>worldToCamera(P.x,P.y,P.z,cam);
 for(let d=-span;d<span;d+=step){
   const s0=mod(c.overS+d,L),s1=mod(c.overS+Math.min(span,d+step),L),q0=renderRibbonFrameAt(s0),q1=renderRibbonFrameAt(s1),e0=envProfile(s0),e1=envProfile(s1),w0=e0.half+.78,w1=e1.half+.78,deck=.46;
   const L0=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q0,w0,1,0)),R0=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q0,w0,-1,0)),L1=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q1,w1,1,0)),R1=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q1,w1,-1,0)),L0b=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q0,w0,1,-deck)),R0b=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q0,w0,-1,-deck)),L1b=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q1,w1,1,-deck)),R1b=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q1,w1,-1,-deck));
   // Underside and side beams create the bridge volume; the top follows the actual road.
   webglPushCamQuad(L0b,R0b,R1b,L1b,pitch,'#494f54',.60);webglPushCamQuad(L0,L0b,L1b,L1,pitch,'#737a80',.60);webglPushCamQuad(R0b,R0,R1,R1b,pitch,'#686f75',.60);webglPushCamQuad(L0,R0,R1,L1,pitch,shadeColor(palette().road,2),.60);
   if(lod<=1){const ew=.20,li0=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q0,w0-ew,1,.018)),li1=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q1,w1-ew,1,.018)),ri0=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q0,w0-ew,-1,.018)),ri1=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q1,w1-ew,-1,.018));webglPushCamQuad(L0,li0,li1,L1,pitch,'#f4f6f7',.60,-.006);webglPushCamQuad(ri0,R0,R1,ri1,pitch,'#f4f6f7',.60,-.006);}
   if(lod===0){const bh=.68,l0t=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q0,w0,1,bh)),l1t=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q1,w1,1,bh)),r0t=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q0,w0,-1,bh)),r1t=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(q1,w1,-1,bh));webglPushCamQuad(L0,l0t,l1t,L1,pitch,'#c3c9cd',.60);webglPushCamQuad(r0t,R0,R1,r1t,pitch,'#c3c9cd',.60);}
 }
 // Darken only the actual lower-road opening, not a broad floating slab.
 const uq0=renderRibbonFrameAt(mod(c.underS-13,L)),uq1=renderRibbonFrameAt(mod(c.underS+13,L)),ue=envProfile(c.underS),h=ue.half+.50;
 const A=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(uq0,h,1,.03)),B=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(uq0,h,-1,.03)),C=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(uq1,h,-1,.03)),D=wc(ribbonWorldPoint(uq1,h,1,.03));webglPushCamQuad(A,B,C,D,pitch,'rgba(10,14,18,.22)',.60,-.018);
}

function apexWeatherPushCloudsWebGL(cam,pitch,hy){
 if(!apexWeatherRuntime||!cam)return;const sm=apexWeatherSample();if(sm.cloud<.025)return;const mobile=mobilePerformanceMode(),t=Number.isFinite(raceT)?raceT:0,field=apexWeatherCloudField();if(!field)return;
 const hiTarget=sm.high<.08?0:Math.max(1,Math.round((mobile?7:11)*sm.high+sm.cloud*2)),loTarget=sm.low<.06?0:Math.max(1,Math.round((mobile?8:13)*sm.low+sm.cloud*2));
 const hi=apexWeatherVisibleClouds(field.high,cam,pitch,sm,t,hiTarget),lo=apexWeatherVisibleClouds(field.low,cam,pitch,sm,t,loTarget);
 for(const p of hi){
   const a=((.070+.12*sm.high)*(p.fade||1)),c=sm.dark>.30?`rgba(190,199,208,${a})`:`rgba(255,255,255,${a})`,x=p.x,y=p.y,w=p.w,h=p.h;
   webglScreenQuad([x-w*.44,y],[x+w*.44,y-h*.06],[x+w*.39,y+h*.38],[x-w*.39,y+h*.40],c);webglScreenQuad([x-w*.12,y-h*.34],[x+w*.30,y-h*.27],[x+w*.34,y+h*.30],[x-w*.18,y+h*.31],c);
 }
 for(const p of lo){
   const a=((.10+.18*sm.low)*(p.fade||1)),c=sm.state==='STORM'?`rgba(93,104,114,${a})`:sm.dark>.20?`rgba(145,157,166,${a})`:`rgba(232,238,242,${a})`,x=p.x,y=p.y,w=p.w,h=p.h;
   webglScreenQuad([x-w*.48,y+h*.05],[x-w*.10,y-h*.22],[x+w*.08,y+h*.36],[x-w*.43,y+h*.40],c);webglScreenQuad([x-w*.18,y-h*.40],[x+w*.24,y-h*.33],[x+w*.31,y+h*.34],[x-w*.23,y+h*.34],c);webglScreenQuad([x+w*.10,y-h*.18],[x+w*.49,y],[x+w*.44,y+h*.40],[x+w*.05,y+h*.35],c);
 }
 const flash=apexWeatherLightningFlash();if(flash>.01)webglScreenQuad([0,0],[W,0],[W,hy],[0,hy],`rgba(245,249,255,${(.12*flash).toFixed(3)})`);
}

function webglBackgroundPalette(){const p=palette();return{top:p.sky1,horizon:p.sky2,ground:p.ground,far:p.ground}}
function webglPushBackground(cam,pitch){if(apexInspectionActive()){webglGradientRect(0,H,'#24313d','#24313d');return;}const p=webglBackgroundPalette(),hy=clamp(sceneHorizon(pitch),H*.18,H*.70);webglGradientRect(0,hy,p.top,p.horizon);apexWeatherPushCloudsWebGL(cam,pitch,hy);webglGradientRect(hy,H,p.far,p.ground)}
function webglUsesAuthoredWorldBackdrop(){return !!(trackData&&trackData.spec&&apexCircuitVisualFeature('worldBackdrop',null))}
function webglCircuitVisualAvailable(){return !!(trackData&&trackData.spec&&player)}

function buildCircuitWorldBackdrop(){
 if(!trackData||!trackData.spec||apexCircuitVisualFeature('worldBackdrop',null)!=='hills')return{hills:[]};
 if(trackData.webglWorldBackdrop)return trackData.webglWorldBackdrop;
 const L=trackData.km*1000;
 const defs=[
  {f:.03,lat:-270,w:190,h:14,c:"#58704f"},{f:.11,lat:255,w:175,h:13,c:"#5b7351"},{f:.20,lat:292,w:225,h:17,c:"#607855"},
  {f:.31,lat:-310,w:270,h:20,c:"#617a57"},{f:.43,lat:-345,w:315,h:23,c:"#647d59"},{f:.56,lat:365,w:350,h:26,c:"#637b58"},
  {f:.67,lat:-305,w:280,h:21,c:"#5d7653"},{f:.77,lat:350,w:330,h:24,c:"#607956"},{f:.88,lat:-330,w:300,h:22,c:"#5d7653"},{f:.96,lat:270,w:210,h:15,c:"#597150"}
 ];
 const hills=[];
 for(let i=0;i<defs.length;i++){const d=defs[i],a=safeWorldAnchor(L*d.f,d.lat,0,0,Math.max(135,d.w*.46));if(!a)continue;hills.push({x:a.x,y:a.y-d.h*.58,z:a.z,w:d.w,h:d.h,color:d.c,shade:i%2?"#445e43":"#40593f",forest:"#28482d",seed:i});}
 trackData.webglWorldBackdrop={hills};return trackData.webglWorldBackdrop;
}
function webglPushBillboardHill(cam,obj,pitch,lod=0){
 const c=worldToCamera(obj.x,obj.y,obj.z,cam);if(c.z<105||c.z>1900)return false;
 const baseY=c.y-obj.h*.30,skirtY=baseY-Math.max(24,obj.h*1.55),hw=obj.w*.5,top=obj.w*.19;
 // Falda hasta muy por debajo del horizonte: nunca queda una montaña suspendida.
 webglPushCamPoly([{x:c.x-hw*1.24,y:skirtY,z:c.z+.8},{x:c.x-hw,y:baseY,z:c.z},{x:c.x+hw,y:baseY,z:c.z},{x:c.x+hw*1.24,y:skirtY,z:c.z+.8}],pitch,"#3b5639",.70);
 webglPushCamPoly([{x:c.x-hw,y:baseY,z:c.z},{x:c.x-top,y:baseY+obj.h,z:c.z},{x:c.x+top*.55,y:baseY+obj.h*.88,z:c.z},{x:c.x+hw,y:baseY,z:c.z}],pitch,obj.color,.72);
 if(lod<=1){const shadeW=obj.w*.35;webglPushCamPoly([{x:c.x-shadeW,y:baseY+obj.h*.12,z:c.z+.2},{x:c.x-obj.w*.04,y:baseY+obj.h*.82,z:c.z+.2},{x:c.x+shadeW*.30,y:baseY+obj.h*.26,z:c.z+.2}],pitch,obj.shade,.72);}
 if(lod===0)webglPushCamPoly([{x:c.x-hw*.98,y:baseY-1,z:c.z-.1},{x:c.x-hw*.82,y:baseY+obj.h*.20,z:c.z-.1},{x:c.x-hw*.25,y:baseY+obj.h*.28,z:c.z-.1},{x:c.x+hw*.22,y:baseY+obj.h*.24,z:c.z-.1},{x:c.x+hw*.92,y:baseY+obj.h*.16,z:c.z-.1},{x:c.x+hw*.99,y:baseY-1,z:c.z-.1}],pitch,obj.forest,.70);
 return true;
}

function webglPushCircuitWorldBackdrop(cam,pitch){
 const set=buildCircuitWorldBackdrop();if(!set||!set.hills||!set.hills.length)return;
 const visible=[];
 for(let hi=0;hi<set.hills.length;hi++){const hill=set.hills[hi],c=worldToCamera(hill.x,hill.y,hill.z,cam),r=hill.w*.58,lod=webglCircuitLod('backdrop',c.z,r,'hill:'+hi);if(lod<3&&webglCamPointVisible(c,{near:90,far:1850,radius:r,margin:1.28,minPx:1.5,pitch}))visible.push({hill,z:c.z,lod});}
 visible.sort((A,B)=>B.z-A.z);
 for(const item of visible)webglPushBillboardHill(cam,item.hill,pitch,item.lod);
}

function webglPushMovingClouds(cam,pitch,hy){apexWeatherPushCloudsWebGL(cam,pitch,hy)}
function webglPushCircuitBackground(cam,pitch){
 const hy=clamp(sceneHorizon(pitch),H*.20,H*.64);
 // El cielo sigue siendo de pantalla: es esencialmente infinito.
 webglGradientRect(0,hy*.72,"#6f9fc4","#b9d2df");
 webglGradientRect(hy*.72,hy,"#b9d2df","#e3e6df");
 webglGradientRect(hy,H,"#536842","#20311f");
 const haze=webglCssColor("rgba(233,239,232,.18)");
 webglVertex(-1,1-(hy-7)/H*2,WEBGL_BG_NDC,haze);webglVertex(1,1-(hy-7)/H*2,WEBGL_BG_NDC,haze);webglVertex(1,1-(hy+16)/H*2,WEBGL_BG_NDC,[haze[0],haze[1],haze[2],0]);
 webglVertex(-1,1-(hy-7)/H*2,WEBGL_BG_NDC,haze);webglVertex(1,1-(hy+16)/H*2,WEBGL_BG_NDC,[haze[0],haze[1],haze[2],0]);webglVertex(-1,1-(hy+16)/H*2,WEBGL_BG_NDC,[haze[0],haze[1],haze[2],0]);
 // Fondo lejano en coordenadas de mundo: ya no queda pegado a la pantalla.
 webglPushCircuitWorldBackdrop(cam,pitch);
 // v5.173: nubes vectoriales con deriva lenta y parallax temporal.
 webglPushMovingClouds(cam,pitch,hy);
}
function webglCircuitRoadColor(seg){
 const base=palette().road,seed=Math.floor((seg.a.s+seg.b.s)*.22),z=seg.z;
 const dist=z>260?-12:z>120?-8:z<28?1:-3,alt=(seed&1)?1:-1;
 return shadeColor(base,dist+alt);
}
function webglPushTerrainSegment(seg,cam,pitch){
 const a=seg.a,b=seg.b;if(!a||!b||!a.v||!b.v)return;
 if(apexPlateTerrainGrid()||['OVER','UNDER'].includes((authoredStructureZoneAtS(a.s)||{}).kind)||['OVER','UNDER'].includes((authoredStructureZoneAtS(b.s)||{}).kind))return;
 const mp=mobileControlProfile(),inspectionFull=(typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene()),mobile=mobilePerformanceMode()&&!inspectionFull;let lod=webglCircuitLod('terrain',seg.z,24);if(seg.z<(mobile?105:250))lod=0;else if(seg.z<(mobile?190:480))lod=Math.min(lod,1);
 // La pista y su suelo tienen ahora el mismo horizonte efectivo. Si el presupuesto de
 // terreno se agota antes que el de carretera conservamos un soporte simplificado.
 const supportOnly=lod>=3||(!inspectionFull&&seg.z>mp.terrainMax);if(lod>=3)lod=2;
 const visual=terrainVisualProfile(a),water=terrainWaterSideProfile(a),seed=Math.floor((Math.abs(a.s)+Math.abs(b.s))*.17),sa=terrainSpread(a),sb=terrainSpread(b),t=renderQualityT(),authoredTunnelA=terrainAuthoredTunnelReliefAtS(a.s),authoredTunnelB=terrainAuthoredTunnelReliefAtS(b.s),authoredTunnel=authoredTunnelA||authoredTunnelB;
 const nearA=mobile?Math.min(sa.near,lerp(7.4,10.2,t)):sa.near,nearB=mobile?Math.min(sb.near,lerp(7.4,10.2,t)):sb.near;
 const aLN=terrainPointCamera(a,1,nearA,cam,.04,.035),bLN=terrainPointCamera(b,1,nearB,cam,.04,.035),aRN=terrainPointCamera(a,-1,nearA,cam,.04,.035),bRN=terrainPointCamera(b,-1,nearB,cam,.04,.035);
 const inTunnel=!!((a.env&&a.env.tunnel)||(b.env&&b.env.tunnel)),baseL=inTunnel?'#4b5055':terrainShade(water.leftNear||visual.near,false,seed&1),baseR=inTunnel?'#474c51':terrainShade(water.rightNear||visual.near,false,(seed+1)&1);
 // v5.230.35: no base quad bajo la carretera; dentro del túnel ocultamos juntas verdes.
 webglPushCamQuad(a.v.lx,aLN,bLN,b.v.lx,pitch,baseL);webglPushCamQuad(aRN,a.v.rx,b.v.rx,bRN,pitch,baseR);
 if(lod<=2&&(!mobile||t>.24||supportOnly)){
   // En móvil el límite histórico de 30 m borraba el relieve SVG situado detrás del túnel.
   // Para túneles con cotas SVG usamos la distancia real calculada por terrainSpread.
   const supportMid=supportOnly?Math.max(24,lerp(13,30,t)):lerp(13,30,t),midA=(mobile&&!authoredTunnelA)?Math.min(sa.mid,supportMid):sa.mid,midB=(mobile&&!authoredTunnelB)?Math.min(sb.mid,supportMid):sb.mid;
   const aLM=terrainPointCamera(a,1,midA,cam,.72,.07),bLM=terrainPointCamera(b,1,midB,cam,.72,.07),aRM=terrainPointCamera(a,-1,midA,cam,.72,.07),bRM=terrainPointCamera(b,-1,midB,cam,.72,.07);
   const midL=terrainShade(water.leftMid||visual.mid,false,(seed>>1)&1),midR=terrainShade(water.rightMid||visual.mid,false,((seed>>1)+1)&1);
   if(!apexPlateTerrainGrid()){webglPushCamQuad(aLN,aLM,bLM,bLN,pitch,midL);webglPushCamQuad(aRM,aRN,bRN,bRM,pitch,midR);}
   if(!apexPlateTerrainGrid()&&lod<=1&&(!mobile||t>.68||authoredTunnel)){
     // Igual para la banda exterior: si hay relieve authored, no lo cortamos a 62 m.
     const farA=(mobile&&!authoredTunnelA)?Math.min(sa.far,lerp(28,62,t)):sa.far,farB=(mobile&&!authoredTunnelB)?Math.min(sb.far,lerp(28,62,t)):sb.far;
     const aLF=terrainPointCamera(a,1,farA,cam,1,.12),bLF=terrainPointCamera(b,1,farB,cam,1,.12),aRF=terrainPointCamera(a,-1,farA,cam,1,.12),bRF=terrainPointCamera(b,-1,farB,cam,1,.12);
     const farL=terrainShade(water.leftFar||visual.far,true,(seed>>2)&1),farR=terrainShade(water.rightFar||visual.far,true,((seed>>2)+1)&1);
     if(authoredTunnel&&seg.z<520){
       // Muestra intermedia aproximadamente al 76 % del alcance. Es suficiente para conservar
       // una cresta/cumbre estrecha sin aumentar la malla global del circuito.
       const ridgeA=lerp(midA,farA,.56),ridgeB=lerp(midB,farB,.56),aLR=terrainPointCamera(a,1,ridgeA,cam,.90,.09),bLR=terrainPointCamera(b,1,ridgeB,cam,.90,.09),aRR=terrainPointCamera(a,-1,ridgeA,cam,.90,.09),bRR=terrainPointCamera(b,-1,ridgeB,cam,.90,.09),ridgeL=terrainShade(water.leftFar||visual.far,false,(seed>>3)&1),ridgeR=terrainShade(water.rightFar||visual.far,false,((seed>>3)+1)&1);
       webglPushCamQuad(aLM,aLR,bLR,bLM,pitch,ridgeL);webglPushCamQuad(aLR,aLF,bLF,bLR,pitch,farL);webglPushCamQuad(aRR,aRM,bRM,bRR,pitch,ridgeR);webglPushCamQuad(aRF,aRR,bRR,bRF,pitch,farR);
     }else{
       webglPushCamQuad(aLM,aLF,bLF,bLM,pitch,farL);webglPushCamQuad(aRF,aRM,bRM,bRF,pitch,farR);
     }
   }
 }
}

function webglPushRunoffDetail(seg,pitch){
 const a=seg.a,b=seg.b,env=a.env,V0=a.v,V1=b.v;if(!env||!V0||!V1)return;
 // Driving corridor detail is protected: no global LOD culling here.
 const full=(typeof apexInspectionFullScene==='function'&&apexInspectionFullScene()),budget=full?{q:1,pressure:0,farScale:1}:apexSceneDetailBudget(),mobile=mobilePerformanceMode();
 const maxZ=full?apexInspectionFarDistance():Math.round((mobile?lerp(105,190,budget.q):lerp(300,430,budget.q))*budget.farScale*(budget.pressure>=2?.86:1));if(seg.z>maxZ)return;
 const surf=String(env.runoffSurface||'ASPHALT').toUpperCase(),seed=Math.floor(((a.s+b.s)*
